Generation Models Know Space: Unleashing Implicit 3D Priors for Scene Understanding
Authors: Xianjin Wu, Dingkang Liang, Tianrui Feng, Kui Xia, Yumeng Zhang, Xiaofan Li, Xiao Tan, Xiang Bai
First: 2026-03-19T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:59:58+00:00
Comments: 31 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
While Multimodal Large Language Models demonstrate impressive semantic capabilities, they often suffer from spatial blindness, struggling with fine-grained geometric reasoning and physical dynamics. Existing solutions typically rely on explicit 3D modalities or complex geometric scaffolding, which are limited by data scarcity and generalization challenges. In this work, we propose a paradigm shift by leveraging the implicit spatial prior within large-scale video generation models. We posit that to synthesize temporally coherent videos, these models inherently learn robust 3D structural priors and physical laws. We introduce VEGA-3D (Video Extracted Generative Awareness), a plug-and-play framework that repurposes a pre-trained video diffusion model as a Latent World Simulator. By extracting spatiotemporal features from intermediate noise levels and integrating them with semantic representations via a token-level adaptive gated fusion mechanism, we enrich MLLMs with dense geometric cues without explicit 3D supervision. Extensive experiments across 3D scene understanding, spatial reasoning, and embodied manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, validating that generative priors provide a scalable foundation for physical-world understanding. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/VEGA-3D.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the spatial limitations of multimodal large language models, this work proposes harnessing implicit 3D priors embedded in video generation models to enhance scene understanding. The method, VEGA-3D, repurposes a pre-trained video diffusion model as a latent world simulator, extracting spatiotemporal features from intermediate noise levels and fusing them with semantic representations via a token-level adaptive gated mechanism. Experimental results across 3D scene understanding, spatial reasoning, and embodied manipulation benchmarks show that this approach significantly outperforms existing methods, demonstrating that generative models inherently encode useful 3D structural knowledge without explicit 3D supervision.
鉴于多模态大语言模型在几何推理和物理动态方面的空间能力不足，本文提出利用视频生成模型中隐含的3D先验知识。VEGA-3D框架将预训练的视频扩散模型重用为潜在世界模拟器，从中间噪声层级提取时空特征，并通过令牌级自适应门控融合机制与语义表示结合。在3D场景理解、空间推理和具身操作等多个基准测试中，该方法显著优于现有方法，验证了生成模型无需显式3D监督即可编码有效3D结构知识的能力。
Matryoshka Gaussian Splatting
Authors: Zhilin Guo, Boqiao Zhang, Hakan Aktas, Kyle Fogarty, Jeffrey Hu, Nursena Koprucu Aslan, Wenzhao Li, Canberk Baykal, Albert Miao, Josef Bengtson, Chenliang Zhou, Weihao Xia, Cristina Nader Vasconcelos. Cengiz Oztireli
First: 2026-03-19T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:59:56+00:00
Comments: project page: https://zhilinguo.github.io/MGS
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The ability to render scenes at adjustable fidelity from a single model, known as level of detail (LoD), is crucial for practical deployment of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). Existing discrete LoD methods expose only a limited set of operating points, while concurrent continuous LoD approaches enable smoother scaling but often suffer noticeable quality degradation at full capacity, making LoD a costly design decision. We introduce Matryoshka Gaussian Splatting (MGS), a training framework that enables continuous LoD for standard 3DGS pipelines without sacrificing full-capacity rendering quality. MGS learns a single ordered set of Gaussians such that rendering any prefix, the first k splats, produces a coherent reconstruction whose fidelity improves smoothly with increasing budget. Our key idea is stochastic budget training: each iteration samples a random splat budget and optimises both the corresponding prefix and the full set. This strategy requires only two forward passes and introduces no architectural modifications. Experiments across four benchmarks and six baselines show that MGS matches the full-capacity performance of its backbone while enabling a continuous speed-quality trade-off from a single model. Extensive ablations on ordering strategies, training objectives, and model capacity further validate the designs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：套娃高斯点绘
从单一模型渲染可调节保真度场景的能力，即细节层次（LoD），对3D高斯点绘（3DGS）的实际部署至关重要。现有离散LoD方法仅暴露有限的操作点，而并行的连续LoD方法虽能实现平滑缩放，但在满容量时常出现明显质量下降，使LoD成为昂贵的设计决策。我们提出套娃高斯点绘（MGS），一种训练框架，可在标准3DGS流程中实现连续LoD而不牺牲满容量渲染质量。MGS学习一个有序的高斯点集，使得渲染任意前缀（前k个点）可生成连贯的重建结果，且随着预算增加，保真度平滑提升。我们的核心思想是随机预算训练：每轮迭代随机采样一个点预算，并同时优化对应前缀和完整点集。该策略仅需两次前向传播，无需架构修改。在四个基准和六个基线方法上的实验表明，MGS在保持骨干模型满容量性能的同时，从单一模型实现连续的速度-质量权衡。对排序策略、训练目标和模型容量的大量消融实验进一步验证了设计。
Summary / 总结
The ability to render scenes at adjustable fidelity from a single model, known as level of detail (LoD), is crucial for practical deployment of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS).
为实现灵活且高质量的可变计算成本3D渲染，Matryoshka Gaussian Splatting（MGS）提出一种训练框架，支持连续的细节层次（LoD）调整而不牺牲满容量渲染质量。其核心方法是学习一个有序的高斯点集，使得渲染任意前缀（前k个点）都能生成连贯且随预算增加而逐步提升的重建效果。该方法采用随机预算训练策略，每轮迭代随机采样一个高斯点预算，并同时优化对应前缀与完整点集，仅需两次前向传播且无需架构修改。在四个基准和六个基线方法上的实验表明，MGS在保持满容量性能的同时，能从单一模型中实现平滑的速度-质量权衡。
Cubic Discrete Diffusion: Discrete Visual Generation on High-Dimensional Representation Tokens
Authors: Yuqing Wang, Chuofan Ma, Zhijie Lin, Yao Teng, Lijun Yu, Shuai Wang, Jiaming Han, Jiashi Feng, Yi Jiang, Xihui Liu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-19T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:59:55+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026 main track; Code: https://github.com/YuqingWang1029/CubiD
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Visual generation with discrete tokens has gained significant attention as it enables a unified token prediction paradigm shared with language models, promising seamless multimodal architectures. However, current discrete generation methods remain limited to low-dimensional latent tokens (typically 8-32 dims), sacrificing the semantic richness essential for understanding. While high-dimensional pretrained representations (768-1024 dims) could bridge this gap, their discrete generation poses fundamental challenges. In this paper, we present Cubic Discrete Diffusion (CubiD), the first discrete generation model for high-dimensional representations. CubiD performs fine-grained masking throughout the high-dimensional discrete representation -- any dimension at any position can be masked and predicted from partial observations. This enables the model to learn rich correlations both within and across spatial positions, with the number of generation steps fixed at $T$ regardless of feature dimensionality, where $T \ll hwd$. On ImageNet-256, CubiD achieves state-of-the-art discrete generation with strong scaling behavior from 900M to 3.7B parameters. Crucially, we validate that these discretized tokens preserve original representation capabilities, demonstrating that the same discrete tokens can effectively serve both understanding and generation tasks. We hope this work will inspire future research toward unified multimodal architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/YuqingWang1029/CubiD.
中文标题/摘要
标题：立方体离散扩散：高维表示令牌上的离散视觉生成
离散令牌的视觉生成因其与语言模型共享统一的令牌预测范式而备受关注，有望实现无缝的多模态架构。然而，当前离散生成方法仍局限于低维潜在令牌（通常为8-32维），牺牲了理解所必需的语义丰富性。虽然高维预训练表示（768-1024维）可弥合此差距，但其离散生成面临根本性挑战。本文提出Cubic Discrete Diffusion（CubiD），这是首个面向高维表示的离散生成模型。CubiD在高维离散表示中进行细粒度掩码——任何位置的任一维度均可被掩码并从部分观测中预测。这使模型能够学习空间位置内及跨位置的丰富相关性，且生成步数固定为T，与特征维度无关，其中T << hwd。在ImageNet-256上，CubiD在离散生成中达到最先进水平，并在900M至3.7B参数规模下表现出强扩展性。关键的是，我们验证了这些离散令牌保留了原始表示能力，表明同一组离散令牌可有效服务于理解与生成任务。我们希望本工作能激励未来统一多模态架构的研究。代码见：https://github.com/YuqingWang1029/CubiD。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for discrete visual generation that matches the semantic richness of high-dimensional pretrained representations, this paper introduces Cubic Discrete Diffusion (CubiD), the first model enabling discrete generation in high-dimensional token spaces. CubiD employs fine-grained masking across all dimensions of the representation, allowing any position to be predicted from partial observations, while maintaining a fixed number of generation steps regardless of feature dimensionality. Experiments on ImageNet-256 show CubiD achieves state-of-the-art performance with strong scaling from 900M to 3.7B parameters, and crucially, the generated discrete tokens retain the original representation’s capabilities, supporting both understanding and generation tasks.
为解决现有离散生成方法受限于低维潜在令牌、无法充分表达语义的问题，Cubic Discrete Diffusion（CubiD）首次提出面向高维（768-1024维）表示的离散生成方法。该方法通过在任意位置和维度上进行细粒度掩码预测，使模型能学习空间内及跨位置的复杂关联，且生成步数T固定，与特征维度无关。在ImageNet-256上，CubiD在900M至3.7B参数规模下均表现最优，并验证生成的离散令牌保留原始表示能力，支持理解与生成任务统一，推动多模态架构发展。
Not All Features Are Created Equal: A Mechanistic Study of Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Bryce Grant, Xijia Zhao, Peng Wang
Venue: ICLR
First: 2026-03-19T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:59:55+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Multimodal Intelligence Workshop @ ICLR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models combine perception, language, and motor control in a single architecture, yet how they translate multimodal inputs into actions remains poorly understood. We apply activation injection, sparse autoencoders (SAEs), and linear probes to six models spanning 80M--7B parameters across 394,000+ rollout episodes on four benchmarks. The visual pathway dominates action generation across all architectures: injecting baseline activations into null-prompt episodes recovers near-identical behavior, while cross-task injection steers robots toward source-task positions (99.8\% of X-VLA episodes align with the source trajectory), exposing spatially bound motor programs tied to scene coordinates rather than abstract task representations. Language sensitivity depends on task structure, not model design: when visual context uniquely specifies the task, language is ignored; when multiple goals share a scene, language becomes essential (X-VLA \texttt{libero\_goal}: 94\%$\to$10\% under wrong prompts vs.\ \texttt{libero\_object}: 60--100\% regardless). In all three multi-pathway architectures (\pizhalf{}, SmolVLA, GR00T), expert pathways encode motor programs while VLM pathways encode goal semantics ($2\times$ greater behavioral displacement from expert injection), and subspace injection confirms these occupy separable activation subspaces. Per-token SAE processing is essential for action fidelity on most architectures, though mean-pooling improves fidelity on X-VLA. Contrastive identification recovers 82+ manipulation concepts, and causal ablation reveals sensitivity spanning 28--92\% zero-effect rates independent of representation width. We release \textbf{Action Atlas} (https://action-atlas.com) for interactive exploration of VLA representations across all six models.
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how Vision-Language-Action models translate multimodal inputs into actions by applying activation injection, sparse autoencoders, and linear probes across six models on four benchmarks. The visual pathway consistently dominates action generation, with injected activations recovering near-identical behavior and cross-task injection steering robots to source-task positions in 99.8% of cases, indicating spatially bound motor programs. Language sensitivity varies by task structure: ignored when visual context uniquely defines the goal, but critical when multiple goals share a scene. Expert pathways encode motor programs while VLM pathways encode goal semantics, occupying separable activation subspaces. Per-token SAE processing is vital for action fidelity, though mean-pooling helps X-VLA. Contrastive identification recovers 82+ manipulation concepts, and causal ablation shows sensitivity ranging from 28–92% zero-effect rates, independent of model width.
该研究通过激活注入、稀疏自动编码器和线性探针方法，分析了六种80M至7B参数规模的视觉-语言-动作模型如何将多模态输入转化为动作。视觉通路在所有架构中主导动作生成，注入基线激活可恢复近似行为，跨任务注入使机器人99.8%情况下沿源任务轨迹运动，揭示了与场景坐标绑定的运动程序。语言敏感性取决于任务结构：当视觉上下文唯一定义任务时语言被忽略，当多个目标共享场景时语言至关重要，如libero_goal任务在错误提示下性能骤降（94%→10%），而libero_object任务表现稳定（60–100%）。三种多通路架构中，专家通路编码运动程序，VLM通路编码目标语义，且二者激活子空间可分离。多数架构依赖逐词SAE处理以保证动作保真度，X-VLA则受益于均值池化，对比识别可恢复82+种操作概念，因果消融显示零效应率28–92%，与表示宽度无关。
MonoArt: Progressive Structural Reasoning for Monocular Articulated 3D Reconstruction
Authors: Haitian Li, Haozhe Xie, Junxiang Xu, Beichen Wen, Fangzhou Hong, Ziwei Liu
First: 2026-03-19T17:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:59:52+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://lihaitian.com/MonoArt
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reconstructing articulated 3D objects from a single image requires jointly inferring object geometry, part structure, and motion parameters from limited visual evidence. A key difficulty lies in the entanglement between motion cues and object structure, which makes direct articulation regression unstable. Existing methods address this challenge through multi-view supervision, retrieval-based assembly, or auxiliary video generation, often sacrificing scalability or efficiency. We present MonoArt, a unified framework grounded in progressive structural reasoning. Rather than predicting articulation directly from image features, MonoArt progressively transforms visual observations into canonical geometry, structured part representations, and motion-aware embeddings within a single architecture. This structured reasoning process enables stable and interpretable articulation inference without external motion templates or multi-stage pipelines. Extensive experiments on PartNet-Mobility demonstrate that OM achieves state-of-the-art performance in both reconstruction accuracy and inference speed. The framework further generalizes to robotic manipulation and articulated scene reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MonoArt：面向单目可变形3D重建的渐进式结构推理
从单张图像重建可变形3D物体需联合推断物体几何、部件结构与运动参数，难点在于运动线索与物体结构的耦合导致直接运动回归不稳定。现有方法通过多视角监督、基于检索的组装或辅助视频生成应对，常牺牲可扩展性或效率。我们提出MonoArt，一种基于渐进式结构推理的统一框架。该框架不直接从图像特征预测运动，而是在单一架构内逐步将视觉观测转化为规范几何、结构化部件表示与运动感知嵌入。此结构化推理过程实现稳定且可解释的运动推断，无需外部运动模板或多阶段流程。在PartNet-Mobility上的大量实验表明，MonoArt在重建精度与推理速度上均达当前最优。该框架还可推广至机器人操作与可变形场景重建。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of directly regressing articulation from monocular images due to entangled motion and structure cues, MonoArt introduces a unified framework that progressively transforms visual inputs into structured representations: canonical geometry, part-based structure, and motion-aware embeddings. Instead of relying on multi-view data, retrieval, or video generation, the method uses a single architecture to reason about structure incrementally, enabling stable and interpretable 3D reconstruction without external motion templates. Experiments on PartNet-Mobility show it achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and speed, and demonstrates generalization to robotic manipulation and articulated scene reconstruction.
为了解决从单张图像重建可动3D物体时运动与结构耦合导致直接回归不稳定的问题，MonoArt提出基于渐进式结构推理的统一框架。它不直接预测运动参数，而是通过单模型逐步将视觉输入转化为规范几何、结构化部件表示和运动感知嵌入，无需外部模板或多阶段流程。在PartNet-Mobility上的实验表明其在重建精度与推理速度上达到当前最优，且可扩展至机器人操作与可动场景重建。
NavTrust: Benchmarking Trustworthiness for Embodied Navigation
Authors: Huaide Jiang, Yash Chaudhary, Yuping Wang, Zehao Wang, Raghav Sharma, Manan Mehta, Yang Zhou, Lichao Sun, Zhiwen Fan, Zhengzhong Tu, Jiachen Li
First: 2026-03-19T17:59:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:59:51+00:00
Comments: Project Website: https://navtrust.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
There are two major categories of embodied navigation: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN), where agents navigate by following natural language instructions; and Object-Goal Navigation (OGN), where agents navigate to a specified target object. However, existing work primarily evaluates model performance under nominal conditions, overlooking the potential corruptions that arise in real-world settings. To address this gap, we present NavTrust, a unified benchmark that systematically corrupts input modalities, including RGB, depth, and instructions, in realistic scenarios and evaluates their impact on navigation performance. To our best knowledge, NavTrust is the first benchmark that exposes embodied navigation agents to diverse RGB-Depth corruptions and instruction variations in a unified framework. Our extensive evaluation of seven state-of-the-art approaches reveals substantial performance degradation under realistic corruptions, which highlights critical robustness gaps and provides a roadmap toward more trustworthy embodied navigation systems. Furthermore, we systematically evaluate four distinct mitigation strategies to enhance robustness against RGB-Depth and instructions corruptions. Our base models include Uni-NaVid and ETPNav. We deployed them on a real mobile robot and observed improved robustness to corruptions. The project website is: https://navtrust.github.io.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NavTrust：具身导航可信度基准
具身导航主要分为两类：视觉-语言导航（VLN），即代理根据自然语言指令导航；以及目标对象导航（OGN），即代理导航至指定目标物体。然而，现有工作主要在理想条件下评估模型性能，忽视了现实场景中可能存在的干扰。为弥补这一不足，我们提出了NavTrust，一个统一基准，系统性地在真实场景中对RGB、深度和指令等输入模态进行干扰，并评估其对导航性能的影响。据我们所知，NavTrust是首个在统一框架下暴露具身导航代理于多样RGB-深度干扰和指令变化的基准。我们对七种前沿方法的广泛评估表明，在真实干扰下性能显著下降，揭示了关键的鲁棒性缺陷，并为构建更可信的具身导航系统提供了路径。此外，我们系统评估了四种不同的缓解策略，以增强对RGB-深度和指令干扰的鲁棒性。我们的基线模型包括Uni-NaVid和ETPNav，并在真实移动机器人上部署，观察到其对干扰的鲁棒性提升。项目网站：https://navtrust.github.io。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of robustness evaluation in embodied navigation under real-world corruptions, NavTrust introduces a unified benchmark that systematically corrupts RGB, depth, and instruction inputs in realistic scenarios to assess navigation performance degradation. It evaluates seven state-of-the-art models, revealing significant performance drops under realistic corruptions, thereby exposing critical robustness gaps. The study also tests four mitigation strategies, finding that deploying robustified models on a real mobile robot improves resilience to corruptions, offering a clear path toward more trustworthy navigation systems.
为应对现有导航系统在真实世界干扰下鲁棒性评估缺失的问题，NavTrust构建了一个统一基准，系统性地在真实场景中对RGB、深度和指令输入进行干扰，评估导航性能下降情况。对七种先进模型的广泛测试显示，其在现实干扰下性能显著下降，揭示了关键的鲁棒性缺陷。研究还测试了四种缓解策略，结果表明在真实移动机器人上部署优化模型可提升对干扰的抗性，为构建更可靠的导航系统提供了明确路径。
Bridging Semantic and Kinematic Conditions with Diffusion-based Discrete Motion Tokenizer
Authors: Chenyang Gu, Mingyuan Zhang, Haozhe Xie, Zhongang Cai, Lei Yang, Ziwei Liu
First: 2026-03-19T17:59:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:59:51+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://rheallyc.github.io/projects/motok GitHub: https://github.com/rheallyc/MoTok
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Prior motion generation largely follows two paradigms: continuous diffusion models that excel at kinematic control, and discrete token-based generators that are effective for semantic conditioning. To combine their strengths, we propose a three-stage framework comprising condition feature extraction (Perception), discrete token generation (Planning), and diffusion-based motion synthesis (Control). Central to this framework is MoTok, a diffusion-based discrete motion tokenizer that decouples semantic abstraction from fine-grained reconstruction by delegating motion recovery to a diffusion decoder, enabling compact single-layer tokens while preserving motion fidelity. For kinematic conditions, coarse constraints guide token generation during planning, while fine-grained constraints are enforced during control through diffusion-based optimization. This design prevents kinematic details from disrupting semantic token planning. On HumanML3D, our method significantly improves controllability and fidelity over MaskControl while using only one-sixth of the tokens, reducing trajectory error from 0.72 cm to 0.08 cm and FID from 0.083 to 0.029. Unlike prior methods that degrade under stronger kinematic constraints, ours improves fidelity, reducing FID from 0.033 to 0.014.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于扩散模型的离散运动标记器：弥合语义与运动学条件
先前的运动生成主要遵循两种范式：擅长运动学控制的连续扩散模型，以及适用于语义条件的离散标记生成器。为结合二者优势，我们提出三阶段框架：条件特征提取（感知）、离散标记生成（规划）和基于扩散的运动合成（控制）。核心是MoTok，一种基于扩散的离散运动标记器，通过将运动恢复任务委派给扩散解码器，实现语义抽象与精细重建的解耦，从而在保持运动保真度的同时使用紧凑的单层标记。对于运动学条件，规划阶段由粗约束引导标记生成，控制阶段通过扩散优化施加精细约束，避免运动学细节干扰语义标记规划。在HumanML3D数据集上，我们的方法相比MaskControl显著提升可控性与保真度，仅用其六分之一标记，轨迹误差从0.72厘米降至0.08厘米，FID从0.083降至0.029。与先前方法在强运动学约束下性能下降不同，我们的方法反而提升保真度，FID从0.033降至0.014。
Summary / 总结
Prior motion generation largely follows two paradigms: continuous diffusion models that excel at kinematic control, and discrete token-based generators that are effective for semantic conditioning.
为统一运动生成中的语义条件与运动学控制，本文提出MoTok，一种基于扩散的离散运动分词器，嵌入三阶段框架：感知、规划与控制。MoTok通过扩散解码器分离语义抽象与运动重建，实现紧凑单层分词且保持运动保真度。规划阶段由粗粒度运动约束引导分词生成，控制阶段通过扩散优化施加细粒度约束，避免运动学细节干扰语义规划。在HumanML3D数据集上，MoTok相较MaskControl将轨迹误差从0.72厘米降至0.08厘米，FID从0.083降至0.029，且仅用六分之一的分词量；在更强运动约束下，其保真度进一步提升，FID降至0.014。
SAMA: Factorized Semantic Anchoring and Motion Alignment for Instruction-Guided Video Editing
Authors: Xinyao Zhang, Wenkai Dong, Yuxin Song, Bo Fang, Qi Zhang, Jing Wang, Fan Chen, Hui Zhang, Haocheng Feng, Yu Lu, Hang Zhou, Chun Yuan, Jingdong Wang
First: 2026-03-19T17:59:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:59:51+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current instruction-guided video editing models struggle to simultaneously balance precise semantic modifications with faithful motion preservation. While existing approaches rely on injecting explicit external priors (e.g., VLM features or structural conditions) to mitigate these issues, this reliance severely bottlenecks model robustness and generalization. To overcome this limitation, we present SAMA (factorized Semantic Anchoring and Motion Alignment), a framework that factorizes video editing into semantic anchoring and motion modeling. First, we introduce Semantic Anchoring, which establishes a reliable visual anchor by jointly predicting semantic tokens and video latents at sparse anchor frames, enabling purely instruction-aware structural planning. Second, Motion Alignment pre-trains the same backbone on motion-centric video restoration pretext tasks (cube inpainting, speed perturbation, and tube shuffle), enabling the model to internalize temporal dynamics directly from raw videos. SAMA is optimized with a two-stage pipeline: a factorized pre-training stage that learns inherent semantic-motion representations without paired video-instruction editing data, followed by supervised fine-tuning on paired editing data. Remarkably, the factorized pre-training alone already yields strong zero-shot video editing ability, validating the proposed factorization. SAMA achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models and is competitive with leading commercial systems (e.g., Kling-Omni). Code, models, and datasets will be released.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of balancing semantic precision with motion fidelity in instruction-guided video editing, SAMA introduces a factorized framework that decouples semantic anchoring from motion modeling. It first establishes visual anchors via joint prediction of semantic tokens and video latents at sparse frames, enabling instruction-aware structural planning without external priors. Then, it pre-trains motion modeling on motion-centric tasks like cube inpainting and speed perturbation to internalize temporal dynamics. The two-stage training pipeline—factorized pre-training followed by supervised fine-tuning—enables strong zero-shot editing performance even without paired editing data, and SAMA outperforms existing open-source models while competing with commercial systems.
为解决指令引导视频编辑中语义修改与运动保持难以兼顾的问题，SAMA提出将视频编辑分解为语义锚定与运动建模两个独立模块。其首先通过联合预测稀疏帧的语义标记与视频潜在表示，建立无需外部先验的视觉锚点，实现纯指令驱动的结构规划；其次，通过在运动中心任务（如立方体修复、速度扰动、管状打乱）上预训练，使模型直接从原始视频中内化时序动态。两阶段训练流程（先因子化预训练，再监督微调）使模型在无配对编辑数据时仍具备强零样本编辑能力，且在开源模型中达到最先进水平，与主流商业系统表现相当。
Under One Sun: Multi-Object Generative Perception of Materials and Illumination
Authors: Nobuo Yoshii, Xinran Nicole Han, Ryo Kawahara, Todd Zickler, Ko Nishino
First: 2026-03-19T17:59:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:59:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Multi-Object Generative Perception (MultiGP), a generative inverse rendering method for stochastic sampling of all radiometric constituents -- reflectance, texture, and illumination -- underlying object appearance from a single image. Our key idea to solve this inherently ambiguous radiometric disentanglement is to leverage the fact that while their texture and reflectance may differ, objects in the same scene are all lit by the same illumination. MultiGP exploits this consensus to produce samples of reflectance, texture, and illumination from a single image of known shapes based on four key technical contributions: a cascaded end-to-end architecture that combines image-space and angular-space disentanglement; Coordinated Guidance for diffusion convergence to a single consistent illumination estimate; Axial Attention applied to facilitate ``cross-talk'' between objects of different reflectance; and a Texture Extraction ControlNet to preserve high-frequency texture details while ensuring decoupling from estimated lighting. Experimental results demonstrate that MultiGP effectively leverages the complementary spatial and frequency characteristics of multiple object appearances to recover individual texture and reflectance as well as the common illumination.
中文标题/摘要
标题：同一片阳光下：多物体生成感知材料与光照
我们提出了多物体生成感知（MultiGP），这是一种生成式逆渲染方法，可从单张图像中对所有辐射成分——反射率、纹理和光照——进行随机采样。为解决这一固有的辐射量解耦模糊问题，我们的核心思想是利用同一场景中所有物体虽纹理与反射率不同，但均受同一光照照射的事实。MultiGP基于四个关键技术贡献，从已知形状的单张图像中生成反射率、纹理和光照样本：一种级联端到端架构，结合图像空间与角度空间的解耦；用于引导扩散模型收敛至单一一致光照估计的协同引导机制；应用于促进不同反射率物体间“交叉通信”的轴向注意力机制；以及一种纹理提取控制网络，以在确保与估计光照解耦的同时保留高频纹理细节。实验结果表明，MultiGP能有效利用多个物体外观的空间与频率互补特性，恢复单个物体的纹理与反射率以及共同的光照。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of disentangling reflectance, texture, and illumination from a single image under ambiguous radiometric conditions, MultiGP introduces a generative inverse rendering method that samples all three components jointly. It leverages the shared illumination across multiple objects in the same scene to guide consistent lighting estimation. The method employs a cascaded end-to-end architecture combining image-space and angular-space disentanglement, Coordinated Guidance to converge diffusion to a single illumination, Axial Attention to enable cross-object reflectance interaction, and a Texture Extraction ControlNet to preserve high-frequency details while decoupling texture from lighting. Experiments show MultiGP successfully recovers individual object textures and reflectances while accurately estimating the common illumination, outperforming prior methods in both fidelity and disentanglement.
为解决单图像中反射率、纹理与光照难以分离的问题，MultiGP提出一种生成式逆渲染方法，联合采样三者并利用场景内物体共享同一光照的特性进行约束。其方法包含级联端到端架构（融合图像空间与角度空间解耦）、协同引导机制（确保光照收敛一致）、轴向注意力（促进不同反射率物体间交互）及纹理提取ControlNet（保留高频细节并解耦光照）。实验表明，MultiGP能有效恢复各物体独立纹理与反射率，并准确估计公共光照，优于现有方法在保真度与解耦性能上。
FinTradeBench: A Financial Reasoning Benchmark for LLMs
Authors: Yogesh Agrawal, Aniruddha Dutta, Md Mahadi Hasan, Santu Karmaker, Aritra Dutta
First: 2026-03-19T17:59:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:59:41+00:00
Comments: 8 pages main text, 22 pages total (including references and appendix). 5 figures, 14 tables. Preprint under review. Code and data will be made available upon publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-world financial decision-making is a challenging problem that requires reasoning over heterogeneous signals, including company fundamentals derived from regulatory filings and trading signals computed from price dynamics. Recently, with the advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs), financial analysts have begun to use them for financial decision-making tasks. However, existing financial question answering benchmarks for testing these models primarily focus on company balance sheet data and rarely evaluate reasoning over how company stocks trade in the market or their interactions with fundamentals. To take advantage of the strengths of both approaches, we introduce FinTradeBench, a benchmark for evaluating financial reasoning that integrates company fundamentals and trading signals. FinTradeBench contains 1,400 questions grounded in NASDAQ-100 companies over a ten-year historical window. The benchmark is organized into three reasoning categories: fundamentals-focused, trading-signal-focused, and hybrid questions requiring cross-signal reasoning. To ensure reliability at scale, we adopt a calibration-then-scaling framework that combines expert seed questions, multi-model response generation, intra-model self-filtering, numerical auditing, and human-LLM judge alignment. We evaluate 14 LLMs under zero-shot prompting and retrieval-augmented settings and witness a clear performance gap. Retrieval substantially improves reasoning over textual fundamentals, but provides limited benefit for trading-signal reasoning. These findings highlight fundamental challenges in the numerical and time-series reasoning for current LLMs and motivate future research in financial intelligence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FinTradeBench：面向大语言模型的金融推理基准
现实中的金融决策是一个复杂问题，需要对异构信号进行推理，包括从监管文件中推导出的公司基本面和从价格动态计算出的交易信号。随着大语言模型（LLMs）的发展，金融分析师开始将其用于金融决策任务。然而，现有的金融问答基准主要聚焦于公司资产负债表数据，很少评估模型对股票市场交易行为及其与基本面交互的推理能力。为结合两种方法的优势，我们提出了FinTradeBench，一个融合公司基本面与交易信号的金融推理评估基准。该基准包含1400个基于纳斯达克100指数公司、覆盖十年历史窗口的问题。基准分为三类推理任务：基本面导向、交易信号导向及需跨信号推理的混合问题。为确保大规模评估的可靠性，我们采用“校准-扩展”框架，结合专家种子问题、多模型响应生成、模型内自过滤、数值审计及人机评估对齐。我们在零样本提示和检索增强设置下评估14个LLM，观察到显著性能差异：检索显著提升对文本基本面的推理能力，但对交易信号推理提升有限。这些发现揭示了当前LLMs在数值与时间序列推理方面的根本性挑战，并推动未来金融智能研究。

Evaluating 14 LLMs under zero-shot and retrieval-augmented settings, they find that retrieval significantly boosts performance on textual fundamentals but offers little improvement for trading-signal reasoning, revealing persistent challenges in numerical and time-series reasoning for current LLMs.
为评估大语言模型在现实金融决策中的能力，作者构建了FinTradeBench基准，包含1400道基于纳斯达克100公司十年历史数据的问题，涵盖基本面、交易信号及混合推理三类任务。为确保评估可靠性，采用专家种子问题校准、多模型生成、模型自过滤、数值审计及人机对齐框架。在零样本和检索增强设置下测试14个模型，发现检索对基本面推理提升显著，但对交易信号推理帮助有限，揭示当前大模型在数值与时间序列推理上的根本性挑战。
EffectErase: Joint Video Object Removal and Insertion for High-Quality Effect Erasing
Authors: Yang Fu, Yike Zheng, Ziyun Dai, Henghui Ding
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-19T17:59:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:59:22+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026, Project Page: https://henghuiding.com/EffectErase/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video object removal aims to eliminate dynamic target objects and their visual effects, such as deformation, shadows, and reflections, while restoring seamless backgrounds. Recent diffusion-based video inpainting and object removal methods can remove the objects but often struggle to erase these effects and to synthesize coherent backgrounds. Beyond method limitations, progress is further hampered by the lack of a comprehensive dataset that systematically captures common object effects across varied environments for training and evaluation. To address this, we introduce VOR (Video Object Removal), a large-scale dataset that provides diverse paired videos, each consisting of one video where the target object is present with its effects and a counterpart where the object and effects are absent, with corresponding object masks. VOR contains 60K high-quality video pairs from captured and synthetic sources, covers five effects types, and spans a wide range of object categories as well as complex, dynamic multi-object scenes. Building on VOR, we propose EffectErase, an effect-aware video object removal method that treats video object insertion as the inverse auxiliary task within a reciprocal learning scheme. The model includes task-aware region guidance that focuses learning on affected areas and enables flexible task switching. Then, an insertion-removal consistency objective that encourages complementary behaviors and shared localization of effect regions and structural cues. Trained on VOR, EffectErase achieves superior performance in extensive experiments, delivering high-quality video object effect erasing across diverse scenarios.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing diffusion-based methods in erasing visual effects like shadows and reflections during video object removal, this work introduces VOR, a large-scale dataset of 60K paired videos capturing five types of effects across diverse objects and scenes. Building on VOR, EffectErase proposes a reciprocal learning framework that jointly trains object removal and insertion tasks, using region-guided attention to focus on affected areas and a consistency objective to align effect localization with structural cues. Experiments demonstrate that EffectErase outperforms prior methods in generating seamless, high-fidelity video backgrounds while effectively erasing dynamic visual artifacts across varied environments.
为解决动态目标及其视觉效应（如阴影、反射）的移除与背景无缝恢复难题，作者构建了包含6万对视频的VOR数据集，涵盖五类视觉效应与多样场景。在此基础上提出EffectErase方法，通过双向学习框架联合实现目标移除与插入，引入区域引导任务切换机制与插入-移除一致性目标，强化效应区域定位与结构线索对齐。实验表明，该方法在多种物体类别和复杂多目标场景中显著优于现有方法，实现高质量视觉效应消除与背景重建。
F2LLM-v2: Inclusive, Performant, and Efficient Embeddings for a Multilingual World
Authors: Ziyin Zhang, Zihan Liao, Hang Yu, Peng Di, Rui Wang
First: 2026-03-19T17:59:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:59:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present F2LLM-v2, a new family of general-purpose, multilingual embedding models in 8 distinct sizes ranging from 80M to 14B. Trained on a newly curated composite of 60 million publicly available high-quality data samples, F2LLM-v2 supports more than 200 languages, with a particular emphasis on previously underserved mid- and low-resource languages. By integrating a two-stage LLM-based embedding training pipeline with matryoshka learning, model pruning, and knowledge distillation techniques, we present models that are far more efficient than previous LLM-based embedding models while retaining competitive performances. Extensive evaluations confirm that F2LLM-v2-14B ranks first on 11 MTEB benchmarks, while the smaller models in the family also set a new state of the art for resource-constrained applications. To facilitate open-source embedding model research, we release all models, data, code, and intermediate checkpoints.

为满足对高效、高性能多语言嵌入的需求，特别是支持高资源与低资源语言，F2LLM-v2 提出一个从80M到14B参数的8个模型系列，基于6000万高质量样本数据集训练。方法结合两阶段LLM嵌入训练流程，辅以Matryoshka学习、模型剪枝与知识蒸馏，实现效率显著提升且性能不降。实验表明，14B模型在11个MTEB基准上排名第一，而较小模型在资源受限场景下也创下新性能纪录。
Spectrally-Guided Diffusion Noise Schedules
Authors: Carlos Esteves, Ameesh Makadia
First: 2026-03-19T17:59:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:59:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Denoising diffusion models are widely used for high-quality image and video generation. Their performance depends on noise schedules, which define the distribution of noise levels applied during training and the sequence of noise levels traversed during sampling. Noise schedules are typically handcrafted and require manual tuning across different resolutions. In this work, we propose a principled way to design per-instance noise schedules for pixel diffusion, based on the image's spectral properties. By deriving theoretical bounds on the efficacy of minimum and maximum noise levels, we design ``tight'' noise schedules that eliminate redundant steps. During inference, we propose to conditionally sample such noise schedules. Experiments show that our noise schedules improve generative quality of single-stage pixel diffusion models, particularly in the low-step regime.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于频谱引导的扩散噪声调度
去噪扩散模型广泛用于高质量图像和视频生成，其性能依赖于噪声调度，即训练时噪声水平的分布及采样时噪声水平的序列。噪声调度通常由人工设计，需在不同分辨率下手动调整。本文提出一种基于图像频谱特性的原则性方法，为像素扩散设计每实例的噪声调度。通过推导最小和最大噪声水平的有效性理论界限，我们设计出“紧凑”的噪声调度，消除冗余步骤。推理时，我们提出条件采样此类噪声调度。实验表明，我们的噪声调度提升了单阶段像素扩散模型的生成质量，尤其在低步数情况下表现突出。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve generative quality in pixel diffusion models without manual tuning, this work proposes spectrally-guided noise schedules that adapt to each image's spectral properties. The method derives theoretical bounds on optimal noise levels and constructs compact, redundant-free schedules that conditionally sample during inference. Experimental results demonstrate that these schedules enhance image generation quality, especially under low-step sampling regimes, without requiring resolution-specific adjustments.
为提升像素扩散模型的生成质量并减少手动调参需求，本文提出基于图像频谱特性的噪声调度方法。该方法通过理论推导确定最优噪声上下限，构建无冗余的紧凑调度方案，并在推理时条件采样。实验表明，该方法在低步数采样下显著提升生成图像质量，且无需针对不同分辨率单独调整。
Online Learning and Equilibrium Computation with Ranking Feedback
Authors: Mingyang Liu, Yongshan Chen, Zhiyuan Fan, Gabriele Farina, Asuman Ozdaglar, Kaiqing Zhang
First: 2026-03-19T17:59:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:59:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Online learning in arbitrary, and possibly adversarial, environments has been extensively studied in sequential decision-making, and it is closely connected to equilibrium computation in game theory. Most existing online learning algorithms rely on \emph{numeric} utility feedback from the environment, which may be unavailable in human-in-the-loop applications and/or may be restricted by privacy concerns. In this paper, we study an online learning model in which the learner only observes a \emph{ranking} over a set of proposed actions at each timestep. We consider two ranking mechanisms: rankings induced by the \emph{instantaneous} utility at the current timestep, and rankings induced by the \emph{time-average} utility up to the current timestep, under both \emph{full-information} and \emph{bandit} feedback settings. Using the standard external-regret metric, we show that sublinear regret is impossible with instantaneous-utility ranking feedback in general. Moreover, when the ranking model is relatively deterministic, \emph{i.e.}, under the Plackett-Luce model with a temperature that is sufficiently small, sublinear regret is also impossible with time-average utility ranking feedback. We then develop new algorithms that achieve sublinear regret under the additional assumption that the utility sequence has sublinear total variation. Notably, for full-information time-average utility ranking feedback, this additional assumption can be removed. As a consequence, when all players in a normal-form game follow our algorithms, repeated play yields an approximate coarse correlated equilibrium. We also demonstrate the effectiveness of our algorithms in an online large-language-model routing task.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于排序反馈的在线学习与均衡计算
在线学习在任意且可能为对抗性的环境中已被广泛研究，与博弈论中的均衡计算密切相关。现有大多数在线学习算法依赖于环境提供的\emph{数值}效用反馈，但在人机交互应用中该反馈可能不可用，或受限于隐私问题。本文研究一种在线学习模型，其中学习者仅在每一步观察一组提议动作的\emph{排序}。我们考虑两种排序机制：由\emph{瞬时}效用在当前时间步诱导的排序，以及由\emph{时间平均}效用至当前时间步诱导的排序，分别在\emph{完全信息}和\emph{多臂老虎机}反馈设置下。使用标准外部遗憾度量，我们证明在一般情况下，瞬时效用排序反馈下不可能实现次线性遗憾。此外，当排序模型相对确定性时，即在温度参数足够小的Plackett-Luce模型下，时间平均效用排序反馈下同样不可能实现次线性遗憾。随后，我们开发了新算法，在额外假设效用序列总变差为次线性时，可实现次线性遗憾。值得注意的是，对于完全信息下时间平均效用排序反馈，该额外假设可被移除。因此，当正常形式博弈中所有玩家遵循我们的算法时，重复博弈可产生近似粗相关均衡。我们还在在线大语言模型路由任务中展示了算法的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of numeric utility feedback in human-in-the-loop and privacy-sensitive settings, this paper investigates online learning with only ranking feedback over actions. It considers two ranking types—based on instantaneous or time-average utility—and analyzes both full-information and bandit settings. The authors show that sublinear regret is generally impossible under instantaneous utility rankings, and even under time-average rankings, it remains impossible under deterministic models like the Plackett-Luce with low temperature. However, under the additional assumption of sublinear total variation in the utility sequence, new algorithms achieve sublinear regret; this assumption can be dropped for full-information time-average feedback. As a consequence, when multiple players use these algorithms in a normal-form game, repeated play converges to an approximate coarse correlated equilibrium. Experiments in an online large-language-model routing task confirm the algorithms' practical effectiveness.
为解决人类参与或隐私受限场景下数值效用反馈不可用的问题，本文研究仅基于动作排序反馈的在线学习模型。考虑两种排序机制——基于瞬时效用或时间平均效用——以及两种反馈设置——完全信息与带状反馈。作者证明，瞬时效用排序下一般无法实现次线性后悔，且在排序模型较确定（如低温Plackett-Luce模型）下，时间平均效用排序也无法实现次线性后悔。随后提出新算法，在效用序列总变差次线性条件下可实现次线性后悔，且完全信息下可移除此假设。因此，多玩家在正常型博弈中重复使用该算法可收敛至近似粗相关均衡。实验在在线大语言模型路由任务中验证了算法的有效性。
Nemotron-Cascade 2: Post-Training LLMs with Cascade RL and Multi-Domain On-Policy Distillation
Authors: Zhuolin Yang, Zihan Liu, Yang Chen, Wenliang Dai, Boxin Wang, Sheng-Chieh Lin, Chankyu Lee, Yangyi Chen, Dongfu Jiang, Jiafan He, Renjie Pi, Grace Lam, Nayeon Lee, Alexander Bukharin, Mohammad Shoeybi, Bryan Catanzaro, Wei Ping
First: 2026-03-19T17:58:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:58:52+00:00
Comments: We release the model and data at https://huggingface.co/collections/nvidia/nemotron-cascade-2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce Nemotron-Cascade 2, an open 30B MoE model with 3B activated parameters that delivers best-in-class reasoning and strong agentic capabilities. Despite its compact size, its mathematical and coding reasoning performance approaches that of frontier open models. It is the second open-weight LLM, after DeepSeekV3.2-Speciale-671B-A37B, to achieve Gold Medal-level performance in the 2025 International Mathematical Olympiad (IMO), the International Olympiad in Informatics (IOI), and the ICPC World Finals, demonstrating remarkably high intelligence density with 20x fewer parameters. In contrast to Nemotron-Cascade 1, the key technical advancements are as follows. After SFT on a meticulously curated dataset, we substantially expand Cascade RL to cover a much broader spectrum of reasoning and agentic domains. Furthermore, we introduce multi-domain on-policy distillation from the strongest intermediate teacher models for each domain throughout the Cascade RL process, allowing us to efficiently recover benchmark regressions and sustain strong performance gains along the way. We release the collection of model checkpoint and training data.
中文标题/摘要

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-performance, parameter-efficient LLMs, Nemotron-Cascade 2 employs Cascade RL expanded across diverse reasoning and agentic domains, combined with multi-domain on-policy distillation from intermediate teacher models to preserve performance gains. The 30B MoE model, activating only 3B parameters, achieves Gold Medal-level results in the 2025 IMO, IOI, and ICPC World Finals, outperforming frontier models with 20x fewer parameters, demonstrating exceptional intelligence density.
为提升参数效率与性能，Nemotron-Cascade 2采用扩展至多领域推理与代理任务的级联强化学习，并结合多领域在线蒸馏技术，从各领域最强中间教师模型中持续优化性能。该30B稀疏模型仅激活3B参数，即在2025年IMO、IOI和ICPC世界总决赛中斩获金牌级表现，参数量仅为前沿模型的五分之一，展现出极高的智能密度。
DriveTok: 3D Driving Scene Tokenization for Unified Multi-View Reconstruction and Understanding
Authors: Dong Zhuo, Wenzhao Zheng, Sicheng Zuo, Siming Yan, Lu Hou, Jie Zhou, Jiwen Lu
First: 2026-03-19T17:58:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:58:22+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://paryi555.github.io/DriveTok/ Code: https://github.com/paryi555/DriveTok
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
With the growing adoption of vision-language-action models and world models in autonomous driving systems, scalable image tokenization becomes crucial as the interface for the visual modality. However, most existing tokenizers are designed for monocular and 2D scenes, leading to inefficiency and inter-view inconsistency when applied to high-resolution multi-view driving scenes. To address this, we propose DriveTok, an efficient 3D driving scene tokenizer for unified multi-view reconstruction and understanding. DriveTok first obtains semantically rich visual features from vision foundation models and then transforms them into the scene tokens with 3D deformable cross-attention. For decoding, we employ a multi-view transformer to reconstruct multi-view features from the scene tokens and use multiple heads to obtain RGB, depth, and semantic reconstructions. We also add a 3D head directly on the scene tokens for 3D semantic occupancy prediction for better spatial awareness. With the multiple training objectives, DriveTok learns unified scene tokens that integrate semantic, geometric, and textural information for efficient multi-view tokenization. Extensive experiments on the widely used nuScenes dataset demonstrate that the scene tokens from DriveTok perform well on image reconstruction, semantic segmentation, depth prediction, and 3D occupancy prediction tasks.
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for scalable and consistent visual representation in autonomous driving systems, DriveTok introduces a 3D scene tokenizer designed to unify multi-view reconstruction and understanding. It leverages vision foundation models to extract semantic visual features, then encodes them into scene tokens via 3D deformable cross-attention. For decoding, a multi-view transformer reconstructs RGB, depth, and semantic maps from tokens, while a dedicated 3D head predicts occupancy for spatial awareness. Evaluated on nuScenes, DriveTok’s tokens outperform existing methods in image reconstruction, semantic segmentation, depth prediction, and 3D occupancy tasks, demonstrating strong generalization across modalities.
为解决现有2D图像tokenizer在多视角驾驶场景中效率低、跨视角不一致的问题，DriveTok提出一种3D场景tokenizer，通过视觉基础模型提取语义特征，再用3D可变形交叉注意力编码为场景token。解码时，多视角Transformer从token重建RGB、深度与语义图，另设3D头预测3D语义占据以增强空间感知。在nuScenes数据集上的实验表明，DriveTok在图像重建、语义分割、深度预测和3D占据预测任务上均达到最优性能，验证其统一整合几何、语义与纹理信息的能力。
Rethinking Vector Field Learning for Generative Segmentation
Authors: Chaoyang Wang, Yaobo Liang, Boci Peng, Fan Duan, Jingdong Wang, Yunhai Tong
First: 2026-03-19T17:58:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:58:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Taming diffusion models for generative segmentation has attracted increasing attention. While existing approaches primarily focus on architectural tweaks or training heuristics, there remains a limited understanding of the intrinsic mismatch between continuous flow matching objectives and discrete perception tasks. In this work, we revisit diffusion segmentation from the perspective of vector field learning. We identify two key limitations of the commonly used flow matching objective: gradient vanishing and trajectory traversing, which result in slow convergence and poor class separation. To tackle these issues, we propose a principled vector field reshaping strategy that augments the learned velocity field with a detached distance-aware correction term. This correction introduces both attractive and repulsive interactions, enhancing gradient magnitudes near centroids while preserving the original diffusion training framework. Furthermore, we design a computationally efficient, quasi-random category encoding scheme inspired by Kronecker sequences, which integrates seamlessly with an end-to-end pixel neural field framework for pixel-level semantic alignment. Extensive experiments consistently demonstrate significant improvements over vanilla flow matching approaches, substantially narrowing the performance gap between generative segmentation and strong discriminative specialists.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新思考用于生成分割的向量场学习
控制扩散模型以实现生成分割已引起越来越多的关注。尽管现有方法主要关注架构调整或训练启发式方法，但对连续流匹配目标与离散感知任务之间固有不匹配的理解仍显不足。在本工作中，我们从向量场学习的角度重新审视扩散分割。我们识别出常用流匹配目标的两个关键局限：梯度消失和轨迹遍历，导致收敛缓慢和类别分离效果差。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种原则性的向量场重塑策略，通过添加一个独立的距离感知校正项来增强所学习的速度场。该校正项引入了吸引和排斥的相互作用，增强了靠近质心区域的梯度幅度，同时保留了原始扩散训练框架。此外，我们设计了一种受克罗内克序列启发的计算高效的准随机类别编码方案，该方案可无缝集成到端到端像素神经场框架中，实现像素级语义对齐。大量实验一致表明，该方法显著优于基础流匹配方法，大幅缩小了生成分割与强判别专家之间的性能差距。

Motivated by the mismatch between continuous flow matching objectives and discrete segmentation tasks, this work reexamines diffusion-based generative segmentation through vector field learning. Experiments show consistent performance gains over vanilla flow matching, significantly reducing the gap between generative and discriminative segmentation methods.

LVOmniBench: Pioneering Long Audio-Video Understanding Evaluation for Omnimodal LLMs
Authors: Keda Tao, Yuhua Zheng, Jia Xu, Wenjie Du, Kele Shao, Hesong Wang, Xueyi Chen, Xin Jin, Junhan Zhu, Bohan Yu, Weiqiang Wang, Jian Liu, Can Qin, Yulun Zhang, Ming-Hsuan Yang, Huan Wang
First: 2026-03-19T17:58:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:58:13+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://kd-tao.github.io/LVOmniBench/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advancements in omnimodal large language models (OmniLLMs) have significantly improved the comprehension of audio and video inputs. However, current evaluations primarily focus on short audio and video clips ranging from 10 seconds to 5 minutes, failing to reflect the demands of real-world applications, where videos typically run for tens of minutes. To address this critical gap, we introduce LVOmniBench, a new benchmark designed specifically for the cross-modal comprehension of long-form audio and video. This dataset comprises high-quality videos sourced from open platforms that feature rich audio-visual dynamics. Through rigorous manual selection and annotation, LVOmniBench comprises 275 videos, ranging in duration from 10 to 90 minutes, and 1,014 question-answer (QA) pairs. LVOmniBench aims to rigorously evaluate the capabilities of OmniLLMs across domains, including long-term memory, temporal localization, fine-grained understanding, and multimodal perception. Our extensive evaluation reveals that current OmniLLMs encounter significant challenges when processing extended audio-visual inputs. Open-source models generally achieve accuracies below 35%, whereas the Gemini 3 Pro reaches a peak accuracy of approximately 65%. We anticipate that this dataset, along with our empirical findings, will stimulate further research and the development of advanced models capable of resolving complex cross-modal understanding problems within long-form audio-visual contexts.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate omnimodal large language models (OmniLLMs) on real-world long-form audio-video understanding, the authors introduce LVOmniBench, a new benchmark comprising 275 high-quality videos (10–90 minutes) and 1,014 QA pairs, manually curated for rich multimodal dynamics. The benchmark targets core capabilities such as long-term memory, temporal localization, and fine-grained cross-modal perception. Experimental results show that current OmniLLMs struggle with long-form inputs, with open-source models scoring below 35% accuracy, while Gemini 3 Pro achieves around 65%, highlighting a significant performance gap and underscoring the need for improved architectures and training strategies.
为填补当前多模态大语言模型在长音频视频理解能力评估方面的空白，作者构建了LVOmniBench基准数据集，包含275个时长10至90分钟的高质量视频及1014组问答对，旨在检验模型的长期记忆、时序定位与细粒度多模态理解能力。实验表明，开源模型准确率普遍低于35%，而Gemini 3 Pro最高可达约65%，凸显现有模型在处理长时序多模态输入时仍面临显著挑战。
DreamPartGen: Semantically Grounded Part-Level 3D Generation via Collaborative Latent Denoising
Authors: Tianjiao Yu, Xinzhuo Li, Muntasir Wahed, Jerry Xiong, Yifan Shen, Ying Shen, Ismini Lourentzou
First: 2026-03-19T17:58:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:58:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding and generating 3D objects as compositions of meaningful parts is fundamental to human perception and reasoning. However, most text-to-3D methods overlook the semantic and functional structure of parts. While recent part-aware approaches introduce decomposition, they remain largely geometry-focused, lacking semantic grounding and failing to model how parts align with textual descriptions or their inter-part relations. We propose DreamPartGen, a framework for semantically grounded, part-aware text-to-3D generation. DreamPartGen introduces Duplex Part Latents (DPLs) that jointly model each part's geometry and appearance, and Relational Semantic Latents (RSLs) that capture inter-part dependencies derived from language. A synchronized co-denoising process enforces mutual geometric and semantic consistency, enabling coherent, interpretable, and text-aligned 3D synthesis. Across multiple benchmarks, DreamPartGen delivers state-of-the-art performance in geometric fidelity and text-shape alignment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DreamPartGen：通过协同潜在去噪实现语义引导的部件级3D生成

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate 3D objects that reflect their semantic and functional part structures, DreamPartGen introduces a novel framework that jointly models geometry and appearance via Duplex Part Latents and captures inter-part relationships through Relational Semantic Latents derived from language. The method employs a synchronized co-denoising process to enforce geometric and semantic consistency, enabling coherent 3D synthesis aligned with textual descriptions. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in geometric fidelity and text-shape alignment compared to existing part-aware methods.

$R$-equivalence on Cubic Surfaces I: Existing Cases with Non-Trivial Universal Equivalence
Authors: Dimitri Kanevsky, Julian Salazar, Matt Harvey
First: 2026-03-19T17:57:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:57:38+00:00
Comments: 23 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Let $V$ be a smooth cubic surface over a $p$-adic field $k$ with good reduction. Swinnerton-Dyer (1981) proved that $R$-equivalence is trivial on $V(k)$ except perhaps if $V$ is one of three special types--those whose $R$-equivalence he could not bound by proving the universal (admissible) equivalence is trivial. We consider all surfaces $V$ currently known to have non-trivial universal equivalence. Beyond being intractable to Swinnerton-Dyer's approach, we observe that if these surfaces also had non-trivial $R$-equivalence, they would contradict Colliot-Thélène and Sansuc's conjecture regarding the $k$-rationality of universal torsors for geometrically rational surfaces. By devising new methods to study $R$-equivalence, we prove that for 2-adic surfaces with all-Eckardt reductions (the third special type, which contains every existing case of non-trivial universal equivalence), $R$-equivalence is trivial or of exponent 2. For the explicit cases, we confirm triviality: the diagonal cubic $X^3+Y^3+Z^3+ζ_3 T^3=0$ over $\mathbb{Q}_2(ζ_3)$--answering a long-standing question of Manin's (Cubic Forms, 1972)--and the cubic with universal equivalence of exponent 2 (Kanevsky, 1982). This is the first in a series of works derived from a year of interactions with generative AI models such as AlphaEvolve and Gemini 3 Deep Think, with the latter proving many of our lemmas. We disclose the timeline and nature of their use towards this paper, and describe our broader AI-assisted research program in a companion report (in preparation).
Summary / 总结
Let $V$ be a smooth cubic surface over a $p$-adic field $k$ with good reduction.
受斯温顿-戴维1981年关于p进域上光滑三次曲面R等价通常平凡的结果启发，该文研究其未解决的三种特殊情形是否也存在非平凡R等价，这将与科利奥-特勒和桑苏关于几何有理曲面万有托尔斯k-有理性的猜想矛盾。作者借助与生成式AI模型合作开发的新方法，证明所有Eckardt点减缩的2进域三次曲面（包含所有已知非平凡万有等价情形）的R等价要么平凡，要么阶为2。具体而言，他们证实了曼宁提出的对角三次曲面在Q₂(ζ₃)上R等价平凡，解决了长期悬而未决的问题，并验证了卡内夫斯基构造的阶为2情形。
Constitutive vs. Corrective: A Causal Taxonomy of Human Runtime Involvement in AI Systems
Authors: Kevin Baum, Johann Laux
First: 2026-03-19T17:57:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:57:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As AI systems increasingly permeate high-stakes decision-making, the terminology regarding human involvement - Human-in-the-Loop (HITL), Human-on-the-Loop (HOTL), and Human Oversight - has become vexingly ambiguous. This ambiguity complicates interdisciplinary collaboration between computer science, law, philosophy, psychology, and sociology and can lead to regulatory uncertainty. We propose a clarification grounded in causal structure, focused on human involvement during the runtime of AI systems. The distinction between HITL and HOTL, we argue, is not primarily spatial but causal: HITL is constitutive (a human contribution is necessary for the decision output), while HOTL is corrective (external to the primary causal chain, capable of preventing or modifying outputs). Within HOTL, we distinguish three temporal modes - synchronous, asynchronous, and anticipatory - situated within a nested model of provider and deployer runtime that clarifies their different capacities for intervention. A second, orthogonal dimension captures cognitive integration: whether human and machine operate as complementary or hybrid intelligence, yielding four structurally distinct configurations. Finally, we distinguish these descriptive categories from the normative requirements they serve: statutory "Human Oversight" is a specific normative mode of HOTL that demands not merely a corrective causal position, but genuine preparedness and capacity for effective intervention. Because the same person may occupy both HITL and HOTL roles simultaneously, we argue that this role duality must be treated as a design problem requiring architectural and epistemic mitigation rather than mere acknowledgment.
Summary / 总结
As AI systems grow more central to high-stakes decisions, the ambiguous terminology surrounding human involvement—such as HITL, HOTL, and oversight—hinders interdisciplinary collaboration and regulatory clarity. The authors propose a causal taxonomy distinguishing HITL as constitutive (human input necessary for output) from HOTL as corrective (external to the causal chain, capable of modifying or preventing outputs). Within HOTL, three temporal modes—synchronous, asynchronous, anticipatory—are mapped to nested provider/deployer runtime models, while a second dimension of cognitive integration (complementary vs. hybrid intelligence) yields four structural configurations. The paper further clarifies that statutory 'Human Oversight' is a normative subset of HOTL requiring preparedness and capacity for intervention, not just presence. Crucially, since individuals may simultaneously occupy HITL and HOTL roles, the authors argue this duality must be addressed through architectural and epistemic design solutions, not merely acknowledged.
随着AI系统在高风险决策中日益重要，HITL与HOTL等术语的模糊性阻碍了跨学科协作与监管明确性。本文提出一种基于因果结构的分类法，将HITL（构成性参与）定义为人类对输出结果的必要贡献，而HOTL（纠正性参与）则指人类在主因果链外进行干预以修正或阻止结果。HOTL进一步按时间模式（同步、异步、前瞻性）划分，并嵌套于提供者与部署者的运行时模型中，同时结合认知整合维度（互补或混合智能），形成四种结构差异的配置。研究还区分了描述性类别与规范性要求，指出法定“人类监督”不仅要求纠正性能力，更需具备有效干预的准备性。关键在于，同一人同时承担HITL与HOTL角色时，应通过架构与认知设计加以缓解，而非仅作形式承认。
Do VLMs Need Vision Transformers? Evaluating State Space Models as Vision Encoders
Authors: Shang-Jui Ray Kuo, Paola Cascante-Bonilla
First: 2026-03-19T17:56:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:56:32+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://lab-spell.github.io/vlm-ssm-vision-encoders/ ; Code: https://github.com/raykuo18/vlm-ssm-vision-encoders
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Large vision--language models (VLMs) often use a frozen vision backbone, whose image features are mapped into a large language model through a lightweight connector. While transformer-based encoders are the standard visual backbone, we ask whether state space model (SSM) vision backbones can be a strong alternative. We systematically evaluate SSM vision backbones for VLMs in a controlled setting. Under matched ImageNet-1K initialization, the SSM backbone achieves the strongest overall performance across both VQA and grounding/localization. We further adapt both SSM and ViT-family backbones with detection or segmentation training and find that dense-task tuning generally improves performance across families; after this adaptation, the SSM backbone remains competitive while operating at a substantially smaller model scale. We further observe that (i) higher ImageNet accuracy or larger backbones do not reliably translate into better VLM performance, and (ii) some visual backbones are unstable in localization. Based on these findings, we propose stabilization strategies that improve robustness for both backbone families and highlight SSM backbones as a strong alternative to transformer-based vision encoders in VLMs.
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether state space model (SSM) vision encoders can replace transformer-based backbones in large vision-language models (VLMs). Under matched ImageNet-1K initialization, SSM backbones outperformed ViT-family models across VQA and grounding tasks. After adaptation via detection or segmentation training, SSM models retained strong performance while being significantly smaller. The research also found that higher ImageNet accuracy or larger model size does not guarantee better VLM results, and some backbones exhibit instability in localization. The authors propose stabilization techniques that improve robustness for both backbone types, positioning SSMs as a viable, compact alternative to transformers in VLMs.
该研究探讨了状态空间模型（SSM）视觉编码器能否替代基于Transformer的视觉骨干网络用于大型视觉-语言模型（VLM）。在匹配ImageNet-1K初始化条件下，SSM骨干网络在VQA和定位任务中表现优于ViT类模型。经过检测或分割任务微调后，SSM模型仍保持强劲性能且规模显著更小。研究还发现，更高的ImageNet准确率或更大的模型规模并不必然带来更好的VLM效果，部分骨干网络在定位任务中表现不稳定。作者提出稳定化策略以提升两类骨干网络的鲁棒性，指出SSM是Transformer视觉编码器在VLM中有力的紧凑替代方案。
Enhancing Lexicon-Based Text Embeddings with Large Language Models
Authors: Yibin Lei, Tao Shen, Yu Cao, Andrew Yates
Venue: ACL 2025
First: 2025-01-16T18:57:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:55:24+00:00
Comments: ACL 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated exceptional performance on general-purpose text embedding tasks. While dense embeddings have dominated related research, we introduce the first lexicon-based embeddings (LENS) leveraging LLMs that achieve competitive performance on these tasks. LENS consolidates the vocabulary space through token embedding clustering to handle the issue of token redundancy in LLM vocabularies. To further improve performance, we investigate bidirectional attention and various pooling strategies. Specifically, LENS simplifies lexical matching with redundant vocabularies by assigning each dimension to a specific token cluster, where semantically similar tokens are grouped together. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LENS outperforms dense embeddings on the Massive Text Embedding Benchmark (MTEB), delivering compact representations with dimensionality comparable to dense counterparts. Furthermore, LENS inherently supports efficient embedding dimension pruning without any specialized objectives like Matryoshka Representation Learning. Notably, combining LENS with dense embeddings achieves state-of-the-art performance on the retrieval subset of MTEB (i.e., BEIR).
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用大语言模型增强基于词典的文本嵌入
近期的大语言模型（LLM）在通用文本嵌入任务中表现出色。尽管密集嵌入主导了相关研究，我们首次引入了利用LLM的基于词典的嵌入（LENS），在这些任务中实现了具有竞争力的性能。LENS通过词嵌入聚类整合词汇空间，以解决LLM词汇表中的词元冗余问题。为进一步提升性能，我们研究了双向注意力和多种池化策略。具体而言，LENS通过将每个维度分配给特定的词元簇，简化了冗余词汇的词典匹配，其中语义相似的词元被归为一组。大量实验表明，LENS在大规模文本嵌入基准（MTEB）上优于密集嵌入，同时提供与密集嵌入相当维度的紧凑表示。此外，LENS无需任何特殊目标（如Matryoshka表示学习）即可自然支持嵌入维度剪枝。值得注意的是，将LENS与密集嵌入结合，在MTEB的检索子集（即BEIR）上实现了最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and interpretable text embeddings, this work introduces LENS, the first lexicon-based embedding method leveraging large language models to compete with dense embeddings. LENS addresses token redundancy in LLM vocabularies by clustering token embeddings into semantic groups and assigning each dimension to a specific cluster, enabling simplified lexical matching. Experimental results on MTEB show LENS achieves competitive performance with compact dimensions, and it supports dimension pruning without additional training objectives. When combined with dense embeddings, LENS further boosts retrieval performance on BEIR, reaching state-of-the-art results.
为提升文本嵌入的效率与可解释性，本文提出LENS方法，首次利用大语言模型构建词典式嵌入，其性能可与稠密嵌入竞争。LENS通过聚类词嵌入为语义组，并将每个维度分配给特定组，以解决LLM词汇表冗余问题，简化词匹配。在MTEB实验中，LENS表现优异，维度与稠密嵌入相当，且与稠密嵌入结合后在BEIR检索子集上达到最先进水平。
RPiAE: A Representation-Pivoted Autoencoder Enhancing Both Image Generation and Editing
Authors: Yue Gong, Hongyu Li, Shanyuan Liu, Bo Cheng, Yuhang Ma, Liebucha Wu, Xiaoyu Wu, Manyuan Zhang, Dawei Leng, Yuhui Yin, Lijun Zhang
First: 2026-03-19T17:54:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:54:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models have become the dominant paradigm for image generation and editing, with latent diffusion models shifting denoising to a compact latent space for efficiency and scalability. Recent attempts to leverage pretrained visual representation models as tokenizer priors either align diffusion features to representation features or directly reuse representation encoders as frozen tokenizers. Although such approaches can improve generation metrics, they often suffer from limited reconstruction fidelity due to frozen encoders, which in turn degrades editing quality, as well as overly high-dimensional latents that make diffusion modeling difficult. To address these limitations, We propose Representation-Pivoted AutoEncoder, a representation-based tokenizer that improves both generation and editing. We introduce Representation-Pivot Regularization, a training strategy that enables a representation-initialized encoder to be fine-tuned for reconstruction while preserving the semantic structure of the pretrained representation space, followed by a variational bridge which compress latent space into a compact one for better diffusion modeling. We adopt an objective-decoupled stage-wise training strategy that sequentially optimizes generative tractability and reconstruction-fidelity objectives. Together, these components yield a tokenizer that preserves strong semantics, reconstructs faithfully, and produces latents with reduced diffusion modeling complexity. Experiments demonstrate that RPiAE outperforms other visual tokenizers on text-to-image generation and image editing, while delivering the best reconstruction fidelity among representation-based tokenizers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RPiAE：一种基于表示的自编码器，同时增强图像生成与编辑
扩散模型已成为图像生成与编辑的主流范式，其中潜扩散模型将去噪过程转移到紧凑的潜在空间以提升效率与可扩展性。近期尝试利用预训练视觉表示模型作为标记器先验，或对扩散特征对齐表示特征，或直接复用表示编码器作为冻结标记器。尽管此类方法可提升生成指标，但因冻结编码器导致重建保真度受限，进而降低编辑质量，且高维潜在空间使扩散建模困难。为解决上述局限，我们提出表示驱动自编码器（RPiAE），一种基于表示的标记器，可同时提升生成与编辑效果。我们引入表示锚定正则化训练策略，使初始化表示的编码器在保持预训练表示空间语义结构的前提下进行重建微调，并辅以变分桥压缩潜在空间以利于扩散建模。我们采用目标解耦的分阶段训练策略，依次优化生成可处理性与重建保真度目标。上述组件协同作用，生成的标记器兼具强语义性、高重建保真度及降低扩散建模复杂度的潜在表示。实验表明，RPiAE在文本到图像生成与图像编辑任务中优于其他视觉标记器，且在基于表示的标记器中重建保真度最佳。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of frozen representation encoders in diffusion-based image generation and editing, the authors propose RPiAE, a representation-pivoted autoencoder that enhances both tasks. The method introduces Representation-Pivot Regularization to fine-tune an encoder initialized from a pretrained visual representation model while preserving semantic structure, followed by a variational bridge to compress latent space for efficient diffusion modeling. A stage-wise training strategy decouples generative tractability and reconstruction fidelity optimization. Experimental results show RPiAE achieves superior performance in text-to-image generation and image editing compared to existing tokenizers, while also delivering the best reconstruction fidelity among representation-based approaches.
为解决冻结表示编码器在扩散模型图像生成与编辑中重建精度低、潜在空间维度过高的问题，本文提出RPiAE表示驱动自编码器，兼顾生成与编辑性能。其核心是通过表示枢轴正则化策略，在保留预训练表示空间语义结构的前提下微调编码器，并引入变分桥压缩潜在空间以提升扩散建模效率。采用分阶段训练策略，先后优化生成可塑性与重建保真度。实验表明，RPiAE在图像生成与编辑任务上优于其他视觉标记器，且在表示类标记器中重建保真度最高。
Robustness, Cost, and Attack-Surface Concentration in Phishing Detection
Authors: Julian Allagan, Mohamed Elbakary, Zohreh Safari, Weizheng Gao, Gabrielle Morgan, Essence Morgan, Vladimir Deriglazov
First: 2026-03-19T17:53:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:53:32+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 4 figures, 9 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Phishing detectors built on engineered website features attain near-perfect accuracy under i.i.d.\ evaluation, yet deployment security depends on robustness to post-deployment feature manipulation. We study this gap through a cost-aware evasion framework that models discrete, monotone feature edits under explicit attacker budgets. Three diagnostics are introduced: minimal evasion cost (MEC), the evasion survival rate $S(B)$, and the robustness concentration index (RCI). On the UCI Phishing Websites benchmark (11\,055 instances, 30 ternary features), Logistic Regression, Random Forests, Gradient Boosted Trees, and XGBoost all achieve $\mathrm{AUC}\ge 0.979$ under static evaluation. Under budgeted sanitization-style evasion, robustness converges across architectures: the median MEC equals 2 with full features, and over 80\% of successful minimal-cost evasions concentrate on three low-cost surface features. Feature restriction improves robustness only when it removes all dominant low-cost transitions. Under strict cost schedules, infrastructure-leaning feature sets exhibit 17-19\% infeasible mass for ensemble models, while the median MEC among evadable instances remains unchanged. We formalize this convergence: if a positive fraction of correctly detected phishing instances admit evasion through a single feature transition of minimal cost $c_{\min}$, no classifier can raise the corresponding MEC quantile above $c_{\min}$ without modifying the feature representation or cost model. Adversarial robustness in phishing detection is governed by feature economics rather than model complexity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：钓鱼检测中的鲁棒性、成本与攻击面集中度
基于工程化网站特征构建的钓鱼检测器在i.i.d.评估下可达到近完美的准确率，但部署安全依赖于对部署后特征操纵的鲁棒性。本文通过一个成本感知的逃避框架研究这一差距，该框架在显式攻击者预算下建模离散、单调的特征编辑。引入三个诊断指标：最小逃避成本（MEC）、逃避存活率S(B）和鲁棒性集中度指数（RCI）。在UCI钓鱼网站基准数据集（11,055个样本，30个三元特征）上，逻辑回归、随机森林、梯度提升树和XGBoost在静态评估下均达到AUC≥0.979。在预算约束的净化式逃避下，不同架构的鲁棒性趋于一致：全特征时中位数MEC为2，超过80%的最小成本成功逃避集中于三个低成本表面特征。仅当特征限制移除所有主导的低成本转换时，鲁棒性才会提升。在严格成本约束下，依赖基础设施的特征集在集成模型中表现出17-19%的不可行质量，但可逃避样本的中位数MEC保持不变。我们形式化了这一收敛性：若正比例的正确检测钓鱼样本可通过单次最小成本c_min的特征转换实现逃避，则任何分类器在不修改特征表示或成本模型的情况下，无法将对应MEC分位数提升至c_min以上。钓鱼检测中的对抗鲁棒性由特征经济学而非模型复杂度决定。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between high accuracy in static phishing detection and poor real-world robustness, this study introduces a cost-aware evasion framework that models discrete feature edits under attacker budgets. It evaluates four classifiers—Logistic Regression, Random Forests, Gradient Boosted Trees, and XGBoost—on the UCI Phishing Websites dataset, finding near-perfect AUC under static conditions. Under budgeted evasion, robustness converges across models: median minimal evasion cost (MEC) is 2 with full features, and over 80% of successful evasions target three low-cost surface features. Feature restriction only improves robustness if it eliminates all dominant low-cost transitions; otherwise, it fails to help. Under strict cost constraints, infrastructure-leaning features cause 17–19% infeasible mass for ensembles, yet median MEC remains unchanged. The study formalizes that robustness is governed by feature economics, not model complexity, and that no classifier can raise MEC quantiles above the minimal cost without altering feature or cost models.
为弥合静态评估中高准确率与实际部署中脆弱性之间的差距，本文提出一种基于成本的对抗性逃逸框架，模拟攻击者在预算约束下对离散特征的编辑。在UCI钓鱼网站数据集上，四种模型（逻辑回归、随机森林、梯度提升树、XGBoost）在静态评估下AUC均≥0.979。在预算逃逸场景下，模型鲁棒性趋于一致：完整特征集的中位数最小逃逸成本（MEC）为2，且80%以上成功逃逸集中在三个低成本表面特征上。仅当特征限制移除所有主导的低成本转换时，鲁棒性才提升；否则无效。在严格成本约束下，依赖基础设施的特征集导致17–19%的不可行样本，但逃逸样本的中位数MEC不变。研究正式证明：鲁棒性由特征经济性决定，而非模型复杂度，且任何模型若存在单特征最小成本逃逸，其MEC分位数无法超越该成本，除非修改特征或成本模型。
Tinted Frames: Question Framing Blinds Vision-Language Models
Authors: Wan-Cyuan Fan, Jiayun Luo, Declan Kutscher, Leonid Sigal, Ritwik Gupta
First: 2026-03-19T17:53:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:53:09+00:00
Comments: Preprint. Project page: https://davidhalladay.github.io/tinted_frames_demo/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have been shown to be blind, often underutilizing their visual inputs even on tasks that require visual reasoning. In this work, we demonstrate that VLMs are selectively blind. They modulate the amount of attention applied to visual inputs based on linguistic framing even when alternative framings demand identical visual reasoning. Using visual attention as a probe, we quantify how framing alters both the amount and distribution of attention over the image. Constrained framings, such as multiple choice and yes/no, induce substantially lower attention to image context compared to open-ended, reduce focus on task-relevant regions, and shift attention towards uninformative tokens. We further demonstrate that this attention misallocation is the principal cause of degraded accuracy and cross-framing inconsistency. Building on this mechanistic insight, we introduce a lightweight prompt-tuning method using learnable tokens that encourages the robust, visually grounded attention patterns observed in open-ended settings, improving visual grounding and improving performance across framings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：有色镜框：问题框架使视觉-语言模型失明
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）已被证明存在视觉盲区，即使在需要视觉推理的任务中，也常未能充分利用视觉输入。本研究证明VLMs具有选择性盲区：它们会根据语言框架调整对视觉输入的关注程度，即使不同框架要求相同的视觉推理。我们利用视觉注意力作为探针，量化了框架如何影响注意力的总量与分布。受限框架（如多项选择和是/否）相比开放式框架，会显著降低对图像上下文的关注，减少对任务相关区域的聚焦，并将注意力转向无信息的标记。我们进一步证明，这种注意力分配不当是准确率下降和跨框架不一致的主要原因。基于这一机制洞察，我们引入一种轻量级提示调优方法，使用可学习标记鼓励在开放式设置中观察到的稳健、视觉基础的注意力模式，从而提升视觉锚定能力并改善各框架下的性能。

Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have been shown to be blind, often underutilizing their visual inputs even on tasks that require visual reasoning.
该研究探讨了视觉语言模型（VLMs）在视觉推理任务中表现不佳的原因，发现其注意力会根据问题表述方式选择性调整。作者通过视觉注意力作为探针，表明受限格式（如多选题或是/否题）会降低对图像上下文的关注，并将注意力转向无信息的词元，而开放式问题则保持稳健的视觉锚定。他们进一步证明，这种注意力分配不当是性能下降和跨表述不一致的主要原因，并提出一种轻量级提示调优方法，通过可学习词元恢复开放式问题中的注意力模式，从而提升各类问题格式下的准确率。
OmniVTA: Visuo-Tactile World Modeling for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Yuhang Zheng, Songen Gu, Weize Li, Yupeng Zheng, Yujie Zang, Shuai Tian, Xiang Li, Ruihai Wu, Ce Hao, Chen Gao, Si Liu, Haoran Li, Yilun Chen, Shuicheng Yan, Wenchao Ding
First: 2026-03-19T17:52:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:52:42+00:00
Comments: TARS Robotics Project Page: https://mrsecant.github.io/OmniVTA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Contact-rich manipulation tasks, such as wiping and assembly, require accurate perception of contact forces, friction changes, and state transitions that cannot be reliably inferred from vision alone. Despite growing interest in visuo-tactile manipulation, progress is constrained by two persistent limitations: existing datasets are small in scale and narrow in task coverage, and current methods treat tactile signals as passive observations rather than using them to model contact dynamics or enable closed-loop control explicitly. In this paper, we present \textbf{OmniViTac}, a large-scale visuo-tactile-action dataset comprising $21{,}000+$ trajectories across $86$ tasks and $100+$ objects, organized into six physics-grounded interaction patterns. Building on this dataset, we propose \textbf{OmniVTA}, a world-model-based visuo-tactile manipulation framework that integrates four tightly coupled modules: a self-supervised tactile encoder, a two-stream visuo-tactile world model for predicting short-horizon contact evolution, a contact-aware fusion policy for action generation, and a 60Hz reflexive controller that corrects deviations between predicted and observed tactile signals in a closed loop. Real-robot experiments across all six interaction categories show that OmniVTA outperforms existing methods and generalizes well to unseen objects and geometric configurations, confirming the value of combining predictive contact modeling with high-frequency tactile feedback for contact-rich manipulation. All data, models, and code will be made publicly available on the project website at https://mrsecant.github.io/OmniVTA.
中文标题/摘要

接触密集型操作任务（如擦拭和装配）需要准确感知接触力、摩擦变化和状态转换，这些无法仅通过视觉可靠推断。尽管视觉-触觉操作受到越来越多关注，但进展受限于两个持续存在的局限：现有数据集规模小、任务覆盖窄，且当前方法将触觉信号视为被动观测，而非用于显式建模接触动力学或实现闭环控制。本文提出\textbf{OmniViTac}，一个包含21,000+条轨迹、86项任务和100+个物体的大规模视觉-触觉-动作数据集，按六种物理基础交互模式组织。基于该数据集，我们提出\textbf{OmniVTA}，一种基于世界模型的视觉-触觉操作框架，包含四个紧密耦合模块：自监督触觉编码器、双流视觉-触觉世界模型（用于预测短期接触演化）、接触感知融合策略（用于动作生成）和60Hz反射控制器（用于闭环中修正预测与观测触觉信号偏差）。在所有六类交互场景的实物机器人实验中，OmniVTA优于现有方法，且能良好泛化至未见物体和几何构型，验证了结合预测接触建模与高频触觉反馈对接触密集型操作的价值。所有数据、模型和代码将在项目网站https://mrsecant.github.io/OmniVTA公开。
