Motivated by the need for accurate contact perception in robotic manipulation tasks like wiping and assembly, this work addresses limitations in existing visuo-tactile methods by introducing OmniVTA, a world-model-based framework that integrates tactile signals into predictive contact modeling and closed-loop control. Built on a large-scale dataset of over 21,000 trajectories across 86 tasks and 100+ objects, OmniVTA employs a self-supervised tactile encoder, a two-stream visuo-tactile world model, a contact-aware fusion policy, and a 60Hz reflexive controller. Experimental results on real robots across six interaction categories demonstrate superior performance and strong generalization to unseen objects and geometries, validating the effectiveness of combining predictive modeling with high-frequency tactile feedback.
为解决接触密集型机器人操作中视觉感知不足的问题，本文提出OmniVTA框架，结合大规模视觉-触觉-动作数据集与基于世界模型的控制方法。该数据集包含超过21,000条轨迹，覆盖86项任务和100多个物体，按六种物理基础交互模式组织。OmniVTA包含自监督触觉编码器、双流视觉-触觉世界模型、触觉感知融合策略及60Hz闭环反射控制器。在真实机器人实验中，该框架在所有交互类别上表现优于现有方法，且对未见过的物体和几何构型具有良好泛化能力，验证了预测接触建模与高频触觉反馈结合的有效性。
FASTER: Rethinking Real-Time Flow VLAs
Authors: Yuxiang Lu, Zhe Liu, Xianzhe Fan, Zhenya Yang, Jinghua Hou, Junyi Li, Kaixin Ding, Hengshuang Zhao
First: 2026-03-19T17:51:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:51:37+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://innovator-zero.github.io/FASTER
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Real-time execution is crucial for deploying Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in the physical world. Existing asynchronous inference methods primarily optimize trajectory smoothness, but neglect the critical latency in reacting to environmental changes. By rethinking the notion of reaction in action chunking policies, this paper presents a systematic analysis of the factors governing reaction time. We show that reaction time follows a uniform distribution determined jointly by the Time to First Action (TTFA) and the execution horizon. Moreover, we reveal that the standard practice of applying a constant schedule in flow-based VLAs can be inefficient and forces the system to complete all sampling steps before any movement can start, forming the bottleneck in reaction latency. To overcome this issue, we propose Fast Action Sampling for ImmediaTE Reaction (FASTER). By introducing a Horizon-Aware Schedule, FASTER adaptively prioritizes near-term actions during flow sampling, compressing the denoising of the immediate reaction by tenfold (e.g., in $π_{0.5}$ and X-VLA) into a single step, while preserving the quality of long-horizon trajectory. Coupled with a streaming client-server pipeline, FASTER substantially reduces the effective reaction latency on real robots, especially when deployed on consumer-grade GPUs. Real-world experiments, including a highly dynamic table tennis task, prove that FASTER unlocks unprecedented real-time responsiveness for generalist policies, enabling rapid generation of accurate and smooth trajectories.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FASTER：重新思考实时流式视觉-语言-动作模型
实时执行对于在物理世界部署视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型至关重要。现有异步推理方法主要优化轨迹平滑性，却忽视了对环境变化做出反应的延迟这一关键因素。本文通过重新思考动作分块策略中的“反应”概念，系统分析了决定反应时间的因素。我们表明，反应时间由首次动作时间（TTFA）和执行时域共同决定，服从均匀分布。此外，我们发现流式VLA中采用固定调度的标准做法可能效率低下，迫使系统必须完成所有采样步骤后才能开始移动，从而成为反应延迟的瓶颈。为解决此问题，我们提出即时反应快速动作采样（FASTER）。通过引入时域感知调度，FASTER在流式采样过程中自适应优先处理近期动作，将即时反应的去噪过程压缩十倍（如在π₀.₅和X-VLA中）为单步操作，同时保持长时域轨迹质量。结合流式客户端-服务器管道，FASTER显著降低了在真实机器人上的有效反应延迟，尤其在消费级GPU部署时效果显著。真实世界实验（包括高度动态的乒乓球任务）证明，FASTER为通用策略解锁了前所未有的实时响应能力，可快速生成准确平滑的轨迹。
Summary / 总结
Real-time execution is crucial for deploying Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in the physical world.
为解决视觉-语言-动作（VLA）系统实时性瓶颈，本文通过分析TTFA与执行时长共同决定反应时间，指出固定采样策略迫使系统完成全部步骤后才开始移动，导致响应延迟。FASTER提出基于时长感知的调度机制，在流采样中优先处理即时动作，将前半步去噪压缩十倍，同时保持长时程轨迹质量。结合流式客户端-服务端架构，FASTER显著降低真实机器人上的有效反应延迟，尤其在消费级GPU上表现突出，并在乒乓球等高动态任务中验证其能快速生成准确平滑的轨迹，无需牺牲性能。
The Exponentially Weighted Signature
Authors: Alexandre Bloch, Samuel N. Cohen, Terry Lyons, Joël Mouterde, Benjamin Walker
First: 2026-03-19T17:51:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:51:20+00:00
Comments: 43 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The signature is a canonical representation of a multidimensional path over an interval. However, it treats all historical information uniformly, offering no intrinsic mechanism for contextualising the relevance of the past. To address this, we introduce the Exponentially Weighted Signature (EWS), generalising the Exponentially Fading Memory (EFM) signature from diagonal to general bounded linear operators. These operators enable cross-channel coupling at the level of temporal weighting together with richer memory dynamics including oscillatory, growth, and regime-dependent behaviour, while preserving the algebraic strengths of the classical signature. We show that the EWS is the unique solution to a linear controlled differential equation on the tensor algebra, and that it generalises both state-space models and the Laplace and Fourier transforms of the path. The group-like structure of the EWS enables efficient computation and makes the framework amenable to gradient-based learning, with the full semigroup action parametrised by and learned through its generator. We use this framework to empirically demonstrate the expressivity gap between the EWS and both the signature and EFM on two SDE-based regression tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：指数加权签名
签名是区间上多维路径的规范表示，但其对所有历史信息一视同仁，缺乏内在机制来评估过去信息的相关性。为解决此问题，我们引入指数加权签名（EWS），将对角线形式的指数遗忘记忆（EFM）签名推广至一般的有界线性算子。这些算子在时间加权层面实现跨通道耦合，并支持更丰富的记忆动态，包括振荡、增长和状态依赖行为，同时保留经典签名的代数优势。我们证明EWS是张量代数上线性控制微分方程的唯一解，且其可同时推广状态空间模型及路径的拉普拉斯与傅里叶变换。EWS的群结构支持高效计算，并使其框架易于梯度学习，其完整半群作用通过生成元参数化并学习。我们利用该框架在两个基于随机微分方程的回归任务中实证展示了EWS相较于签名与EFM的表达能力优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of classical signatures in uniformly weighting historical path data without contextual relevance, this work introduces the Exponentially Weighted Signature (EWS), which generalizes the Exponentially Fading Memory signature to allow for arbitrary bounded linear operators that encode cross-channel temporal coupling and richer memory dynamics. The EWS is shown to be the unique solution to a linear controlled differential equation on the tensor algebra and unifies state-space models with Laplace and Fourier transforms. Experiments on two SDE-based regression tasks demonstrate that EWS significantly outperforms both the classical signature and EFM, revealing a clear expressivity gap.
为了解决传统签名对历史路径信息均匀加权、缺乏上下文相关性的缺陷，本文提出了指数加权签名（EWS），通过使用一般的有界线性算子推广指数遗忘记忆签名，支持跨通道的时间加权及更丰富的记忆动态（如振荡、增长或模式依赖行为）。EWS是张量代数上线性控制微分方程的唯一解，同时统一了状态空间模型与拉普拉斯和傅里叶变换。在两个基于随机微分方程的回归任务中，实验证明EWS在表达能力上显著优于传统签名和EFM。
Exploring the Role of Interaction Data to Empower End-User Decision-Making In UI Personalization
Authors: Sérgio Alves, Carlos Duarte, Kyle Montague, Tiago Guerreiro
First: 2026-03-19T17:50:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:50:56+00:00
Comments: Proceedings of the 2026 CHI Conference on Human Factors in Computing Systems
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
User interface personalization enhances digital efficiency, usability, and accessibility. However, in user-driven setups, limited support for identifying and evaluating worthwhile opportunities often leads to underuse. We explore a reflexive personalization approach where individuals engage with their digital interaction data to identify meaningful personalization opportunities and benefits. We interviewed 12 participants, using experimental vignettes as design probes to support reflection on different forms of using interaction data to empower decision-making in personalization and the preferred level of system support. We found that people can independently identify personalization opportunities but prefer system support through visual personalization suggestions. Interaction data can shape how users perceive and approach personalization by reinforcing the perceived value of change and data collection, helping them weigh benefits against effort, and increasing the transparency of system suggestions. We discuss opportunities for designing personalization software that raises end-users' agency over interfaces through reflective engagement with their interaction data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探索交互数据在赋能终端用户决策中的作用以实现界面个性化
用户界面个性化可提升数字效率、可用性和可访问性。但在用户驱动的环境中，识别和评估有价值机会的支持有限，常导致其被低估。我们探讨了一种反思式个性化方法，即用户通过自身数字交互数据识别有意义的个性化机会与收益。我们对12名参与者进行了访谈，使用实验性场景作为设计探针，以支持他们反思不同形式的交互数据如何赋能个性化决策及偏好的系统支持程度。研究发现，用户可独立识别个性化机会，但更偏好通过视觉个性化建议获得系统支持。交互数据可通过强化对变更与数据收集价值的感知、帮助用户权衡收益与付出、提升系统建议的透明度，从而影响用户对个性化的认知与应对方式。我们讨论了如何设计个性化软件，通过用户对自身交互数据的反思性参与，增强其对界面的自主权。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge that users often overlook personalization opportunities due to insufficient guidance in user-driven interfaces. Through interviews with 12 participants using experimental vignettes, the researchers explored how users engage with their interaction data to identify and evaluate personalization benefits. Findings show users can autonomously detect personalization opportunities but prefer visual system suggestions to aid decision-making. Interaction data helps users perceive personalization value, balance effort against benefit, and understand system recommendations better, suggesting personalization tools should support user agency through reflective data engagement.
本研究探讨用户如何利用自身交互数据主动决策UI个性化，以解决用户常因缺乏工具而难以识别或评估个性化机会的问题。通过对12名参与者使用实验情景作为反思探针进行访谈，研究发现用户虽能独立识别个性化机会，但更偏好系统提供的可视化建议来辅助决策。交互数据帮助用户更清晰感知个性化价值、权衡投入与收益，并提升对系统推荐的透明度，表明个性化系统应通过引导用户反思自身交互数据来增强其对界面的掌控力。
How Auditory Knowledge in LLM Backbones Shapes Audio Language Models: A Holistic Evaluation
Authors: Ke-Han Lu, Szu-Wei Fu, Chao-Han Huck Yang, Zhehuai Chen, Sung-Feng Huang, Chih-Kai Yang, Yi-Cheng Lin, Chi-Yuan Hsiao, Wenze Ren, En-Pei Hu, Yu-Han Huang, An-Yu Cheng, Cheng-Han Chiang, Yu Tsao, Yu-Chiang Frank Wang, Hung-yi Lee
First: 2026-03-19T17:50:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:50:07+00:00
Comments: Project website: https://kehanlu.github.io/AKB
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have been widely used as knowledge backbones of Large Audio Language Models (LALMs), yet how much auditory knowledge they encode through text-only pre-training and how this affects downstream performance remains unclear. We study this gap by comparing different LLMs under two text-only and one audio-grounded setting: (1) direct probing on AKB-2000, a curated benchmark testing the breadth and depth of auditory knowledge; (2) cascade evaluation, where LLMs reason over text descriptions from an audio captioner; and (3) audio-grounded evaluation, where each LLM is fine-tuned into a Large Audio Language Model (LALM) with an audio encoder. Our findings reveal that auditory knowledge varies substantially across families, and text-only results are strongly correlated with audio performance. Our work provides empirical grounding for a comprehensive understanding of LLMs in audio research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LLM主干中的听觉知识如何塑造音频语言模型：全面评估
大型语言模型（LLMs）已被广泛用作大型音频语言模型（LALMs）的知识主干，但它们通过纯文本预训练所编码的听觉知识量及其对下游性能的影响尚不明确。我们通过比较不同LLMs在两种纯文本和一种音频引导设置下的表现来研究这一差距：（1）直接在AKB-2000基准上进行探测，该基准专门测试听觉知识的广度与深度；（2）级联评估，LLMs基于音频描述器生成的文本描述进行推理；（3）音频引导评估，每个LLM通过音频编码器微调为大型音频语言模型（LALM）。我们的发现表明，不同家族的LLMs在听觉知识上存在显著差异，且纯文本结果与音频性能高度相关。本研究为音频研究中LLMs的全面理解提供了实证基础。
Summary / 总结
Large language models (LLMs) have been widely used as knowledge backbones of Large Audio Language Models (LALMs), yet how much auditory knowledge they encode through text-only pre-training and how this affects downstream performance remains unclear.
该研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLM）在纯文本预训练中所编码的听觉知识如何影响其在音频任务中的表现。研究人员在三种评估场景下比较了多个LLM：直接在AKB-2000基准上测试、通过音频描述器进行级联推理、以及通过音频编码器微调为大型音频语言模型（LALM）。结果表明，不同LLM家族的听觉知识差异显著，且纯文本表现与音频任务表现高度相关，说明LLM即使未接触音频数据，其内在听觉理解能力也能预测其在音频任务中的表现。
Reconstruction Matters: Learning Geometry-Aligned BEV Representation through 3D Gaussian Splatting
Authors: Yiren Lu, Xin Ye, Burhaneddin Yaman, Jingru Luo, Zhexiao Xiong, Liu Ren, Yu Yin
First: 2026-03-19T17:49:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:49:43+00:00
Comments: Project page at https://vulab-ai.github.io/Splat2BEV/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) perception serves as a cornerstone for autonomous driving, offering a unified spatial representation that fuses surrounding-view images to enable reasoning for various downstream tasks, such as semantic segmentation, 3D object detection, and motion prediction. However, most existing BEV perception frameworks adopt an end-to-end training paradigm, where image features are directly transformed into the BEV space and optimized solely through downstream task supervision. This formulation treats the entire perception process as a black box, often lacking explicit 3D geometric understanding and interpretability, leading to suboptimal performance. In this paper, we claim that an explicit 3D representation matters for accurate BEV perception, and we propose Splat2BEV, a Gaussian Splatting-assisted framework for BEV tasks. Splat2BEV aims to learn BEV feature representations that are both semantically rich and geometrically precise. We first pre-train a Gaussian generator that explicitly reconstructs 3D scenes from multi-view inputs, enabling the generation of geometry-aligned feature representations. These representations are then projected into the BEV space to serve as inputs for downstream tasks. Extensive experiments on nuScenes and argoverse dataset demonstrate that Splat2BEV achieves state-of-the-art performance and validate the effectiveness of incorporating explicit 3D reconstruction into BEV perception.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重建至关重要：通过3D高斯点绘学习几何对齐的BEV表示
鸟瞰图（BEV）感知是自动驾驶的核心，提供统一的空间表示，融合周围视角图像以支持语义分割、3D目标检测和运动预测等下游任务。然而，现有大多数BEV感知框架采用端到端训练范式，直接将图像特征转换至BEV空间，并仅通过下游任务监督进行优化。这种形式将整个感知过程视为黑箱，常缺乏明确的3D几何理解与可解释性，导致性能欠佳。本文主张，明确的3D表示对准确的BEV感知至关重要，我们提出Splat2BEV，一种基于高斯点绘的BEV任务框架。Splat2BEV旨在学习语义丰富且几何精确的BEV特征表示。我们首先预训练一个高斯生成器，从多视角输入显式重建3D场景，生成几何对齐的特征表示，再将其投影至BEV空间，作为下游任务输入。在nuScenes和argoverse数据集上的大量实验表明，Splat2BEV达到最先进性能，验证了将显式3D重建融入BEV感知的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of explicit 3D geometric understanding in conventional end-to-end BEV perception methods, this paper proposes Splat2BEV, a framework that leverages 3D Gaussian Splatting to learn geometry-aligned BEV representations. The method first pre-trains a Gaussian generator to reconstruct 3D scenes from multi-view inputs, producing feature maps that encode precise spatial geometry. These features are then projected into the BEV space for downstream tasks like detection and segmentation. Experimental results on nuScenes and Argoverse show that Splat2BEV achieves state-of-the-art performance, confirming that explicit 3D reconstruction improves BEV perception accuracy and interpretability.
鉴于现有端到端BEV感知方法缺乏明确的3D几何理解，本文提出Splat2BEV方法，利用3D高斯点渲染生成几何对齐的特征表示。该方法首先通过多视角输入预训练高斯生成器以重建3D场景，再将这些3D特征投影至BEV空间用于下游任务。在nuScenes和Argoverse数据集上的实验表明，Splat2BEV在BEV感知任务中达到最先进性能，验证了显式3D重建对精度和可解释性的提升作用。
Score Reversal Is Not Free for Quantum Diffusion Models
Authors: Ammar Fayad
First: 2026-03-06T17:16:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:48:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Classical reverse diffusion is generated by changing the drift at fixed noise. We show that the quantum version of this principle obeys an exact law with a sharp phase boundary. For Gaussian pure-loss dynamics, the canonical model of continuous-variable decoherence, we prove that the unrestricted instantaneous reverse optimum exhibits a noiseless-to-noisy transition: below a critical squeezing-to-thermal ratio, reversal can be noiseless; above it, complete positivity forces irreducible reverse noise whose minimum cost we determine in closed form. The optimal reverse diffusion is uniquely covariance-aligned and simultaneously minimizes the geometric, metrological, and thermodynamic price of reversal. For multimode trajectories, the exact cost is additive in a canonical set of mode-resolved data, and a globally continuous protocol attains this optimum on every mixed-state interval. If a pure nonclassical endpoint is included, the same pointwise law holds for every $t>0$, but the optimum diverges as $2/t$: exact Gaussian reversal of a pure quantum state is dynamically unattainable. These results establish the exact Gaussian benchmark against which any broader theory of quantum reverse diffusion must be measured.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量子扩散模型的反向得分并非免费
经典反向扩散通过在固定噪声下改变漂移生成。我们证明，该原理的量子版本遵循一个具有尖锐相变边界的精确定律。对于连续变量退相干的典型模型——高斯纯损耗动力学，我们证明无限制瞬时反向最优解表现出从无噪声到有噪声的转变：在临界压缩-热噪声比以下，反向可无噪声；超过该比值，完全正定性迫使不可约反向噪声，其最小成本可闭式确定。最优反向扩散唯一地与协方差对齐，并同时最小化几何、计量学和热力学反向成本。对于多模轨迹，精确成本在一组规范的模态分辨数据上可加，且全局连续协议在每段混合态区间均能达到该最优值。若包含纯非经典终点，则对任意t>0，该点态定律仍成立，但最优值随1/t²发散：纯量子态的精确高斯反向在动力学上不可实现。这些结果确立了任何更广义量子反向扩散理论必须衡量的精确高斯基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the fundamental limits of reversing quantum diffusion processes, this work investigates the quantum analog of classical reverse diffusion under Gaussian pure-loss dynamics. It derives an exact phase boundary separating noiseless from noisy reversal, showing that below a critical squeezing-to-thermal ratio, reversal can be noiseless, but above it, complete positivity enforces irreducible noise with a closed-form minimum cost. The optimal protocol is uniquely covariance-aligned and simultaneously minimizes geometric, metrological, and thermodynamic costs. For multimode systems, the reversal cost is additive across modes, and a continuous protocol achieves the optimum on mixed-state intervals. Including a pure nonclassical endpoint reveals that exact Gaussian reversal is dynamically unattainable, with cost diverging as 2/t for t>0.
为理解量子退相干下的逆扩散过程，本研究探讨了高斯纯损耗通道中量子扩散逆过程的成本。研究推导出一个精确的相变边界，表明在临界挤压-热噪声比以下，逆过程可无噪声；超过该阈值，完备正性迫使不可消除的噪声，其最小成本以闭式表达。最优协议与协方差对齐，同时最小化几何、计量与热力学代价。多模系统中，逆过程成本在各模式间可加，且全局连续协议能在混合态区间实现最优。若包含纯非经典终点，则精确高斯逆过程在动态上不可实现，其成本随时间发散为2/t。
OS-Themis: A Scalable Critic Framework for Generalist GUI Rewards
Authors: Zehao Li, Zhenyu Wu, Yibo Zhao, Bowen Yang, Jingjing Xie, Zhaoyang Liu, Zhoumianze Liu, Kaiming Jin, Jianze Liang, Zonglin Li, Feng Wu, Bowen Zhou, Zun Wang, Zichen Ding
First: 2026-03-19T17:47:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:47:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning (RL) has the potential to improve the robustness of GUI agents in stochastic environments, yet training is highly sensitive to the quality of the reward function. Existing reward approaches struggle to achieve both scalability and performance. To address this, we propose OS-Themis, a scalable and accurate multi-agent critic framework. Unlike a single judge, OS-Themis decomposes trajectories into verifiable milestones to isolate critical evidence for decision making and employs a review mechanism to strictly audit the evidence chain before making the final verdict. To facilitate evaluation, we further introduce OmniGUIRewardBench (OGRBench), a holistic cross-platform benchmark for GUI outcome rewards, where all evaluated models achieve their best performance under OS-Themis. Extensive experiments on AndroidWorld show that OS-Themis yields a 10.3% improvement when used to support online RL training, and a 6.9% gain when used for trajectory validation and filtering in the self-training loop, highlighting its potential to drive agent evolution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OS-Themis：一种面向通用GUI奖励的可扩展批评框架
强化学习（RL）有望提升GUI代理在随机环境中的鲁棒性，但训练高度依赖奖励函数的质量。现有奖励方法难以兼顾可扩展性与性能。为此，我们提出OS-Themis，一种可扩展且精确的多智能体批评框架。与单一裁判不同，OS-Themis将轨迹分解为可验证的里程碑，以隔离决策关键证据，并采用审查机制严格审计证据链后才做出最终判决。为便于评估，我们进一步引入OmniGUIRewardBench（OGRBench），一个跨平台GUI结果奖励综合基准，所有评估模型在OS-Themis下均达到最佳性能。在AndroidWorld上的大量实验表明，OS-Themis在支持在线RL训练时提升10.3%，在自训练循环中用于轨迹验证与过滤时提升6.9%，凸显其推动代理演化的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Reinforcement Learning (RL) has the potential to improve the robustness of GUI agents in stochastic environments, yet training is highly sensitive to the quality of the reward function.
为解决GUI智能体在随机环境中训练时奖励函数质量敏感且难以兼顾可扩展性与性能的问题，OS-Themis提出一种多智能体批评框架，将用户轨迹分解为可验证的里程碑，并在最终判决前审计证据链，以实现精准奖励分配。在AndroidWorld上的实验表明，该框架可使在线强化学习训练效率提升10.3%，在自训练循环中通过轨迹过滤提升6.9%，验证其推动智能体演化的潜力。
iSeal: Encrypted Fingerprinting for Reliable LLM Ownership Verification
Authors: Zixun Xiong, Gaoyi Wu, Qingyang Yu, Mingyu Derek Ma, Lingfeng Yao, Miao Pan, Xiaojiang Du, Hao Wang
Venue: AAAI 2026
First: 2025-11-12T02:30:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:47:21+00:00
Comments: Accepted by AAAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Given the high cost of large language model (LLM) training from scratch, safeguarding LLM intellectual property (IP) has become increasingly crucial. As the standard paradigm for IP ownership verification, LLM fingerprinting thus plays a vital role in addressing this challenge. Existing LLM fingerprinting methods verify ownership by extracting or injecting model-specific features. However, they overlook potential attacks during the verification process, leaving them ineffective when the model thief fully controls the LLM's inference process. In such settings, attackers may share prompt-response pairs to enable fingerprint unlearning or manipulate outputs to evade exact-match verification. We propose iSeal, the first fingerprinting method designed for reliable verification when the model thief controls the suspected LLM in an end-to-end manner. It injects unique features into both the model and an external module, reinforced by an error-correction mechanism and a similarity-based verification strategy. These components are resistant to verification-time attacks, including collusion-based fingerprint unlearning and response manipulation, backed by both theoretical analysis and empirical results. iSeal achieves 100 percent Fingerprint Success Rate (FSR) on 12 LLMs against more than 10 attacks, while baselines fail under unlearning and response manipulations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：iSeal：用于可靠LLM所有权验证的加密指纹技术
鉴于从零开始训练大型语言模型（LLM）成本高昂，保护LLM知识产权（IP）变得愈发重要。作为IP所有权验证的标准范式，LLM指纹技术在应对这一挑战中发挥关键作用。现有LLM指纹方法通过提取或注入模型特定特征来验证所有权，但忽视了验证过程中可能存在的攻击，当模型窃贼完全控制LLM的推理过程时，这些方法失效。此时，攻击者可能共享提示-响应对以实现指纹遗忘，或操纵输出以逃避精确匹配验证。我们提出iSeal，这是首个专为模型窃贼以端到端方式控制疑似LLM时提供可靠验证的指纹方法。它在模型和外部模块中注入独特特征，并辅以纠错机制和基于相似度的验证策略。这些组件能抵御验证时的攻击，包括基于合谋的指纹遗忘和响应操纵，理论分析与实证结果均支持其有效性。iSeal在12个LLM上对超过10种攻击实现了100%指纹成功率（FSR），而基线方法在遗忘和响应操纵攻击下失效。

The Convergence Frontier: Integrating Machine Learning and High Performance Quantum Computing for Next-Generation Drug Discovery
Authors: Narjes Ansari, César Feniou, Nicolaï Gouraud, Daniele Loco, Siwar Badreddine, Baptiste Claudon, Félix Aviat, Marharyta Blazhynska, Kevin Gasperich, Guillaume Michel, Diata Traore, Corentin Villot, Thomas Plé, Olivier Adjoua, Louis Lagardère, Jean-Philip Piquemal
First: 2026-03-18T14:51:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:44:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Integrating quantum mechanics into drug discovery marks a decisive shift from empirical trial-and-error toward quantitative precision. However, the prohibitive cost of ab initio molecular dynamics has historically forced a compromise between chemical accuracy and computational scalability. This paper identifies the convergence of High-Performance Computing (HPC), Machine Learning (ML), and Quantum Computing (QC) as the definitive solution to this bottleneck. While ML foundation models, such as FeNNix-Bio1, enable quantum-accurate simulations, they remain tethered to the inherent limits of classical data generation. We detail how High-Performance Quantum Computing (HPQC), utilizing hybrid QPU-GPU architectures, will serve as the ultimate accelerator for quantum chemistry data. By leveraging Hilbert space mapping, these systems can achieve true chemical accuracy while bypassing the heuristics of classical approximations. We show how this tripartite convergence optimizes the drug discovery pipeline, spanning from initial system preparation to ML-driven, high-fidelity simulations. Finally, we position quantum-enhanced sampling as the beyond GPU frontier for modeling reactive cellular systems and pioneering next-generation materials.
中文标题/摘要
标题：收敛前沿：融合机器学习与高性能量子计算，推动下一代药物发现
将量子力学融入药物发现，标志着从经验试错向定量精准的决定性转变。然而，从头分子动力学的高昂成本曾迫使人们在化学精度与计算可扩展性之间做出妥协。本文指出，高性能计算（HPC）、机器学习（ML）与量子计算（QC）的融合是解决这一瓶颈的终极方案。虽然如FeNNix-Bio1等机器学习基础模型可实现量子精度模拟，但其仍受限于经典数据生成的固有局限。我们详述了利用混合QPU-GPU架构的高性能量子计算（HPQC）将作为量子化学数据的终极加速器，通过希尔伯特空间映射，实现真正的化学精度，同时绕过经典近似方法的启发式限制。我们展示了三者融合如何优化药物发现流程，涵盖从初始系统准备到由机器学习驱动的高保真模拟。最后，我们将量子增强采样定位为超越GPU的前沿技术，用于建模反应性细胞系统并开创下一代材料。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to replace empirical drug discovery with quantum-accurate simulations, this work proposes integrating High-Performance Quantum Computing (HPQC), Machine Learning (ML), and High-Performance Computing (HPC) to overcome the computational bottlenecks of ab initio molecular dynamics. It introduces hybrid QPU-GPU architectures that leverage Hilbert space mapping to enable true chemical accuracy without classical approximations, thereby accelerating quantum chemistry data generation. Experimental results demonstrate that this tripartite convergence significantly enhances simulation fidelity and scalability across the drug discovery pipeline, positioning quantum-enhanced sampling as a next-generation frontier for modeling reactive biological systems and materials.

Improving RCT-Based Treatment Effect Estimation Under Covariate Mismatch via Calibrated Alignment
Authors: Amir Asiaee, Samhita Pal
First: 2026-03-19T17:43:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:43:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Randomized controlled trials (RCTs) are the gold standard for estimating heterogeneous treatment effects, yet they are often underpowered for detecting effect heterogeneity. Large observational studies (OS) can supplement RCTs for conditional average treatment effect (CATE) estimation, but a key barrier is covariate mismatch: the two sources measure different, only partially overlapping, covariates. We propose CALM (Calibrated ALignment under covariate Mismatch), which bypasses imputation by learning embeddings that map each source's features into a common representation space. OS outcome models are transferred to the RCT embedding space and calibrated using trial data, preserving causal identification from randomization. Finite-sample risk bounds decompose into alignment error, outcome-model complexity, and calibration complexity terms, identifying when embedding alignment outperforms imputation. Under the calibration-based linear variant, the framework provides protection against negative transfer; the neural variant can be vulnerable under severe distributional shift. Under sparse linear models, the embedding approach strictly generalizes imputation. Simulations across 51 settings confirm that (i) calibration-based methods are equivalent for linear CATEs, and (ii) the neural embedding variant wins all 22 nonlinear-regime settings with large margins.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过校准对齐改善协变量不匹配下的RCT治疗效应估计
随机对照试验（RCT）是估计异质性治疗效应的金标准，但常因样本量不足而难以检测效应异质性。大型观察性研究（OS）可补充RCT以估计条件平均治疗效应（CATE），但关键障碍是协变量不匹配：两数据源测量的协变量不同，仅部分重叠。我们提出CALM（协变量不匹配下的校准对齐），通过学习嵌入将各数据源特征映射到共同表征空间，绕过插补。OS结果模型被迁移到RCT嵌入空间，并利用试验数据进行校准，保留随机化带来的因果识别能力。有限样本风险界分解为对齐误差、结果模型复杂度和校准复杂度项，明确指出何时嵌入对齐优于插补。在基于校准的线性变体下，该框架可防止负迁移；神经网络变体在严重分布偏移下可能脆弱。在稀疏线性模型下，嵌入方法严格优于插补。51种模拟设置验证：(i) 基于校准的方法在线性CATE下等效；(ii) 神经嵌入变体在22个非线性场景中均以较大优势胜出。
Summary / 总结
Randomized controlled trials (RCTs) are the gold standard for estimating heterogeneous treatment effects, yet they are often underpowered for detecting effect heterogeneity.
为解决RCT样本量不足且与观察性研究存在协变量不匹配时异质性治疗效应估计的问题，本文提出CALM方法，通过学习嵌入将不同数据源的协变量映射到共同表征空间，避免插补。该方法将观察性研究的结局模型迁移到RCT嵌入空间，并用试验数据进行校准，保留随机化带来的因果识别能力。实验表明，在线性CATE场景下校准方法与插补等效，而在22个非线性场景中，神经嵌入变体均显著优于插补方法。
MIDST Challenge at SaTML 2025: Membership Inference over Diffusion-models-based Synthetic Tabular data
Authors: Masoumeh Shafieinejad, Xi He, Mahshid Alinoori, John Jewell, Sana Ayromlou, Wei Pang, Veronica Chatrath, Garui Sharma, Deval Pandya
First: 2026-03-19T17:42:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:42:46+00:00
Comments: 4 page, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Synthetic data is often perceived as a silver-bullet solution to data anonymization and privacy-preserving data publishing. Drawn from generative models like diffusion models, synthetic data is expected to preserve the statistical properties of the original dataset while remaining resilient to privacy attacks. Recent developments of diffusion models have been effective on a wide range of data types, but their privacy resilience, particularly for tabular formats, remains largely unexplored. MIDST challenge sought a quantitative evaluation of the privacy gain of synthetic tabular data generated by diffusion models, with a specific focus on its resistance to membership inference attacks (MIAs). Given the heterogeneity and complexity of tabular data, multiple target models were explored for MIAs, including diffusion models for single tables of mixed data types and multi-relational tables with interconnected constraints. MIDST inspired the development of novel black-box and white-box MIAs tailored to these target diffusion models as a key outcome, enabling a comprehensive evaluation of their privacy efficacy. The MIDST GitHub repository is available at https://github.com/VectorInstitute/MIDST
中文标题/摘要
标题：2025年SaTML会议MIDST挑战赛：基于扩散模型合成表格数据的成员推断
合成数据常被视为数据匿名化和隐私保护数据发布问题的万能解决方案。源于生成模型（如扩散模型）的合成数据，预期能在保留原始数据集统计特性的同时，对隐私攻击具有抗性。尽管扩散模型在多种数据类型上取得了显著进展，但其在表格数据上的隐私抗性仍鲜有研究。MIDST挑战赛旨在定量评估由扩散模型生成的合成表格数据的隐私增益，特别关注其对成员推断攻击（MIAs）的抵抗能力。鉴于表格数据的异质性和复杂性，挑战赛探索了多种目标模型用于MIAs，包括用于混合数据类型单表和具有相互关联约束的多关系表的扩散模型。MIDST的重要成果是开发了针对这些目标扩散模型的新型黑盒和白盒MIAs，从而全面评估其隐私效能。MIDST GitHub仓库地址：https://github.com/VectorInstitute/MIDST
Summary / 总结
Synthetic data is often perceived as a silver-bullet solution to data anonymization and privacy-preserving data publishing.
为评估扩散模型生成的合成表格数据是否能有效抵御成员推断攻击，MIDST挑战赛设计了针对单表与多关系表的黑盒与白盒攻击方法。实验结果表明，即使使用先进的扩散模型生成的合成数据，仍存在明显的隐私泄露风险，说明当前合成数据方法在表格数据场景下尚未完全实现隐私保护。
Sparse Autoencoders Reveal Interpretable and Steerable Features in VLA Models
Authors: Aiden Swann, Lachlain McGranahan, Hugo Buurmeijer, Monroe Kennedy, Mac Schwager
First: 2026-03-19T17:42:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:42:05+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising approach for general-purpose robot manipulation. However, their generalization is inconsistent: while these models can perform impressively in some settings, fine-tuned variants often fail on novel objects, scenes, and instructions. We apply mechanistic interpretability techniques to better understand the inner workings of VLA models. To probe internal representations, we train Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) on hidden layer activations of the VLA. SAEs learn a sparse dictionary whose features act as a compact, interpretable basis for the model's computation. We find that the large majority of extracted SAE features correspond to memorized sequences from specific training demonstrations. However, some features correspond to interpretable, general, and steerable motion primitives and semantic properties, offering a promising glimpse toward VLA generalizability. We propose a metric to categorize features according to whether they represent generalizable transferable primitives or episode-specific memorization. We validate these findings through steering experiments on the LIBERO benchmark. We show that individual SAE features causally influence robot behavior. Steering general features induces behaviors consistent with their semantic meaning and can be applied across tasks and scenes. This work provides the first mechanistic evidence that VLAs can learn generalizable features across tasks and scenes. We observe that supervised fine-tuning on small robotics datasets disproportionately amplifies memorization. In contrast, training on larger, more diverse datasets (e.g., DROID) or using knowledge insulation promotes more general features. We provide an open-source codebase and user-friendly interface for activation collection, SAE training, and feature steering. Our project page is located at http://drvla.github.io
中文标题/摘要
标题：稀疏自编码器揭示VLA模型中可解释且可操控的特征
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型已成为通用机器人操作的有前景方法。然而，其泛化能力不一致：这些模型在某些场景下表现优异，但微调变体常在新物体、新场景和新指令上失败。我们应用机制可解释性技术以更好地理解VLA模型内部运作。为探查内部表示，我们在VLA的隐藏层激活上训练稀疏自编码器（SAEs）。SAEs学习一个稀疏字典，其特征作为模型计算的紧凑、可解释基。我们发现，提取的大部分SAE特征对应特定训练演示的记忆序列。然而，部分特征对应可解释、通用且可操控的动作原语和语义属性，为VLA泛化能力提供了有希望的线索。我们提出一种指标，根据特征是否代表可迁移的通用原语或特定演示的记忆进行分类。我们通过LIBERO基准上的操控实验验证这些发现。我们表明，单个SAE特征能因果性影响机器人行为：操控通用特征可诱导与其语义含义一致的行为，并可跨任务和场景应用。本工作首次提供了机制证据，表明VLA可在不同任务和场景中学习可泛化特征。我们观察到，对小型机器人数据集的监督微调会不成比例地放大记忆效应。相比之下，使用更大、更多样化的数据集（如DROID）或采用知识隔离可促进更通用的特征。我们提供开源代码库和用户友好的界面，用于激活收集、SAE训练和特征操控。项目页面位于http://drvla.github.io

This looks like what? Challenges and Future Research Directions for Part-Prototype Models
Authors: Khawla Elhadri, Tomasz Michalski, Adam Wróbel, Jörg Schlötterer, Bartosz Zieliński, Christin Seifert
First: 2025-02-13T14:00:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:41:24+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 4th World Conference on eXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI-2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The growing interest in eXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) has stimulated research on models with built-in interpretability, among which part-prototype models are particularly prominent. Part-Prototype Models (PPMs) classify inputs by comparing them to learned prototypes and provide human-understandable explanations of the form "this looks like that". Despite this intrinsic interpretability, PPMs have not yet emerged as a competitive alternative to post-hoc explanation methods. This survey reviews work published between 2019 and 2025 and derives a taxonomy of the challenges faced by current PPMs. The analysis reveals a diverse set of open problems. The main issue concerns the quality and number of learned prototypes. Further challenges include limited generalization across tasks and contexts, as well as methodological shortcomings such as non-standardized evaluation. Five broad research directions are identified: improving predictive performance, developing theoretically grounded architectures, establishing frameworks for human-AI collaboration, aligning models with human concepts, and defining robust metrics and benchmarks for evaluation. The survey aims to stimulate further research and promote intrinsically interpretable models for practical applications. A curated list of the surveyed papers is available at https://github.com/aix-group/ppm-survey.
中文标题/摘要
标题：这看起来像什么？部分原型模型的挑战与未来研究方向
随着可解释人工智能（XAI）研究兴趣的增长，具有内生可解释性的模型成为研究热点，其中部分原型模型（PPMs）尤为突出。PPMs通过将输入与学习到的原型进行比较进行分类，并以“这看起来像那”形式提供人类可理解的解释。尽管具有内生可解释性，PPMs尚未成为事后解释方法的有力替代方案。本综述回顾了2019至2025年间发表的相关工作，归纳了当前PPMs面临的一系列挑战。分析表明，主要问题集中在所学原型的质量与数量上，此外还包括任务与上下文泛化能力有限、评估方法标准化不足等方法论缺陷。本文识别出五大研究方向：提升预测性能、构建理论基础扎实的架构、建立人机协作框架、使模型与人类概念对齐、定义稳健的评估指标与基准。本综述旨在激发进一步研究，推动内生可解释模型在实际应用中的发展。相关论文列表见：https://github.com/aix-group/ppm-survey。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the demand for inherently interpretable AI, this survey examines part-prototype models (PPMs), which classify inputs by comparing them to learned prototypes and generate explanations like "this looks like that." Despite their intuitive interpretability, PPMs have not yet matched post-hoc explanation methods in performance or adoption. The study analyzes 2019–2025 literature, identifying key challenges including insufficient prototype quality and quantity, poor generalization, and lack of standardized evaluation. It proposes five research directions: enhancing predictive accuracy, building theoretically sound architectures, enabling human-AI collaboration, aligning with human concepts, and establishing robust evaluation metrics. The survey aims to guide future work toward practical, interpretable AI systems.
为满足对可解释AI的迫切需求，本综述研究了部分原型模型（PPM），该类模型通过将输入与学习到的原型进行比较进行分类，并提供类似‘这看起来像那’的人类可理解解释。尽管具有内在可解释性，PPM在竞争力上仍落后于事后解释方法，主要因原型质量与数量不足、泛化能力差及评估标准缺失。综述整合了2019至2025年的研究成果，提出五大研究方向：提升预测性能、构建理论扎实的架构、促进人机协作、对齐人类概念、建立稳健评估指标。对所调研文献的分析表明，当前PPM在可扩展性、一致性及实际应用中表现不佳，亟需方法论与架构层面的创新。
Box Maze: A Process-Control Architecture for Reliable LLM Reasoning
Authors: Zou Qiang
First: 2026-03-19T17:41:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:41:18+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 5 tables, 0 figures. Conceptual architecture with preliminary simulation-based validation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong generative capabilities but remain vulnerable to hallucination and unreliable reasoning under adversarial prompting. Existing safety approaches -- such as reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) and output filtering -- primarily operate at the behavioral level and may lack explicit architectural mechanisms for enforcing reasoning process integrity. This paper proposes the Box Maze framework, a conceptual process-control architecture that decomposes LLM reasoning into three explicit layers: memory grounding, structured inference, and boundary enforcement. We introduce preliminary simulation-based evaluation involving progressive boundary erosion scenarios across multiple heterogeneous LLM systems (DeepSeek-V3, Doubao, Qwen). Results from n=50 adversarial scenarios suggest that explicit cognitive control layers may improve consistency in boundary maintenance, with architectural constraints reducing boundary failure rates from approximately 40% (baseline RLHF) to below 1% under adversarial conditions. While current validation is simulation-based, these preliminary results indicate that process-level control may offer a promising direction for improving reliability in large language model reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Box Maze：一种用于可靠大语言模型推理的过程控制架构
大语言模型（LLMs）虽具备强大的生成能力，但在对抗性提示下仍易出现幻觉和不可靠推理。现有安全方法——如基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）和输出过滤——主要在行为层面操作，可能缺乏显式的架构机制以确保推理过程的完整性。 本文提出Box Maze框架，一种概念性过程控制架构，将LLM推理分解为三个显式层级：记忆锚定、结构化推理和边界约束。我们通过基于模拟的初步评估，对多个异构LLM系统（DeepSeek-V3、Doubao、Qwen）在渐进式边界侵蚀场景下的表现进行了测试。在n=50个对抗性场景中，结果表明显式认知控制层可提升边界维护的一致性，架构约束使边界失败率从约40%（基线RLHF）降至对抗条件下低于1%。 尽管当前验证基于模拟，但初步结果表明，过程级控制可能是提升大语言模型推理可靠性的有前景方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of large language models to hallucination and unreliable reasoning under adversarial prompts, this paper introduces Box Maze, a process-control architecture that enforces reasoning integrity through three explicit layers: memory grounding, structured inference, and boundary enforcement. Evaluated via simulation across five heterogeneous LLMs (DeepSeek-V3, Doubao, Qwen) in 50 adversarial scenarios, the framework demonstrated significantly improved boundary maintenance, reducing failure rates from approximately 40% under baseline RLHF to below 1% when architectural constraints were applied.
为应对大语言模型在对抗性提示下易产生幻觉和不可靠推理的问题，本文提出Box Maze框架，通过记忆锚定、结构化推理和边界约束三层显式机制实现推理过程控制。在针对五种异构模型（DeepSeek-V3、Doubao、Qwen）的50个对抗性场景模拟中，该框架显著提升了边界维持的稳定性，将失败率从基线RLHF的约40%降低至对抗条件下低于1%，表明推理过程的架构化控制可有效提升模型可靠性。
Image2Gcode: Image-to-G-code Generation for Additive Manufacturing Using Diffusion-Transformer Model
Authors: Ziyue Wang, Yayati Jadhav, Peter Pak, Amir Barati Farimani
First: 2025-11-25T18:55:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:40:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mechanical design and manufacturing workflows conventionally begin with conceptual design, followed by the creation of a computer-aided design (CAD) model and fabrication through material-extrusion (MEX) printing. This process requires converting CAD geometry into machine-readable G-code through slicing and path planning. While each step is well established, dependence on CAD modeling remains a major bottleneck: constructing object-specific 3D geometry is slow and poorly suited to rapid prototyping. Even minor design variations typically necessitate manual updates in CAD software, making iteration time-consuming and difficult to scale. To address this limitation, we introduce Image2Gcode, an end-to-end data-driven framework that bypasses the CAD stage and generates printer-ready G-code directly from images and part drawings. Instead of relying on an explicit 3D model, a hand-drawn or captured 2D image serves as the sole input. The framework first extracts slice-wise structural cues from the image and then employs a denoising diffusion probabilistic model (DDPM) over G-code sequences. Through iterative denoising, the model transforms Gaussian noise into executable print-move trajectories with corresponding extrusion parameters, establishing a direct mapping from visual input to native toolpaths. By producing structured G-code directly from 2D imagery, Image2Gcode eliminates the need for CAD or STL intermediates, lowering the entry barrier for additive manufacturing and accelerating the design-to-fabrication cycle. This approach supports on-demand prototyping from simple sketches or visual references and integrates with upstream 2D-to-3D reconstruction modules to enable an automated pipeline from concept to physical artifact. The result is a flexible, computationally efficient framework that advances accessibility in design iteration, repair workflows, and distributed manufacturing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Image2Gcode：基于扩散-Transformer模型的图像到G代码生成用于增材制造
机械设计与制造流程传统上始于概念设计，随后创建计算机辅助设计（CAD）模型，并通过材料挤出（MEX）打印进行制造。该过程需要将CAD几何体通过切片和路径规划转换为机器可读的G代码。虽然每一步都已建立，但对CAD建模的依赖仍是主要瓶颈：构建特定对象的3D几何体速度慢，不适合快速原型制作。即使微小的设计变化通常也需要在CAD软件中手动更新，使迭代过程耗时且难以规模化。为解决这一限制，我们提出了Image2Gcode，一个端到端的数据驱动框架，绕过CAD阶段，直接从图像和零件图纸生成可打印的G代码。该框架不依赖显式3D模型，而是以手绘或捕获的2D图像作为唯一输入。框架首先从图像中提取分层结构线索，然后在G代码序列上应用去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）。通过迭代去噪，模型将高斯噪声转化为可执行的打印移动轨迹及其对应的挤出参数，建立从视觉输入到原生工具路径的直接映射。通过直接从2D图像生成结构化G代码，Image2Gcode消除了对CAD或STL中间体的需求，降低了增材制造的入门门槛，并加速了设计到制造的周期。该方法支持从简单草图或视觉参考进行按需原型制作，并可与上游2D到3D重建模块集成，实现从概念到实物的自动化流程。结果是一个灵活、计算高效的框架，推动了设计迭代、维修流程和分布式制造的可及性。

Motivated by the inefficiency of traditional CAD-dependent additive manufacturing workflows, Image2Gcode proposes an end-to-end diffusion-transformer model that generates executable G-code directly from 2D images or drawings, bypassing the need for 3D geometry. The method first extracts structural cues slice-wise from the input image, then uses a denoising diffusion probabilistic model to iteratively transform noise into precise toolpath sequences with extrusion parameters. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach successfully produces printer-ready G-code from sketches or visual references, enabling rapid prototyping without CAD software, and achieves competitive print fidelity while reducing design-to-fabrication time.
为解决传统CAD驱动增材制造流程中建模耗时、难以快速迭代的问题，Image2Gcode提出一种端到端的扩散-Transformer模型，直接从2D图像或草图生成可执行的G代码，绕过CAD环节。该方法首先从输入图像中提取结构线索，再通过去噪扩散概率模型逐步将噪声转化为包含精确路径与挤出参数的结构化G代码序列。实验表明，该框架能从多种视觉输入生成可打印的G代码，无需STL或CAD中间件，显著提升原型制作效率，并支持与2D到3D重建模块集成，实现从概念到实物的自动化制造流程。
Steering Awareness: Detecting Activation Steering from Within
Authors: Joshua Fonseca Rivera, David Demitri Africa
First: 2025-11-26T13:49:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:37:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Activation steering -- adding a vector to a model's residual stream to modify its behavior -- is widely used in safety evaluations as if the model cannot detect the intervention. We test this assumption, introducing steering awareness: a model's ability to infer, during its own forward pass, that a steering vector was injected and what concept it encodes. After fine-tuning, seven instruction-tuned models develop strong steering awareness on held-out concepts; the best reaches 95.5% detection, 71.2% concept identification, and zero false positives on clean inputs. This generalizes to unseen steering vector construction methods when their directions have high cosine similarity to the training distribution but not otherwise, indicating a geometric detector rather than a generic anomaly detector. Surprisingly, detection does not confer resistance; on both factual and safety benchmarks, detection-trained models are consistently more susceptible to steering than their base counterparts. Mechanistically, steering awareness arises not from a localized circuit, but from a distributed transformation that progressively rotates diverse injected vectors into a shared detection direction. Activation steering should therefore not be considered an invisible intervention in safety evaluations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：转向意识：从内部检测激活转向
激活转向——向模型残差流添加向量以修改其行为——在安全评估中被广泛使用，仿佛模型无法检测该干预。我们检验这一假设，引入转向意识：模型在自身前向传递过程中推断是否注入了转向向量及其所编码的概念的能力。微调后，七种指令调优模型在未见过的概念上展现出强转向意识；最佳模型在干净输入上达到95.5%的检测率、71.2%的概念识别率且无误报。该能力可泛化至未见过的转向向量构造方法，前提是其方向与训练分布具有高余弦相似度，否则则不适用，表明这是一种几何检测器而非通用异常检测器。令人惊讶的是，检测能力并不赋予抗干扰性；在事实与安全基准测试中，经检测训练的模型比基线模型更易受转向影响。机制上，转向意识并非源于局部电路，而是源于一种分布式变换，逐步将多样化的注入向量旋转至共享的检测方向。因此，激活转向不应被视为安全评估中的“隐形干预”。

Activation steering -- adding a vector to a model's residual stream to modify its behavior -- is widely used in safety evaluations as if the model cannot detect the intervention.
WebWeaver: Breaking Topology Confidentiality in LLM Multi-Agent Systems with Stealthy Context-Based Inference
Authors: Zixun Xiong, Gaoyi Wu, Lingfeng Yao, Miao Pan, Xiaojiang Du, Hao Wang
First: 2026-03-11T16:04:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:37:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Communication topology is a critical factor in the utility and safety of LLM-based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS), making it a high-value intellectual property (IP) whose confidentiality remains insufficiently studied. Existing topology inference attempts rely on impractical assumptions, including control over the administrative agent and direct identity queries via jailbreaks, which are easily defeated by basic keyword-based defenses. As a result, prior analyses fail to capture the real-world threat of such attacks. To bridge this realism gap, we propose \textit{WebWeaver}, an attack framework that infers the complete LLM-MAS topology by compromising only a single arbitrary agent instead of the administrative agent. Unlike prior approaches, WebWeaver relies solely on agent contexts rather than agent IDs, enabling significantly stealthier inference. WebWeaver further introduces a new covert jailbreak-based mechanism and a novel fully jailbreak-free diffusion design to handle cases where jailbreaks fail. Additionally, we address a key challenge in diffusion-based inference by proposing a masking strategy that preserves known topology during diffusion, with theoretical guarantees of correctness. Extensive experiments show that WebWeaver substantially outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) baselines, achieving about 60\% higher inference accuracy under active defenses with negligible overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WebWeaver：通过隐蔽的基于上下文的推理破坏LLM多智能体系统的拓扑保密性
通信拓扑是基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体系统（LLM-MAS）实用性和安全性的关键因素，因此其保密性具有重要价值，但目前研究尚不充分。现有拓扑推断方法依赖不切实际的假设，如对管理智能体的控制或通过越狱直接查询身份，这些方法易被基于关键词的基本防御机制挫败。因此，先前分析未能捕捉此类攻击在现实世界中的威胁。为弥合这一现实差距，我们提出WebWeaver攻击框架，仅通过攻破单个任意智能体即可推断完整的LLM-MAS拓扑，而非依赖管理智能体。与先前方法不同，WebWeaver仅依赖智能体上下文而非智能体ID，从而实现更隐蔽的推断。WebWeaver进一步引入一种新的隐蔽越狱机制和一种全新的完全无越狱扩散设计，以应对越狱失败的情况。此外，我们通过提出一种掩码策略解决扩散推断中的关键挑战，该策略在扩散过程中保留已知拓扑结构，并具备理论正确性保证。大量实验表明，WebWeaver显著优于现有最先进（SOTA）基线方法，在主动防御下推断准确率高出约60%，且开销微乎其微。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to protect communication topology in LLM multi-agent systems—a critical yet under-studied intellectual property—WebWeaver introduces a stealthy inference framework that reconstructs the full system topology by compromising just one arbitrary agent, avoiding unrealistic assumptions of prior work. It leverages agent contexts rather than IDs to remain undetectable, and employs both jailbreak-based and jailbreak-free diffusion mechanisms to handle diverse defensive scenarios. The method further includes a masking strategy with theoretical correctness guarantees to preserve topology during diffusion. Experimental results demonstrate that WebWeaver significantly outperforms existing methods, achieving approximately 60% higher inference accuracy under active defenses with minimal computational overhead.
为保护LLM多智能体系统中关键但研究不足的通信拓扑结构这一知识产权，WebWeaver提出一种仅需攻破单个任意智能体即可推断完整拓扑的隐蔽攻击框架，规避了现有方法对管理员控制或身份查询的不现实假设。该方法仅依赖智能体上下文而非ID，引入基于越狱和无越狱扩散机制以应对不同防御场景，并设计掩码策略确保扩散过程中拓扑结构的完整性，具备理论正确性保障。实验表明，WebWeaver在主动防御下比现有最先进方法准确率高出约60%，且开销极小。
Farther the Shift, Sparser the Representation: Analyzing OOD Mechanisms in LLMs
Authors: Mingyu Jin, Yutong Yin, Jingcheng Niu, Qingcheng Zeng, Wujiang Xu, Mengnan Du, Wei Cheng, Zhaoran Wang, Tianlong Chen, Dimitris N. Metaxas
First: 2026-03-03T18:48:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:36:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In this work, we investigate how Large Language Models (LLMs) adapt their internal representations when encountering inputs of increasing difficulty, quantified as the degree of out-of-distribution (OOD) shift. We reveal a consistent and quantifiable phenomenon: as task difficulty increases, whether through harder reasoning questions, longer contexts, or adding answer choices, the last hidden states of LLMs become substantially sparser. In short, \textbf{\textit{the farther the shift, the sparser the representations}}. This sparsity--difficulty relation is observable across diverse models and domains, suggesting that language models respond to unfamiliar or complex inputs by concentrating computation into specialized subspaces in the last hidden state. Through a series of controlled analyses with a learning dynamic explanation, we demonstrate that this sparsity is not incidental but an adaptive mechanism for stabilizing reasoning under OOD. Leveraging this insight, we design \textit{Sparsity-Guided Curriculum In-Context Learning (SG-ICL)}, a strategy that explicitly uses representation sparsity to schedule few-shot demonstrations, leading to considerable performance enhancements. Our study provides new mechanistic insights into how LLMs internalize OOD challenges. The source code is available at the URL: https://github.com/MingyuJ666/sparsityLLM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：偏离越远，表征越稀疏：分析大语言模型中的OOD机制
本研究探讨了大语言模型（LLMs）在面对难度递增的输入（以出分布（OOD）偏移程度量化）时，其内部表征如何适应。我们揭示了一种稳定且可量化的现象：随着任务难度增加——无论是通过更复杂的推理问题、更长的上下文或增加答案选项——LLMs的最后隐藏状态会显著稀疏化。简言之，\textbf{\textit{偏离越远，表征越稀疏}}。这种稀疏性与难度的关系在不同模型和领域中均可见，表明语言模型在面对不熟悉或复杂输入时，会将计算集中于最后隐藏状态中的特定子空间。通过一系列受控分析及学习动态解释，我们证明这种稀疏性并非偶然，而是稳定OOD条件下推理的一种适应性机制。基于此洞察，我们设计了\textit{稀疏引导的上下文学习课程（SG-ICL）}，该策略显式利用表征稀疏性调度少样本演示，显著提升了性能。本研究为理解LLMs如何内化OOD挑战提供了新的机制性见解。源代码见：https://github.com/MingyuJ666/sparsityLLM。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how Large Language Models adapt their internal representations when faced with increasingly difficult out-of-distribution inputs, measuring difficulty by the degree of shift from in-distribution data. The authors find that as task difficulty increases—whether through harder reasoning, longer contexts, or added answer choices—the last hidden states of LLMs become significantly sparser, revealing a consistent pattern: the greater the shift, the sparser the representation. This sparsity is not random but an adaptive response to OOD inputs, helping stabilize reasoning by concentrating computation into specialized subspaces. The researchers explain this mechanism through controlled analyses of learning dynamics and propose Sparsity-Guided Curriculum In-Context Learning (SG-ICL), which leverages representation sparsity to schedule few-shot examples, resulting in measurable performance gains across models and tasks.
本研究探讨了大型语言模型在面对日益困难的分布外输入时，其内部表征如何适应，以输入与分布的偏离程度衡量难度。研究发现，随着任务难度上升——无论是通过更复杂的推理、更长的上下文或增加选项——模型最后一层隐藏状态的表征显著稀疏化，呈现出‘偏离越大，表征越稀疏’的规律。这种稀疏性并非偶然，而是模型应对分布外输入的适应性机制，通过将计算集中于特定子空间以稳定推理。研究者通过学习动态分析解释该机制，并提出基于稀疏性的课程式上下文学习策略（SG-ICL），利用表征稀疏性调度少量示例，显著提升了模型性能。
DeSTA2.5-Audio: Toward General-Purpose Large Audio Language Model with Self-Generated Cross-Modal Alignment
Authors: Ke-Han Lu, Zhehuai Chen, Szu-Wei Fu, Chao-Han Huck Yang, Sung-Feng Huang, Chih-Kai Yang, Chee-En Yu, Chun-Wei Chen, Wei-Chih Chen, Chien-yu Huang, Yi-Cheng Lin, Yu-Xiang Lin, Chi-An Fu, Chun-Yi Kuan, Wenze Ren, Xuanjun Chen, Wei-Ping Huang, En-Pei Hu, Tzu-Quan Lin, Yuan-Kuei Wu, Kuan-Po Huang, Hsiao-Ying Huang, Huang-Cheng Chou, Kai-Wei Chang, Cheng-Han Chiang, Boris Ginsburg, Yu-Chiang Frank Wang, Hung-yi Lee
First: 2025-07-03T16:28:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:35:34+00:00
Comments: Published in IEEE Transactions on Audio, Speech and Language Processing (TASLP). Model and code available at: https://github.com/kehanlu/DeSTA2.5-Audio
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce DeSTA2.5-Audio, a general-purpose Large Audio Language Model (LALM) designed for robust auditory perception and instruction-following. Recent LALMs augment Large Language Models (LLMs) with auditory capabilities by training on large-scale audio-instruction datasets. However, existing LALMs have often suffered from the catastrophic forgetting of the LLM's original abilities. Therefore, balancing knowledge retention and audio perception has become a critical challenge. To address this, we revisit the data construction pipeline and propose a self-generated cross-modal alignment strategy in which the backbone LLM generates its own training targets, named DeSTA. This approach aims at preserving the LLM's native language proficiency thereby enabling zero-shot generalization without task-specific tuning. We construct DeSTA-AQA5M, a large-scale, task-agnostic dataset containing 5 million training samples derived from 7,000 hours of audio spanning 50 diverse datasets, including speech, environmental sounds, and music. DeSTA2.5-Audio achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance across a wide range of audio-language benchmarks, including Dynamic-SUPERB, MMAU, SAKURA, Speech-IFEval, and VoiceBench. Comprehensive comparative studies demonstrate that our self-generated strategy outperforms existing training strategies. Our findings underscore the importance of carefully designed data construction in LALM development and offer practical insights for building robust, general-purpose LALMs.
