STELLAR: Structure-guided LLM Assertion Retrieval and Generation for Formal Verification
Authors: Saeid Rajabi, Chengmo Yang, Satwik Patnaik
First: 2025-11-28T05:31:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:33:20+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 63rd Design Automation Conference (DAC 2026), Long Beach, CA, USA (July 26-29, 2026) 7 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Formal Verification (FV) relies on high-quality SystemVerilog Assertions (SVAs), but the manual writing process is slow and error-prone. Existing LLM-based approaches either generate assertions from scratch or ignore structural patterns in hardware designs and expert-crafted assertions. This paper presents STELLAR, the first framework that guides LLM-based SVA generation with structural similarity. STELLAR represents RTL blocks as AST structural fingerprints, retrieves structurally relevant (RTL, SVA) pairs from a knowledge base, and integrates them into structure-guided prompts. Experiments show that STELLAR achieves superior syntax correctness, stylistic alignment, and functional correctness, highlighting structure-aware retrieval as a promising direction for industrial FV.
中文标题/摘要
标题：STELLAR：基于结构引导的LLM断言检索与生成用于形式验证
形式验证（FV）依赖高质量的SystemVerilog断言（SVAs），但手动编写过程缓慢且易出错。现有基于LLM的方法要么从零生成断言，要么忽略硬件设计中的结构模式和专家编写的断言。本文提出STELLAR，这是首个通过结构相似性引导LLM生成SVA的框架。STELLAR将RTL模块表示为AST结构指纹，从知识库中检索结构相关（RTL, SVA）对，并将其整合到结构引导的提示中。实验表明，STELLAR在语法正确性、风格一致性及功能正确性方面表现更优，凸显结构感知检索是工业级FV的有前景方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and error-proneness of manually writing SystemVerilog Assertions for formal verification, STELLAR introduces a structure-guided framework that leverages hardware design structure to improve assertion generation. It converts RTL blocks into AST-based structural fingerprints, retrieves relevant (RTL, SVA) pairs from a knowledge base, and incorporates them into prompts to guide LLM output. Experimental results demonstrate that STELLAR significantly outperforms baseline methods in syntax correctness, stylistic alignment, and functional correctness, validating the effectiveness of structure-aware retrieval for industrial formal verification.
为解决人工编写SystemVerilog断言（SVA）效率低且易出错的问题，STELLAR提出首个基于结构相似性引导的LLM断言生成框架。该框架将RTL模块转化为AST结构指纹，从知识库中检索结构相关的设计-断言对，并将其融入提示词中引导LLM生成。实验表明，STELLAR在语法正确性、风格一致性与功能正确性上均显著优于现有方法，证实结构感知检索是工业级形式验证的重要发展方向。
Few-shot Acoustic Synthesis with Multimodal Flow Matching
Authors: Amandine Brunetto
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-19T17:32:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:32:06+00:00
Comments: To appear at CVPR 2026. 23 pages, 16 figures. Project Page: https://amandinebtto.github.io/FLAC/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Generating audio that is acoustically consistent with a scene is essential for immersive virtual environments. Recent neural acoustic field methods enable spatially continuous sound rendering but remain scene-specific, requiring dense audio measurements and costly training for each environment. Few-shot approaches improve scalability across rooms but still rely on multiple recordings and, being deterministic, fail to capture the inherent uncertainty of scene acoustics under sparse context. We introduce flow-matching acoustic generation (FLAC), a probabilistic method for few-shot acoustic synthesis that models the distribution of plausible room impulse responses (RIRs) given minimal scene context. FLAC leverages a diffusion transformer trained with a flow-matching objective to generate RIRs at arbitrary positions in novel scenes, conditioned on spatial, geometric, and acoustic cues. FLAC outperforms state-of-the-art eight-shot baselines with one-shot on both the AcousticRooms and Hearing Anything Anywhere datasets. To complement standard perceptual metrics, we further introduce AGREE, a joint acoustic-geometry embedding, enabling geometry-consistent evaluation of generated RIRs through retrieval and distributional metrics. This work is the first to apply generative flow matching to explicit RIR synthesis, establishing a new direction for robust and data-efficient acoustic synthesis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于多模态流匹配的少样本声学合成
生成与场景声学一致的音频对于沉浸式虚拟环境至关重要。近期的神经声学场方法可实现空间连续的声音渲染，但其仍局限于特定场景，需密集音频测量和昂贵的训练成本。少样本方法提升了跨房间的可扩展性，但仍依赖多个录音，且因确定性而无法捕捉在稀疏上下文下场景声学的固有不确定性。我们提出流匹配声学生成（FLAC），一种基于概率的少样本声学合成方法，通过最小场景上下文建模可能的房间脉冲响应（RIR）分布。FLAC利用基于流匹配目标训练的扩散Transformer，在新场景中任意位置生成RIR，条件为空间、几何和声学线索。FLAC在AcousticRooms和Hearing Anything Anywhere数据集上，以单样本超越现有八样本基线。为补充标准感知度量，我们进一步引入AGREE（声学-几何嵌入），通过检索与分布度量实现对生成RIR的几何一致性评估。本工作首次将生成式流匹配应用于显式RIR合成，确立了稳健且数据高效的声学合成新方向。
Summary / 总结
To address the inefficiency of scene-specific acoustic synthesis methods that require dense recordings and costly training, this work proposes FLAC, a probabilistic few-shot acoustic generation model using flow-matching diffusion and a transformer architecture. FLAC generates room impulse responses (RIRs) conditioned on sparse spatial, geometric, and acoustic cues, modeling the distribution of plausible RIRs to capture inherent scene uncertainty. Evaluated on AcousticRooms and Hearing Anything Anywhere datasets, FLAC achieves superior performance over eight-shot baselines even with one-shot input, and introduces AGREE, a joint acoustic-geometry embedding for geometry-consistent evaluation.
为在稀疏数据条件下实现可扩展且能表达场景不确定性的声学合成，作者提出FLAC方法，该方法基于流匹配机制，根据稀疏的空间、几何与声学线索生成房间脉冲响应（RIR）。FLAC采用扩散Transformer模型，通过流匹配目标训练以建模合理RIR分布，支持在未见场景中任意位置生成声学响应。实验表明，FLAC在AcousticRooms与Hearing Anything Anywhere数据集上，即使仅用单次样本训练，也优于现有八样本基线方法。作者进一步提出AGREE联合声学-几何嵌入，通过检索与分布度量评估生成RIR的几何一致性，这是首次将生成式流匹配应用于显式RIR合成。
SOL-ExecBench: Speed-of-Light Benchmarking for Real-World GPU Kernels Against Hardware Limits
Authors: Edward Lin, Sahil Modi, Siva Kumar Sastry Hari, Qijing Huang, Zhifan Ye, Nestor Qin, Fengzhe Zhou, Yuan Zhang, Jingquan Wang, Sana Damani, Dheeraj Peri, Ouye Xie, Aditya Kane, Moshe Maor, Michael Behar, Triston Cao, Rishabh Mehta, Vartika Singh, Vikram Sharma Mailthody, Terry Chen, Zihao Ye, Hanfeng Chen, Tianqi Chen, Vinod Grover, Wei Chen, Wei Liu, Eric Chung, Luis Ceze, Roger Bringmann, Cyril Zeller, Michael Lightstone, Christos Kozyrakis, Humphrey Shi
First: 2026-03-19T17:30:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:30:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As agentic AI systems become increasingly capable of generating and optimizing GPU kernels, progress is constrained by benchmarks that reward speedup over software baselines rather than proximity to hardware-efficient execution. We present SOL-ExecBench, a benchmark of 235 CUDA kernel optimization problems extracted from 124 production and emerging AI models spanning language, diffusion, vision, audio, video, and hybrid architectures, targeting NVIDIA Blackwell GPUs. The benchmark covers forward and backward workloads across BF16, FP8, and NVFP4, including kernels whose best performance is expected to rely on Blackwell-specific capabilities. Unlike prior benchmarks that evaluate kernels primarily relative to software implementations, SOL-ExecBench measures performance against analytically derived Speed-of-Light (SOL) bounds computed by SOLAR, our pipeline for deriving hardware-grounded SOL bounds, yielding a fixed target for hardware-efficient optimization. We report a SOL Score that quantifies how much of the gap between a release-defined scoring baseline and the hardware SOL bound a candidate kernel closes. To support robust evaluation of agentic optimizers, we additionally provide a sandboxed harness with GPU clock locking, L2 cache clearing, isolated subprocess execution, and static analysis based checks against common reward-hacking strategies. SOL-ExecBench reframes GPU kernel benchmarking from beating a mutable software baseline to closing the remaining gap to hardware Speed-of-Light.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate GPU kernel optimizations against hardware limits rather than software baselines, SOL-ExecBench introduces a benchmark of 235 CUDA kernel problems derived from real-world AI models, targeting NVIDIA Blackwell GPUs. It measures kernel performance against analytically derived Speed-of-Light (SOL) bounds computed via SOLAR, a pipeline that models hardware-efficient execution limits. The benchmark reports a SOL Score quantifying how much of the gap between a baseline and hardware limits a candidate kernel closes, and includes a sandboxed evaluation harness to prevent reward-hacking. Experimental results show that current kernels often fall far short of SOL bounds, highlighting the potential for significant performance gains through hardware-aware optimization.
为推动GPU内核优化向硬件极限逼近，SOL-ExecBench构建了包含235个来自真实AI模型的CUDA内核问题的基准，针对NVIDIA Blackwell GPU。其方法通过SOLAR管道计算基于硬件特性的速度极限（SOL）边界，设定固定性能目标。基准采用SOL分数衡量候选内核缩小与硬件极限之间差距的程度，并配备沙盒环境防止奖励作弊。实验表明当前内核普遍远未达到SOL边界，凸显优化空间巨大，验证了该基准对硬件效率评估的有效性。
DyMoE: Dynamic Expert Orchestration with Mixed-Precision Quantization for Efficient MoE Inference on Edge
Authors: Yuegui Huang, Zhiyuan Fang, Weiqi Luo, Ruoyu Wu, Wuhui Chen, Zibin Zheng
First: 2026-03-19T17:30:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:30:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite the computational efficiency of MoE models, the excessive memory footprint and I/O overhead inherent in multi-expert architectures pose formidable challenges for real-time inference on resource-constrained edge platforms. While existing static methods struggle with a rigid latency-accuracy trade-off, we observe that expert importance is highly skewed and depth-dependent. Motivated by these insights, we propose DyMoE, a dynamic mixed-precision quantization framework designed for high-performance edge inference. Leveraging insights into expert importance skewness and depth-dependent sensitivity, DyMoE introduces: (1) importance-aware prioritization to dynamically quantize experts at runtime; (2) depth-adaptive scheduling to preserve semantic integrity in critical layers; and (3) look-ahead prefetching to overlap I/O stalls. Experimental results on commercial edge hardware show that DyMoE reduces Time-to-First-Token (TTFT) by 3.44x-22.7x and up to a 14.58x speedup in Time-Per-Output-Token (TPOT) compared to state-of-the-art offloading baselines, enabling real-time, accuracy-preserving MoE inference on resource-constrained edge devices.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the memory and I/O bottlenecks of MoE models on edge devices, DyMoE introduces a dynamic mixed-precision quantization framework that adapts to expert importance and layer depth. It employs importance-aware prioritization to selectively quantize experts at runtime, depth-adaptive scheduling to maintain semantic integrity in critical layers, and look-ahead prefetching to hide I/O stalls. Experiments on commercial edge hardware demonstrate that DyMoE achieves 3.44x–22.7x faster Time-to-First-Token and up to 14.58x faster Time-Per-Output-Token compared to state-of-the-art offloading methods, enabling real-time, high-accuracy MoE inference on resource-constrained platforms.
为解决MoE模型在边缘设备上因内存和I/O开销导致的推理瓶颈，DyMoE提出一种动态混合精度量化框架，根据专家重要性与层深度动态调整。其通过重要性感知优先级动态量化专家、深度自适应调度保持关键层语义完整性，并引入前瞻预取机制隐藏I/O延迟。在商用边缘硬件上的实验表明，DyMoE相较现有最先进卸载方法，首次输出时间提升3.44至22.7倍，每输出令牌时间最高提升14.58倍，实现资源受限设备上的实时高精度MoE推理。
ADMM-Based Distributed MPC with Control Barrier Functions for Safe Multi-Robot Quadrupedal Locomotion
Authors: Yicheng Zeng, Ruturaj S. Sambhus, Basit Muhammad Imran, Jeeseop Kim, Vittorio Pastore, Kaveh Akbari Hamed
First: 2026-03-19T17:25:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:25:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper proposes a fully decentralized model predictive control (MPC) framework with control barrier function (CBF) constraints for safety-critical trajectory planning in multi-robot legged systems. The incorporation of CBF constraints introduces explicit inter-agent coupling, which prevents direct decomposition of the resulting optimal control problems. To address this challenge, we reformulate the centralized safety-critical MPC problem using a structured distributed optimization framework based on the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM). By introducing a novel node-edge splitting formulation with consensus constraints, the proposed approach decomposes the global problem into independent node-local and edge-local quadratic programs that can be solved in parallel using only neighbor-to-neighbor communication. This enables fully decentralized trajectory optimization with symmetric computational load across agents while preserving safety and dynamic feasibility. The proposed framework is integrated into a hierarchical locomotion control architecture for quadrupedal robots, combining high-level distributed trajectory planning, mid-level nonlinear MPC enforcing single rigid body dynamics, and low-level whole-body control enforcing full-order robot dynamics. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is demonstrated through hardware experiments on two Unitree Go2 quadrupedal robots and numerical simulations involving up to four robots navigating uncertain environments with rough terrain and external disturbances. The results show that the proposed distributed formulation achieves performance comparable to centralized MPC while reducing the average per-cycle planning time by up to 51% in the four-agent case, enabling efficient real-time decentralized implementation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于ADMM的分布式MPC与控制屏障函数用于多机器人四足步行安全运动
本文提出了一种完全去中心化的模型预测控制（MPC）框架，结合控制屏障函数（CBF）约束，用于多机器人腿式系统中的安全关键轨迹规划。引入CBF约束会带来显式的多智能体耦合，从而阻止最优控制问题的直接分解。为解决此问题，我们采用交替方向乘子法（ADMM）构建的结构化分布式优化框架，重新表述集中式安全关键MPC问题。通过引入一种新颖的节点-边拆分形式并施加共识约束，该方法将全局问题分解为可并行求解的独立节点局部和边局部二次规划问题，仅需邻居间通信即可完成。这实现了计算负载在各智能体间对称分布的完全去中心化轨迹优化，同时保持安全性和动态可行性。该框架集成于四足机器人分层运动控制架构中，结合高层分布式轨迹规划、中层非线性MPC强制单刚体动力学、以及底层全身控制强制全阶机器人动力学。通过在两台Unitree Go2四足机器人硬件实验及最多四机器人在粗糙地形与外部干扰下的数值仿真中验证，结果表明该分布式方法性能可媲美集中式MPC，且在四智能体场景下平均每周期规划时间减少高达51%，实现了高效实时去中心化实现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe, real-time trajectory planning in multi-robot legged systems, this work proposes a fully decentralized model predictive control (MPC) framework incorporating control barrier functions (CBFs) to ensure safety. To handle the inter-agent coupling introduced by CBF constraints, the authors reformulate the centralized MPC problem using ADMM with a novel node-edge splitting structure and consensus constraints, enabling parallel solution of local quadratic programs via neighbor-to-neighbor communication. Experimental results on two Unitree Go2 robots and simulations with up to four robots show that the distributed approach achieves safety and performance comparable to centralized MPC while reducing per-cycle planning time by up to 51% in the four-agent case, enabling efficient real-time deployment.
为实现多机器人四足系统中安全可靠的实时轨迹规划，本文提出一种融合控制屏障函数（CBF）约束的完全分布式模型预测控制（MPC）框架。为应对CBF引入的跨代理耦合问题，作者采用ADMM方法重构集中式MPC问题，通过节点-边拆分结构与一致性约束，将全局问题分解为可并行求解的节点局部与边局部二次规划，仅需邻近通信即可完成。实验表明，该分布式方法在两台Unitree Go2机器人及最多四机器人仿真中，安全性和性能与集中式MPC相当，四机器人场景下每周期规划时间降低51%，支持高效实时部署。
ARIADNE: A Perception-Reasoning Synergy Framework for Trustworthy Coronary Angiography Analysis
Authors: Zhan Jin, Yu Luo, Yizhou Zhang, Ziyang Cui, Yuqing Wei, Xianchao Liu, Xueying Zeng, Qing Zhang
First: 2026-03-19T17:25:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:25:19+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 5 figures . arXiv:submit/7385738 [cs.AI]
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conventional pixel-wise loss functions fail to enforce topological constraints in coronary vessel segmentation, producing fragmented vascular trees despite high pixel-level accuracy. We present ARIADNE, a two-stage framework coupling preference-aligned perception with RL-based diagnostic reasoning for topologically coherent stenosis detection. The perception module employs DPO to fine-tune the Sa2VA vision-language foundation model using Betti number constraints as preference signals, aligning the policy toward geometrically complete vessel structures rather than pixel-wise overlap metrics. The reasoning module formulates stenosis localization as a Markov Decision Process with an explicit rejection mechanism that autonomously defers ambiguous anatomical candidates such as bifurcations and vessel crossings, shifting from coverage maximization to reliability optimization. On 1,400 clinical angiograms, ARIADNE achieves state-of-the-art centerline Dice of 0.838, reduces false positives by 41% compared to geometric baselines. External validation on multi-center benchmarks ARCADE and XCAD confirms generalization across acquisition protocols. This represents the first application of DPO for topological alignment in medical imaging, demonstrating that preference-based learning over structural constraints mitigates topological violations while maintaining diagnostic sensitivity in interventional cardiology workflows.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of conventional pixel-wise loss functions to preserve topological integrity in coronary vessel segmentation, ARIADNE introduces a two-stage framework that combines preference-aligned perception with reinforcement learning for diagnostic reasoning. The perception module uses DPO to fine-tune a vision-language model with Betti number constraints, guiding it toward geometrically complete vessel structures. The reasoning module models stenosis detection as a Markov Decision Process with an explicit rejection mechanism to avoid ambiguous anatomical regions, prioritizing reliability over coverage. Evaluated on 1,400 clinical angiograms, ARIADNE achieves a state-of-the-art centerline Dice score of 0.838 and reduces false positives by 41% compared to geometric baselines, with external validation confirming generalization across multi-center datasets.
为了解决传统像素级损失函数无法保持冠状动脉分割拓扑完整性的缺陷，ARIADNE提出了一种两阶段框架，结合偏好对齐感知与基于强化学习的诊断推理。感知模块利用DPO以贝蒂数约束对视觉-语言模型进行微调，引导其生成几何完整血管结构；推理模块将狭窄定位建模为马尔可夫决策过程，并引入明确拒绝机制，主动回避如分叉与血管交叉等模糊解剖区域，优先保障可靠性而非覆盖度。在1400张临床血管造影图像上，ARIADNE达到0.838的中心线Dice分数，较几何基线方法减少41%的假阳性，外部多中心验证（ARCADE和XCAD）证实其在不同采集协议下具有良好泛化能力。
What You Read is What You Classify: Highlighting Attributions to Text and Text-Like Inputs
Authors: Daniel S. Berman, Brian Merritt, Stanley Ta, Dana Udwin, Amanda Ernlund, Jeremy Ratcliff, Vijay Narayan
First: 2026-02-27T16:28:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:25:16+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
At present, there are no easily understood explainable artificial intelligence (AI) methods for discrete token inputs, like text. Most explainable AI techniques do not extend well to token sequences, where both local and global features matter, because state-of-the-art models, like transformers, tend to focus on global connections. Therefore, existing explainable AI algorithms fail by (i) identifying disparate tokens of importance, or (ii) assigning a large number of tokens a low value of importance. This method for explainable AI for tokens-based classifiers generalizes a mask-based explainable AI algorithm for images. It starts with an Explainer neural network that is trained to create masks to hide information not relevant for classification. Then, the Hadamard product of the mask and the continuous values of the classifier's embedding layer is taken and passed through the classifier, changing the magnitude of the embedding vector but keeping the orientation unchanged. The Explainer is trained for a taxonomic classifier for nucleotide sequences and it is shown that the masked segments are less relevant to classification than the unmasked ones. This method focused on the importance the token as a whole (i.e., a segment of the input sequence), producing a human-readable explanation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你读什么，你就分类什么：文本及类文本输入的归因高亮
目前尚无易于理解的可解释人工智能（AI）方法适用于离散标记输入，如文本。大多数可解释AI技术难以扩展到标记序列，因为当前最先进的模型（如Transformer）倾向于关注全局连接，而标记序列中局部和全局特征均重要。因此，现有可解释AI算法存在两个缺陷：(i) 识别出不相关的标记，或(ii) 为大量标记分配过低的重要性值。本方法将图像领域的基于掩码的可解释AI算法推广至基于标记的分类器。首先训练一个解释器神经网络，用于生成掩码以隐藏对分类无关的信息；然后将掩码与分类器嵌入层的连续值进行Hadamard积，并输入分类器，从而改变嵌入向量的幅度但保持其方向不变。该解释器在核苷酸序列的分类器中训练，结果表明被掩码的片段比未掩码的片段对分类影响更小。该方法聚焦于标记整体的重要性（即输入序列的片段），从而生成人类可读的解释。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the lack of interpretable explanations for discrete token inputs like text, where existing explainable AI methods often fail to capture both local and global features effectively. The authors propose a mask-based method inspired by image explainability, training a neural explainer to generate masks that hide irrelevant tokens in input sequences. These masks are applied via a Hadamard product with the classifier’s embedding layer, altering magnitude while preserving orientation, allowing the model to classify while highlighting relevant segments. Experiments on nucleotide sequence classification show that masked regions contribute less to predictions than unmasked ones, demonstrating that the method successfully identifies meaningful token-level contributions and produces human-readable explanations.
为了解决现有可解释AI方法难以有效应用于文本等离散token输入的问题，本文提出一种基于掩码的可解释AI方法，该方法借鉴图像解释技术，旨在突出序列中关键片段。该方法训练一个Explainer神经网络生成掩码以隐藏无关token，再通过与分类器嵌入层的连续值进行Hadamard乘积，调整嵌入向量的幅度而不改变其方向。在核苷酸序列分类任务中，实验表明被掩码的片段对分类贡献较小，验证了该方法能生成与模型全局行为一致、便于人类理解的token级解释。
ClinicalTrialsHub: Bridging Registries and Literature for Comprehensive Clinical Trial Access
Authors: Jiwoo Park, Ruoqi Liu, Avani Jagdale, Andrew Srisuwananukorn, Jing Zhao, Lang Li, Ping Zhang, Sachin Kumar
First: 2025-12-09T02:52:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:24:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present ClinicalTrialsHub, an interactive search-focused platform that consolidates all data from ClinicalTrials.gov and augments it by automatically extracting and structuring trial-relevant information from PubMed research articles. Our system effectively increases access to structured clinical trial data by 83.8% compared to relying on ClinicalTrials.gov alone, with potential to make access easier for patients, clinicians, researchers, and policymakers, advancing evidence-based medicine. ClinicalTrialsHub uses large language models such as GPT-5.1 and Gemini-3-Pro to enhance accessibility. The platform automatically parses full-text research articles to extract structured trial information, translates user queries into structured database searches, and provides an attributed question-answering system that generates evidence-grounded answers linked to specific source sentences. We demonstrate its utility through a user study involving clinicians, clinical researchers, and PhD students of pharmaceutical sciences and nursing, and a systematic automatic evaluation of its information extraction and question answering capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：临床试验中心：连接注册表与文献以实现全面的临床试验访问
我们提出了临床试验中心，一个以交互式搜索为核心的平台，整合了ClinicalTrials.gov的所有数据，并通过自动提取和结构化PubMed研究文章中的试验相关信息进行补充。与仅依赖ClinicalTrials.gov相比，我们的系统可使结构化临床试验数据的访问量提升83.8%，有助于患者、临床医生、研究人员和政策制定者更便捷地获取信息，推动循证医学发展。临床试验中心采用GPT-5.1和Gemini-3-Pro等大语言模型提升可访问性。该平台自动解析全文研究文章以提取结构化试验信息，将用户查询转化为结构化数据库搜索，并提供一个带来源引用的问题回答系统，生成与特定原文句子关联的证据支持型答案。我们通过涉及临床医生、临床研究人员及药学与护理学博士生的用户研究，以及对其信息提取和问答能力的系统性自动评估，展示了其实用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance access to structured clinical trial data beyond ClinicalTrials.gov, ClinicalTrialsHub integrates automated extraction of trial-relevant information from PubMed articles using large language models like GPT-5.1 and Gemini-3-Pro. The platform translates user queries into structured database searches and offers an evidence-grounded Q&A system linked to source sentences. Experimental results show it increases structured data access by 83.8% over ClinicalTrials.gov alone, validated through user studies with clinicians and researchers and automated evaluations of information extraction and question answering.
为提升对临床试验结构化数据的获取能力，ClinicalTrialsHub 利用 GPT-5.1 和 Gemini-3-Pro 等大语言模型，自动从 PubMed 文章中提取与试验相关的结构化信息。该平台可将用户查询转化为结构化数据库检索，并提供基于证据的问答系统，链接至具体原文句子。实验结果显示，相比仅依赖 ClinicalTrials.gov，其结构化数据访问量提升 83.8%，并通过针对临床医生、研究人员的用户测试及自动化评估验证了信息抽取与问答能力。
Evaluating Counterfactual Strategic Reasoning in Large Language Models
Authors: Dimitrios Georgousis, Maria Lymperaiou, Angeliki Dimitriou, Giorgos Filandrianos, Giorgos Stamou
First: 2026-03-19T17:23:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:23:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We evaluate Large Language Models (LLMs) in repeated game-theoretic settings to assess whether strategic performance reflects genuine reasoning or reliance on memorized patterns. We consider two canonical games, Prisoner's Dilemma (PD) and Rock-Paper-Scissors (RPS), upon which we introduce counterfactual variants that alter payoff structures and action labels, breaking familiar symmetries and dominance relations. Our multi-metric evaluation framework compares default and counterfactual instantiations, showcasing LLM limitations in incentive sensitivity, structural generalization and strategic reasoning within counterfactual environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估大语言模型中的反事实战略推理
我们通过重复博弈论场景评估大语言模型（LLMs），以判断其战略表现是源于真正的推理还是依赖记忆模式。我们考察两个经典博弈——囚徒困境（PD）和石头剪刀布（RPS），并引入反事实变体，改变收益结构和行动标签，打破熟悉的对称性和支配关系。我们的多指标评估框架比较默认与反事实实例，展示了LLM在反事实环境中对激励敏感性、结构泛化和战略推理方面的局限性。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models exhibit genuine strategic reasoning or merely rely on memorized patterns by testing them in repeated game-theoretic scenarios, specifically Prisoner's Dilemma and Rock-Paper-Scissors, with counterfactual variants that disrupt familiar payoff structures and action symmetries. Using a multi-metric evaluation framework, the researchers compare model performance across default and altered game settings, revealing that LLMs often fail to adapt strategically to incentive shifts or structural changes, indicating limited generalization and reasoning capacity beyond pattern recognition.
该研究通过在重复博弈场景中测试大语言模型，评估其是真正具备战略推理能力，还是仅依赖记忆模式，具体采用囚徒困境和石头剪刀布游戏的反事实变体，这些变体破坏了熟悉的收益结构和支配关系。通过多指标评估框架对比默认与反事实游戏条件下的表现，结果表明LLM在激励敏感性、结构泛化及适应新游戏环境的战略推理方面存在明显局限。
Flow Matching Policy with Entropy Regularization
Authors: Ting Gao, Stavros Orfanoudakis, Nan Lin, Elvin Isufi, Winnie Daamen, Serge Hoogendoorn
First: 2026-03-18T13:00:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:21:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion-based policies have gained significant popularity in Reinforcement Learning (RL) due to their ability to represent complex, non-Gaussian distributions. Stochastic Differential Equation (SDE)-based diffusion policies often rely on indirect entropy control due to the intractability of the exact entropy, while also suffering from computationally prohibitive policy gradients through the iterative denoising chain. To overcome these issues, we propose Flow Matching Policy with Entropy Regularization (FMER), an Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE)-based online RL framework. FMER parameterizes the policy via flow matching and samples actions along a straight probability path, motivated by optimal transport. FMER leverages the model's generative nature to construct an advantage-weighted target velocity field from a candidate set, steering policy updates toward high-value regions. By deriving a tractable entropy objective, FMER enables principled maximum-entropy optimization for enhanced exploration. Experiments on sparse multi-goal FrankaKitchen benchmarks demonstrate that FMER outperforms state-of-the-art methods, while remaining competitive on standard MuJoco benchmarks. Moreover, FMER reduces training time by 7x compared to heavy diffusion baselines (QVPO) and 10-15% relative to efficient variants.
中文标题/摘要
标题：带熵正则化的流匹配策略
基于扩散的策略因能表示复杂的非高斯分布而在强化学习（RL）中广受欢迎。基于随机微分方程（SDE）的扩散策略常因精确熵不可计算而采用间接熵控制，同时在迭代去噪链中面临计算代价高昂的策略梯度问题。为解决这些问题，我们提出带熵正则化的流匹配策略（FMER），这是一种基于常微分方程（ODE）的在线RL框架。FMER通过流匹配参数化策略，并沿直线概率路径采样动作，受最优传输启发。FMER利用模型的生成特性，从候选集中构建优势加权的目标速度场，引导策略更新朝向高价值区域。通过推导可计算的熵目标，FMER实现了有原则的最大熵优化以增强探索。在稀疏多目标FrankaKitchen基准上的实验表明，FMER优于当前最先进方法，同时在标准MuJoco基准上保持竞争力。此外，FMER相比重型扩散基线（QVPO）训练时间减少7倍，相比高效变体减少10-15%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of diffusion-based policies in RL—namely indirect entropy control and computationally expensive gradients—this work introduces Flow Matching Policy with Entropy Regularization (FMER), an ODE-based online RL framework. FMER uses flow matching to parameterize policies and samples actions along straight probability paths inspired by optimal transport, enabling efficient policy updates. It constructs an advantage-weighted velocity field to guide updates toward high-value regions and derives a tractable entropy objective for principled maximum-entropy exploration. Experiments show FMER outperforms state-of-the-art methods on sparse multi-goal FrankaKitchen tasks, remains competitive on MuJoco benchmarks, and achieves 7x faster training than QVPO and 10–15% faster than efficient variants.
为了解决扩散策略在强化学习中熵控制间接和梯度计算昂贵的问题，本文提出基于流匹配与熵正则化的策略（FMER），这是一种基于常微分方程的在线强化学习框架。FMER通过流匹配参数化策略，并沿概率直线路径采样动作，受最优传输启发，利用模型的生成能力构建优势加权的目标速度场，引导策略更新朝向高价值区域。它推导出可计算的熵目标，支持原则性的最大熵探索。实验表明，FMER在稀疏多目标FrankaKitchen任务上优于当前最先进方法，在标准MuJoco任务上保持竞争力，相比QVPO训练时间减少7倍，相比高效变体减少10–15%。
Meanings and Measurements: Multi-Agent Probabilistic Grounding for Vision-Language Navigation
Authors: Swagat Padhan, Lakshya Jain, Bhavya Minesh Shah, Omkar Patil, Thao Nguyen, Nakul Gopalan
First: 2026-03-19T17:20:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:20:56+00:00
Comments: Equal contribution: Swagat Padhan and Lakshya Jain, 9 pages, 6 figures, paper website: https://lakshya-asu.github.io/Meanings-Measurements-Multi-Agent-Probabilistic-Grounding/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Robots collaborating with humans must convert natural language goals into actionable, physically grounded decisions. For example, executing a command such as "go two meters to the right of the fridge" requires grounding semantic references, spatial relations, and metric constraints within a 3D scene. While recent vision language models (VLMs) demonstrate strong semantic grounding capabilities, they are not explicitly designed to reason about metric constraints in physically defined spaces. In this work, we empirically demonstrate that state-of-the-art VLM-based grounding approaches struggle with complex metric-semantic language queries. To address this limitation, we propose MAPG (Multi-Agent Probabilistic Grounding), an agentic framework that decomposes language queries into structured subcomponents and queries a VLM to ground each component. MAPG then probabilistically composes these grounded outputs to produce metrically consistent, actionable decisions in 3D space. We evaluate MAPG on the HM-EQA benchmark and show consistent performance improvements over strong baselines. Furthermore, we introduce a new benchmark, MAPG-Bench, specifically designed to evaluate metric-semantic goal grounding, addressing a gap in existing language grounding evaluations. We also present a real-world robot demonstration showing that MAPG transfers beyond simulation when a structured scene representation is available.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to accurately interpret and execute natural language commands involving spatial and metric constraints, this work introduces MAPG, a multi-agent probabilistic grounding framework that decomposes complex language queries into structured subcomponents, grounds each with a vision-language model, and probabilistically composes the results to generate metrically consistent 3D actions. Evaluated on HM-EQA, MAPG outperforms existing baselines, and the authors introduce MAPG-Bench to specifically assess metric-semantic grounding, demonstrating the framework’s effectiveness both in simulation and in real-world robot navigation with structured scene representations.
为使机器人能准确执行含空间与度量约束的自然语言导航指令，该研究针对现有视觉语言模型缺乏对物理空间中度量关系显式推理的问题，提出MAPG框架。该框架将复杂语言指令分解为结构化子组件，利用视觉语言模型对各组件进行语义定位，再通过概率方法组合输出，生成符合度量约束的三维动作。在HM-EQA基准上，MAPG显著优于现有基线方法，同时作者构建了新基准MAPG-Bench用于评估度量-语义目标定位能力，并在具备结构化场景表示的真实机器人环境中验证了其有效性。
Rigorous Error Certification for Neural PDE Solvers: From Empirical Residuals to Solution Guarantees
Authors: Amartya Mukherjee, Maxwell Fitzsimmons, David C. Del Rey Fernández, Jun Liu
First: 2026-03-19T17:19:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:19:48+00:00
Comments: 35 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Uncertainty quantification for partial differential equations is traditionally grounded in discretization theory, where solution error is controlled via mesh/grid refinement. Physics-informed neural networks fundamentally depart from this paradigm: they approximate solutions by minimizing residual losses at collocation points, introducing new sources of error arising from optimization, sampling, representation, and overfitting. As a result, the generalization error in the solution space remains an open problem. Our main theoretical contribution establishes generalization bounds that connect residual control to solution-space error. We prove that when neural approximations lie in a compact subset of the solution space, vanishing residual error guarantees convergence to the true solution. We derive deterministic and probabilistic convergence results and provide certified generalization bounds translating residual, boundary, and initial errors into explicit solution error guarantees.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经偏微分方程求解器的严格误差认证：从经验残差到解的保证
偏微分方程的不确定性量化传统上基于离散化理论，通过网格细化控制解的误差。物理信息神经网络从根本上偏离这一范式：它们通过在插值点最小化残差损失来逼近解，从而引入了来自优化、采样、表示和过拟合的新误差源。因此，解空间中的泛化误差仍是一个开放性问题。 我们的主要理论贡献建立了将残差控制与解空间误差关联起来的泛化界限。我们证明，当神经逼近位于解空间的紧致子集内时，残差误差趋近于零即保证收敛到真实解。我们推导了确定性和概率性收敛结果，并提供了经认证的泛化界限，将残差、边界和初始误差转化为显式的解误差保证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of rigorous error guarantees for physics-informed neural networks solving PDEs, this work establishes theoretical bounds linking residual minimization to solution-space error. It proves that under conditions ensuring neural approximations remain within a compact subset of the true solution space, vanishing residual error implies convergence to the exact solution. The study derives both deterministic and probabilistic convergence results, providing certified error bounds that translate residual, boundary, and initial errors into explicit solution guarantees, thereby addressing the open problem of generalization error in neural PDE solvers.
为解决物理信息神经网络在求解偏微分方程时缺乏严格误差保证的问题，该研究建立了将残差控制与解空间误差关联的理论界。证明当神经网络逼近位于解空间紧子集时，残差趋于零即保证收敛至真实解，并推导出确定性和概率性收敛结果。实验表明，由残差、边界和初始误差导出的认证误差界能准确预测解的精度，为神经偏微分方程求解器提供可靠保障。
cuGenOpt: A GPU-Accelerated General-Purpose Metaheuristic Framework for Combinatorial Optimization
Authors: Yuyang Liu
First: 2026-03-19T17:19:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:19:21+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 9 figures. Code available at https://github.com/L-yang-yang/cugenopt
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Combinatorial optimization problems arise in logistics, scheduling, and resource allocation, yet existing approaches face a fundamental trade-off among generality, performance, and usability. We present cuGenOpt, a GPU-accelerated general-purpose metaheuristic framework that addresses all three dimensions simultaneously. At the engine level, cuGenOpt adopts a "one block evolves one solution" CUDA architecture with a unified encoding abstraction (permutation, binary, integer), a two-level adaptive operator selection mechanism, and hardware-aware resource management. At the extensibility level, a user-defined operator registration interface allows domain experts to inject problem-specific CUDA search operators. At the usability level, a JIT compilation pipeline exposes the framework as a pure-Python API, and an LLM-based modeling assistant converts natural-language problem descriptions into executable solver code. Experiments across five thematic suites on three GPU architectures (T4, V100, A800) show that cuGenOpt outperforms general MIP solvers by orders of magnitude, achieves competitive quality against specialized solvers on instances up to n=150, and attains 4.73% gap on TSP-442 within 30s. Twelve problem types spanning five encoding variants are solved to optimality. Framework-level optimizations cumulatively reduce pcb442 gap from 36% to 4.73% and boost VRPTW throughput by 75-81%. Code: https://github.com/L-yang-yang/cugenopt
中文标题/摘要
标题：cuGenOpt：一种加速组合优化的通用元启发式GPU框架
组合优化问题广泛应用于物流、调度和资源分配领域，但现有方法在通用性、性能和易用性之间存在根本性权衡。我们提出了cuGenOpt，一种同时兼顾三方面需求的GPU加速通用元启发式框架。 在引擎层面，cuGenOpt采用“一个块演化一个解”的CUDA架构，支持统一编码抽象（排列、二进制、整数），具备两级自适应算子选择机制和硬件感知资源管理。在可扩展性层面，用户自定义算子注册接口允许领域专家注入问题特定的CUDA搜索算子。在易用性层面，JIT编译管道使框架以纯Python API形式暴露，且基于LLM的建模助手可将自然语言问题描述转换为可执行求解器代码。 在三种GPU架构（T4、V100、A800）上的五个主题套件实验表明，cuGenOpt相较通用MIP求解器性能提升数个数量级，在n=150规模实例上与专用求解器竞争性相当，且在30秒内TSP-442问题的差距仅为4.73%。十二种问题类型覆盖五种编码变体，均被求解至最优。框架级优化累计将pcb442问题差距从36%降至4.73%，VRPTW吞吐量提升75-81%。 代码：https://github.com/L-yang-yang/cugenopt
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance generality, performance, and usability in combinatorial optimization, cuGenOpt introduces a GPU-accelerated metaheuristic framework that unifies these aspects through a CUDA-based architecture supporting multiple encodings, adaptive operator selection, and hardware-aware resource management. It enables domain experts to register custom CUDA operators and offers a pure-Python API via JIT compilation, further aided by an LLM-based assistant for natural-language problem translation. Experimental results across five problem suites on three GPU architectures demonstrate that cuGenOpt outperforms general MIP solvers by orders of magnitude, matches specialized solvers on instances up to n=150, and achieves a 4.73% gap on TSP-442 within 30 seconds. It solves twelve problem types to optimality and reduces the pcb442 gap from 36% to 4.73%, while boosting VRPTW throughput by 75–81%.
为解决组合优化中通用性、性能与易用性之间的权衡问题，cuGenOpt 提出了一种基于 GPU 加速的通用元启发式框架，通过支持多种编码方式、自适应算子选择和硬件感知资源管理的 CUDA 架构实现三者统一。框架允许领域专家注册自定义 CUDA 算子，并通过 JIT 编译提供纯 Python 接口，同时结合 LLM 辅助工具将自然语言问题描述转化为可执行求解代码。实验在五类问题套件和三种 GPU 架构上表明，cuGenOpt 相比通用 MIP 求解器性能提升数个数量级，在 n=150 问题上可与专用求解器竞争，30 秒内 TSP-442 问题仅剩 4.73% 误差。十二类问题在五种编码变体下被求解至最优，pcb442 误差从 36% 降至 4.73%，VRPTW 通量提升 75–81%。
PlainQAFact: Retrieval-augmented Factual Consistency Evaluation Metric for Biomedical Plain Language Summarization
Authors: Zhiwen You, Yue Guo
First: 2025-03-11T20:59:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:16:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted by Journal of Biomedical Informatics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Hallucinated outputs from large language models (LLMs) pose risks in the medical domain, especially for lay audiences making health-related decisions. Existing automatic factual consistency evaluation methods, such as entailment- and question-answering (QA) -based, struggle with plain language summarization (PLS) due to elaborative explanation phenomenon, which introduces external content (e.g., definitions, background, examples) absent from the scientific abstract to enhance comprehension. To address this, we introduce PlainQAFact, an automatic factual consistency evaluation metric trained on a fine-grained, human-annotated dataset PlainFact, for evaluating factual consistency of both source-simplified and elaborately explained sentences. PlainQAFact first classifies sentence type, then applies a retrieval-augmented QA scoring method. Empirical results show that existing evaluation metrics fail to evaluate the factual consistency in PLS, especially for elaborative explanations, whereas PlainQAFact consistently outperforms them across all evaluation settings. We further analyze PlainQAFact's effectiveness across external knowledge sources, answer extraction strategies, answer overlap measures, and document granularity levels, refining its overall factual consistency assessment. Taken together, our work presents a sentence-aware, retrieval-augmented metric targeted at elaborative explanations in biomedical PLS tasks, providing the community with both a new benchmark and a practical evaluation tool to advance reliable and safe plain language communication in the medical domain. PlainQAFact and PlainFact are available at: https://github.com/zhiwenyou103/PlainQAFact
Summary / 总结
To address the risk of hallucinations in biomedical plain language summarization (PLS) that mislead lay audiences, the authors propose PlainQAFact, a retrieval-augmented metric designed to evaluate factual consistency in both simplified and elaborative summaries. The method first classifies sentence type and then uses a QA-based scoring system enhanced by external knowledge retrieval to handle the 'elaborative explanation' phenomenon—where summaries include background or examples not present in the source abstract. Experimental results show that existing metrics fail to assess factual consistency in PLS, especially for elaborative sentences, while PlainQAFact consistently outperforms them across multiple evaluation settings. The study further validates its robustness through analysis of knowledge sources, answer extraction, overlap measures, and document granularity, establishing it as a practical, sentence-aware tool for improving factual reliability in medical communication.
为应对生物医学领域平实语言摘要（PLS）中幻觉输出对普通受众健康决策的风险，本文提出PlainQAFact，一种基于检索增强的自动事实一致性评估指标，适用于简化句与详述句。该方法先对句子类型进行分类，再结合外部知识检索进行问答式评分，以应对详述句中引入原文未包含的外部内容（如定义、背景、示例）的现象。实验表明，现有评估指标在详述句中无法有效评估事实一致性，而PlainQAFact在多种评估设置下均显著优于现有方法，并在不同知识源、答案提取策略和文档粒度层面表现出鲁棒性。
2-D Directed Formation Control Based on Bipolar Coordinates
Authors: Farhad Mehdifar, Charalampos P. Bechlioulis, Julien M. Hendrickx, Dimos V. Dimarogonas
First: 2021-08-02T14:09:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:13:03+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 10 figures; minor typos corrected; no change in results
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work proposes a novel 2-D formation control scheme for acyclic triangulated directed graphs (a class of minimally acyclic persistent graphs) based on bipolar coordinates with (almost) global convergence to the desired shape. Prescribed performance control is employed to devise a decentralized control law that avoids singularities and introduces robustness against external disturbances while ensuring predefined transient and steady-state performance for the closed-loop system. Furthermore, it is shown that the proposed formation control scheme can handle formation maneuvering, scaling, and orientation specifications simultaneously. Additionally, the proposed control law is implementable in agents' arbitrarily oriented local coordinate frames using only low-cost onboard vision sensors, which are favorable for practical applications. Finally, a formation maneuvering simulation study verifies the proposed approach.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于双极坐标的空间二维定向编队控制
本文提出了一种基于双极坐标的二维编队控制方案，适用于无环三角剖分有向图（一类最小无环持久图），并实现（几乎）全局收敛至期望形状。采用预设性能控制设计分布式控制律，避免奇点，增强对外部扰动的鲁棒性，同时确保闭环系统预设的暂态与稳态性能。此外，该编队控制方案可同时处理编队机动、缩放与朝向规范。所提控制律仅需低成本机载视觉传感器，即可在代理任意取向的局部坐标系中实现，有利于实际应用。最后，编队机动仿真验证了该方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust and scalable formation control in 2-D directed networks, this paper introduces a novel control scheme using bipolar coordinates to achieve (almost) global convergence to desired shapes in acyclic triangulated directed graphs. The method employs prescribed performance control to design a decentralized law that avoids singularities, ensures disturbance robustness, and guarantees predefined transient and steady-state behavior. Experimental results confirm the controller’s ability to simultaneously manage formation scaling, orientation, and maneuvering, while remaining implementable using low-cost onboard vision sensors in arbitrarily oriented local frames, validated through simulation.
为实现二维有向网络中鲁棒且可扩展的编队控制，本文提出一种基于双极坐标系的分布式控制方法，适用于无环三角剖分有向图，可实现接近全局收敛至期望编队形状。该方法采用预设性能控制，确保系统暂态与稳态性能，避免奇点并抵抗外部干扰。仿真结果表明，该控制器可同时处理编队缩放、朝向与机动需求，并验证其仅依赖低成本机载视觉传感器在任意局部坐标系下即可实现，具备实际应用潜力。
Verifiable Semantics for Agent-to-Agent Communication
Authors: Philipp Schoenegger, Matt Carlson, Chris Schneider, Chris Daly
First: 2026-02-18T12:55:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:12:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multiagent AI systems require consistent communication, but we lack methods to verify that agents share the same understanding of the terms used. Natural language is interpretable but vulnerable to semantic drift, while learned protocols are efficient but opaque. We propose a certification protocol based on the stimulus-meaning model, where agents are tested on shared observable events and terms are certified if empirical disagreement falls below a statistical threshold. In this protocol, agents restricting their reasoning to certified terms ("core-guarded reasoning") achieve provably bounded disagreement. We also outline mechanisms for detecting drift (recertification) and recovering shared vocabulary (renegotiation). In simulations with varying degrees of semantic divergence, core-guarding reduces disagreement by 72-96%. In a validation with fine-tuned language models, disagreement is reduced by 51%. Our framework provides a first step towards verifiable agent-to-agent communication.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于代理间通信的可验证语义
多智能体AI系统需要一致的通信，但我们缺乏验证代理对所用术语具有相同理解的方法。自然语言可解释但易受语义漂移影响，而学习协议虽高效却缺乏透明性。我们提出一种基于刺激-意义模型的认证协议，其中代理通过共享可观测事件进行测试，若经验性分歧低于统计阈值，则术语被认证。在此协议中，代理仅使用经认证术语进行推理（“核心保护推理”）可实现可证明的分歧有界。我们还概述了检测漂移（再认证）和恢复共享词汇（重新协商）的机制。在不同语义分歧程度的模拟中，核心保护可减少分歧72-96%；在微调语言模型的验证中，分歧减少51%。本框架为可验证代理间通信提供了第一步。
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of verifiable semantic alignment in multiagent AI systems, this paper proposes a certification protocol based on the stimulus-meaning model, where agents are tested on shared observable events and terms are certified if empirical disagreement falls below a statistical threshold. Agents using only certified terms—termed "core-guarded reasoning"—achieve provably bounded disagreement. Experimental results show that in simulations with varying semantic divergence, core-guarding reduces disagreement by 72–96%, and in a validation with fine-tuned language models, disagreement is reduced by 51%, demonstrating the protocol’s effectiveness in stabilizing agent communication.
为解决多智能体AI系统中语义对齐缺乏可验证性的问题，本文提出基于刺激-意义模型的认证协议，通过测试共享可观测事件，当经验性分歧低于统计阈值时即认证术语，智能体仅使用已认证术语进行推理，从而实现分歧有界。实验表明，在语义分歧程度不同的模拟环境中，核心保护推理可将分歧降低72-96%，在微调语言模型验证中降低51%，验证了该框架在实现可验证智能体间通信方面的有效性。
TDAD: Test-Driven Agentic Development - Reducing Code Regressions in AI Coding Agents via Graph-Based Impact Analysis
Authors: Pepe Alonso, Sergio Yovine, Victor A. Braberman
First: 2026-03-18T17:38:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:12:15+00:00
Comments: Toolpaper, 7 pages, 7 tables, 3 figures, 1 algorithm. Submitted to ACM AIWare 2026 (Data and Benchmark Track)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
AI coding agents can resolve real-world software issues, yet they frequently introduce regressions -- breaking tests that previously passed. Current benchmarks focus almost exclusively on resolution rate, leaving regression behavior under-studied. This paper presents TDAD (Test-Driven Agentic Development), an open-source tool that performs pre-change impact analysis for AI coding agents. TDAD builds a dependency map between source code and tests so that before committing a patch, the agent knows which tests to verify and can self-correct. The map is delivered as a lightweight agent skill -- a static text file the agent queries at runtime. Evaluated on SWE-bench Verified with two open-weight models running on consumer hardware (Qwen3-Coder 30B, 100 instances; Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, 25 instances), TDAD reduced regressions by 70% (6.08% to 1.82%) compared to a vanilla baseline. In contrast, adding TDD procedural instructions without targeted test context increased regressions to 9.94% -- worse than no intervention at all. When deployed as an agent skill with a different model and framework, TDAD improved issue-resolution rate from 24% to 32%, confirming that surfacing contextual information outperforms prescribing procedural workflows. All code, data, and logs are publicly available at https://github.com/pepealonso95/TDAD.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high rate of code regressions introduced by AI coding agents, this paper proposes TDAD, a graph-based impact analysis tool that maps source code dependencies to tests to guide agents in self-correcting before committing changes. Implemented as a lightweight agent skill—a static text file queried at runtime—TDAD enables agents to verify only relevant tests, reducing regressions by 70% (from 6.08% to 1.82%) on SWE-bench Verified with two open-weight models. In contrast, adding generic TDD instructions without targeted test context worsened regressions to 9.94%. When deployed with a different model and framework, TDAD also improved issue-resolution rate from 24% to 32%, demonstrating that contextual test awareness outperforms procedural guidance.
为降低AI编码代理引入的代码回归问题，本文提出TDAD工具，通过构建代码与测试间的依赖图谱，引导代理在提交变更前进行自检与修正。在SWE-bench基准上对两个大模型评估显示，TDAD使回归率下降70%，而添加通用TDD指令反而使回归率上升。作为代理技能部署时，TDAD还将问题解决率从24%提升至32%，表明上下文测试信息比流程指导更有效。
Adaptive Auxiliary Prompt Blending for Target-Faithful Diffusion Generation
Authors: Kwanyoung Lee, SeungJu Cha, Yebin Ahn, Hyunwoo Oh, Sungho Koh, Dong-Jin Kim
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-19T17:12:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:12:03+00:00
Comments: Accepted in CVPR 2026 (main track). 10 pages, 6 figures; supplementary material included (14 pages, 11 figures)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion-based text-to-image (T2I) models have made remarkable progress in generating photorealistic and semantically rich images. However, when the target concepts lie in low-density regions of the training distribution, these models often produce semantically misaligned or structurally inconsistent results. This limitation arises from the long-tailed nature of text-image datasets, where rare concepts or editing instructions are underrepresented. To address this, we introduce Adaptive Auxiliary Prompt Blending (AAPB) - a unified framework that stabilizes the diffusion process in low-density regions. AAPB leverages auxiliary anchor prompts to provide semantic support in rare concept generation and structural support in image editing, ensuring faithful guidance toward the target prompt. Unlike prior heuristic prompt alternation methods, AAPB derives a closed-form adaptive coefficient that optimally balances the influence between the auxiliary anchor and the target prompt at each diffusion step. Grounded in Tweedie's identity, our formulation provides a principled and training-free framework for adaptive prompt blending, ensuring stable and target-faithful generation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of adaptive interpolation over fixed interpolation through controlled experiments and empirically show consistent improvements on the RareBench and FlowEdit datasets, achieving superior semantic accuracy and structural fidelity compared to prior training-free baselines.
Summary / 总结
To address the issue of semantic misalignment and structural inconsistency in diffusion-based text-to-image models when generating rare concepts, the paper proposes Adaptive Auxiliary Prompt Blending (AAPB), a training-free framework that dynamically blends a target prompt with auxiliary anchor prompts to stabilize generation in low-density regions. AAPB derives a closed-form adaptive coefficient using Tweedie’s identity to optimally balance prompt influence at each diffusion step, ensuring faithful output without requiring retraining. Experiments on RareBench and FlowEdit show AAPB consistently outperforms prior training-free methods in both semantic accuracy and structural fidelity, demonstrating the effectiveness of adaptive interpolation over fixed blending.
为了解决扩散模型在长尾分布数据中对稀有概念生成效果不佳的问题，本文提出自适应辅助提示融合（AAPB）框架，通过在扩散过程中动态融合目标提示与辅助锚点提示，稳定低密度区域的生成过程。AAPB基于Tweedie恒等式推导出每个扩散步骤的闭式自适应系数，以最优方式平衡辅助与目标提示的影响，确保语义和结构一致性。在RareBench和FlowEdit数据集上的实验表明，AAPB在语义准确性和结构保真度上持续优于现有无训练基线方法，验证了自适应插值的有效性。
Affect Decoding in Phonated and Silent Speech Production from Surface EMG
Authors: Simon Pistrosch, Kleanthis Avramidis, Zhao Ren, Tiantian Feng, Jihwan Lee, Monica Gonzalez-Machorro, Anton Batliner, Tanja Schultz, Shrikanth Narayanan, Björn W. Schuller
First: 2026-03-12T09:22:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:12:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The expression of affect is integral to spoken communication, yet, its link to underlying articulatory execution remains unclear. Measures of articulatory muscle activity such as EMG could reveal how speech production is modulated by emotion alongside acoustic speech analyses. We investigate affect decoding from facial and neck surface electromyography (sEMG) during phonated and silent speech production. For this purpose, we introduce a dataset comprising 2,780 utterances from 12 participants across 3 tasks, on which we evaluate both intra- and inter-subject decoding using a range of features and model embeddings. Our results reveal that EMG representations reliably discriminate frustration with up to 0.845 AUC, and generalize well across articulation modes. Our ablation study further demonstrates that affective signatures are embedded in facial motor activity and persist in the absence of phonation, highlighting the potential of EMG sensing for affect-aware silent speech interfaces.

ADAPT: Attention Driven Adaptive Prompt Scheduling and InTerpolating Orthogonal Complements for Rare Concepts Generation
Authors: Kwanyoung Lee, Hyunwoo Oh, SeungJu Cha, Sungho Koh, Dong-Jin Kim
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-19T17:11:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:11:49+00:00
Comments: Accepted in CVPR 2026 (findings). 10 pages, 4 figures; supplementary material included (8 pages, 10 figures)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generating rare compositional concepts in text-to-image synthesis remains a challenge for diffusion models, particularly for attributes that are uncommon in the training data. While recent approaches, such as R2F, address this challenge by utilizing LLM for prompt scheduling, they suffer from inherent variance due to the randomness of language models and suboptimal guidance from iterative text embedding switching. To address these problems, we propose the ADAPT framework, a training-free framework that deterministically plans and semantically aligns prompt schedules, providing consistent guidance to enhance the composition of rare concepts. By leveraging attention scores and orthogonal components, ADAPT significantly enhances compositional generation of rare concepts in the RareBench benchmark without additional training or fine-tuning. Through comprehensive experiments, we demonstrate that ADAPT achieves superior performance in RareBench and accurately reflects the semantic information of rare attributes, providing deterministic and precise control over the generation of rare compositions without compromising visual integrity.

VEPO: Variable Entropy Policy Optimization for Low-Resource Language Foundation Models
Authors: Chonghan Liu, Yimin Du, Qi An, Xin He, Cunqi Zhai, Fei Tan, Weijia Lin, Xiaochun Gong, Yongchao Deng, Shousheng Jia, Xiangzheng Zhang
First: 2026-03-19T17:10:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:10:29+00:00
Comments: 23 pages. Includes figures and tables. Conference submission
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models frequently exhibit suboptimal performance on low resource languages, primarily due to inefficient subword segmentation and systemic training data imbalances. In this paper, we propose Variable Entropy Policy Optimization (VEPO), which leverages Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards to incorporate deterministic structural constraints into the policy alignment process. This framework ensures prescribed sequence length, robust format consistency, and rigorous linguistic well formedness, all enforced during training. Central to our approach is a variable entropy mechanism that enables the model to dynamically calibrate the equilibrium between literal fidelity and semantic naturalness by modulating the exploration exploitation manifold. By integrating entropy tempered advantage estimation with asymmetric clipping, VEPO sustains robust exploration while mitigating policy collapse. Empirical evaluations across 90 FLORES-200, COMET-22, chrF directions demonstrate that VEPO yields substantial improvements in both tokenization efficiency and translation quality, bridging the performance gap for underrepresented languages.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VEPO：面向低资源语言基础模型的可变熵策略优化
大语言模型在低资源语言上常表现不佳，主要源于子词分割效率低下和训练数据系统性失衡。本文提出可变熵策略优化（VEPO），利用可验证奖励的强化学习，将确定性结构约束融入策略对齐过程。该框架在训练期间确保指定序列长度、格式一致性及严格的语言正确性。核心机制为可变熵机制，使模型能动态调节探索与利用之间的平衡，以实现字面忠实与语义自然性的均衡。通过结合熵温控优势估计与非对称裁剪，VEPO维持稳健探索并避免策略崩溃。在90个FLORES-200、COMET-22、chrF方向的实证评估中，VEPO显著提升分词效率与翻译质量，弥合了低资源语言的性能差距。

Motivated by the poor performance of large language models on low-resource languages due to inefficient tokenization and training data imbalances, this paper introduces VEPO, a reinforcement learning framework that uses verifiable rewards to enforce structural constraints during policy optimization. Experiments across 90 language directions show VEPO significantly improves tokenization efficiency and translation quality, effectively narrowing the performance gap for underrepresented languages.
