Optimal Splitting of Language Models from Mixtures to Specialized Domains
Authors: Skyler Seto, Pierre Ablin, Anastasiia Filippova, Jiayuan Ye, Louis Bethune, Angelos Katharopoulos, David Grangier
First: 2026-03-19T17:07:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:07:05+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 11 tables, 17 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models achieve impressive performance on a variety of knowledge, language, and reasoning tasks due to the scale and diversity of pretraining data available. The standard training recipe is a two-stage paradigm: pretraining first on the full corpus of data followed by specialization on a subset of high quality, specialized data from the full corpus. In the multi-domain setting, this involves continued pretraining of multiple models on each specialized domain, referred to as split model training. We propose a method for pretraining multiple models independently over a general pretraining corpus, and determining the optimal compute allocation between pretraining and continued pretraining using scaling laws. Our approach accurately predicts the loss of a model of size N with D pretraining and D' specialization tokens, and extrapolates to larger model sizes and number of tokens. Applied to language model training, our approach improves performance consistently across common sense knowledge and reasoning benchmarks across different model sizes and compute budgets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从混合数据到专用领域的语言模型最优拆分
由于预训练数据的规模和多样性，语言模型在多种知识、语言和推理任务上表现出色。标准训练方法为两阶段范式：首先在完整语料库上进行预训练，然后在高质量的专用子集上进行专业化训练。在多领域设置中，这涉及在每个专用领域上对多个模型持续预训练，称为拆分模型训练。我们提出了一种方法，即在通用预训练语料库上独立预训练多个模型，并利用缩放定律确定预训练与持续预训练之间的最优计算分配。该方法能准确预测大小为N、预训练D个token、专业化D'个token的模型的损失，并可外推至更大模型规模和token数量。应用于语言模型训练，该方法在不同模型规模和计算预算下，持续提升常识知识和推理基准的表现。
Summary / 总结
Language models achieve impressive performance on a variety of knowledge, language, and reasoning tasks due to the scale and diversity of pretraining data available.
为解决多领域场景下传统两阶段语言模型训练效率低的问题，该研究提出一种基于缩放定律的预训练与专业化阶段计算资源分配优化方法。该方法独立预训练多个模型于通用语料库，再通过缩放定律确定每个模型在预训练与专业化阶段应分配的最优token数量。实验表明，该方法在不同模型规模和算力预算下，均能在常识知识与推理基准测试中稳定提升性能，证明其能有效平衡预训练与专业化阶段，最大化下游任务表现。
Fast and Effective Computation of Generalized Symmetric Matrix Factorization
Authors: Lei Yang, Han Wan, Min Zhang, Ling Liang
First: 2026-03-19T17:03:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:03:52+00:00
Comments: 41 pages, 2 figures, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we study a nonconvex, nonsmooth, and non-Lipschitz generalized symmetric matrix factorization model that unifies a broad class of matrix factorization formulations arising in machine learning, image science, engineering, and related areas. We first establish two exactness properties. On the modeling side, we prove an exact penalty property showing that, under suitable conditions, the symmetry-inducing quadratic penalty enforces symmetry whenever the penalty parameter is sufficiently large but finite, thereby exactly recovering the associated symmetric formulation. On the algorithmic side, we introduce an auxiliary-variable splitting formulation and establish an exact relaxation relationship that rigorously links stationary points of the original objective function to those of a relaxed potential function. Building on these exactness properties, we propose an average-type nonmonotone alternating updating method (A-NAUM) based on the relaxed potential function. At each iteration, A-NAUM alternately updates the two factor blocks by (approximately) minimizing the potential function, while the auxiliary block is updated in closed form. To ensure the convergence and enhance practical performance, we further incorporate an average-type nonmonotone line search and show that it is well-defined under mild conditions. Moreover, based on the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz property and its associated exponent, we establish global convergence of the entire sequence to a stationary point and derive convergence rate results. Finally, numerical experiments on real datasets demonstrate the efficiency of A-NAUM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：广义对称矩阵分解的快速有效计算
本文研究了一类非凸、非光滑、非利普希茨的广义对称矩阵分解模型，该模型统一了机器学习、图像科学、工程及相关领域中广泛出现的矩阵分解形式。在建模方面，我们证明了精确惩罚性质：在适当条件下，当惩罚参数足够大但有限时，对称诱导的二次惩罚能精确恢复对应的对称形式。在算法方面，我们引入辅助变量分裂形式，并建立了精确松弛关系，严格关联原始目标函数的驻点与松弛势函数的驻点。基于这些精确性质，我们提出了一种基于松弛势函数的平均型非单调交替更新方法（A-NAUM）。在每一步迭代中，A-NAUM交替更新两个因子块（近似最小化势函数），而辅助块以闭式更新。为确保收敛性并提升实际性能，我们进一步引入平均型非单调线搜索，并证明其在温和条件下是良定义的。此外，基于Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz性质及其关联指数，我们建立了整个序列全局收敛至驻点的结论，并推导了收敛速率结果。最后，基于真实数据集的数值实验验证了A-NAUM的高效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently solve a broad class of matrix factorization problems in machine learning and engineering, this paper addresses a nonconvex, nonsmooth, and non-Lipschitz generalized symmetric matrix factorization model. The authors establish two key exactness properties: an exact penalty property ensuring symmetry under large but finite penalty parameters, and an exact relaxation relationship linking stationary points of the original and relaxed potential functions. Based on these, they propose A-NAUM, an average-type nonmonotone alternating updating method that updates factor blocks by approximately minimizing the relaxed potential and updates the auxiliary block in closed form. The method is enhanced with a nonmonotone line search and proven to converge globally to a stationary point under mild conditions, with convergence rates derived via the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz property. Numerical experiments on real datasets confirm A-NAUM’s efficiency and effectiveness.
为解决机器学习与工程领域广泛存在的矩阵分解问题，本文研究了一类非凸、非光滑、非利普希茨的广义对称矩阵分解模型。作者首先建立了两个精确性性质：一是证明在适当条件下，二次惩罚项可精确强制对称性；二是建立原问题与松弛势函数之间精确的松弛关系，将原问题的驻点与松弛函数的驻点严格关联。基于此，提出A-NAUM算法，交替更新两个因子块并以闭式更新辅助变量，结合非单调线搜索以提升收敛性与实用性。在温和条件下，算法全局收敛性由Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz性质保证，并推导出收敛速率。实验证明该算法在真实数据集上效率显著优于现有方法。
D5P4: Partition Determinantal Point Process for Diversity in Parallel Discrete Diffusion Decoding
Authors: Jonathan Lys, Vincent Gripon, Bastien Pasdeloup, Axel Marmoret, Lukas Mauch, Fabien Cardinaux, Ghouthi Boukli Hacene
First: 2026-03-19T17:02:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:02:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Discrete diffusion models are promising alternatives to autoregressive approaches for text generation, yet their decoding methods remain under-studied. Standard decoding methods for autoregressive models, such as beam search, do not directly apply to iterative denoising, and existing diffusion decoding techniques provide limited control over in-batch diversity. To bridge this gap, we introduce a generalized beam-search framework for discrete diffusion that generates candidates in parallel and supports modular beam-selection objectives. As a diversity-focused instantiation, we propose D5P4, which formulates the selection step as MAP inference over a Determinantal Point Process. Leveraging a scalable greedy solver, D5P4 maintains multi-GPU compatibility and enables an explicit trade-off between model probability and target diversity with near-zero compute overhead. Experiments on free-form generation and question answering demonstrate that D5P4 improves diversity over strong baselines while maintaining competitive generation quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：D5P4：用于并行离散扩散解码中多样性的划分行列点过程
离散扩散模型是文本生成领域相对于自回归方法的有前景替代方案，但其解码方法仍研究不足。自回归模型的标准解码方法（如束搜索）无法直接应用于迭代去噪过程，且现有扩散解码技术对批内多样性控制有限。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了一种适用于离散扩散的通用束搜索框架，可并行生成候选解并支持模块化束选择目标。作为以多样性为导向的具体实现，我们提出D5P4，将选择步骤建模为行列点过程上的最大后验推断。借助可扩展的贪心求解器，D5P4保持多GPU兼容性，并能在计算开销近零的情况下显式平衡模型概率与目标多样性。在自由形式生成和问答任务上的实验表明，D5P4在保持生成质量竞争力的同时，显著提升了多样性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for better diversity control in discrete diffusion models for text generation, this work introduces D5P4, a parallel decoding method that uses a Determinantal Point Process to model diversity during beam search. By formulating candidate selection as MAP inference over the DPP, D5P4 enables explicit trade-offs between model likelihood and diversity without significant computational cost, while remaining compatible with multi-GPU setups. Experimental results show that D5P4 outperforms existing baselines in generating diverse outputs across free-form and question-answering tasks, without sacrificing generation quality.
为解决离散扩散模型解码中缺乏多样性控制的问题，本文提出D5P4方法，将束搜索的选取步骤建模为行列式点过程上的MAP推理，从而在几乎无额外计算开销下实现模型概率与多样性的显式权衡。该方法基于通用束搜索框架，支持并行生成多个候选序列，保持多GPU兼容性。实验表明，D5P4在自由文本生成和问答任务中显著提升批内多样性，同时保持与强基线相当的生成质量。
Enhancing Pretrained Model-based Continual Representation Learning via Guided Random Projection
Authors: Ruilin Li, Heming Zou, Xiufeng Yan, Zheming Liang, Jie Yang, Chenliang Li, Xue Yang
First: 2026-03-19T17:01:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:01:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent paradigms in Random Projection Layer (RPL)-based continual representation learning have demonstrated superior performance when building upon a pre-trained model (PTM). These methods insert a randomly initialized RPL after a PTM to enhance feature representation in the initial stage. Subsequently, a linear classification head is used for analytic updates in the continual learning stage. However, under severe domain gaps between pre-trained representations and target domains, a randomly initialized RPL exhibits limited expressivity under large domain shifts. While largely scaling up the RPL dimension can improve expressivity, it also induces an ill-conditioned feature matrix, thereby destabilizing the recursive analytic updates of the linear head. To this end, we propose the Stochastic Continual Learner with MemoryGuard Supervisory Mechanism (SCL-MGSM). Unlike random initialization, MGSM constructs the projection layer via a principled, data-guided mechanism that progressively selects target-aligned random bases to adapt the PTM representation to downstream tasks. This facilitates the construction of a compact yet expressive RPL while improving the numerical stability of analytic updates. Extensive experiments on multiple exemplar-free Class Incremental Learning (CIL) benchmarks demonstrate that SCL-MGSM achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art methods.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of randomly initialized Random Projection Layers (RPLs) in handling large domain shifts when building upon pre-trained models, this work proposes SCL-MGSM, a method that constructs the RPL via a data-guided mechanism to progressively select target-aligned random bases, thereby enhancing expressivity while maintaining numerical stability. The approach inserts this guided RPL after the pre-trained model and uses a linear head for continual updates. Experimental results across multiple Class Incremental Learning benchmarks show that SCL-MGSM outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods without requiring exemplars.
为了解决在大域偏移下随机初始化的随机投影层（RPL）表达能力不足且易导致数值不稳定的问题，本文提出SCL-MGSM方法，通过数据引导机制逐步选择与目标任务对齐的随机基向量，构建紧凑且表达力强的投影层。该方法在预训练模型后接入引导式RPL，并配合线性分类头进行持续更新。在多个无样本增量学习基准上的实验表明，SCL-MGSM在严重域偏移场景下显著优于现有最先进方法，展现出更强的鲁棒性和准确性。
Milco: Learned Sparse Retrieval Across Languages via a Multilingual Connector
Authors: Thong Nguyen, Yibin Lei, Jia-Huei Ju, Eugene Yang, Andrew Yates
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-10-01T08:58:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T17:00:17+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learned Sparse Retrieval (LSR) combines the efficiency of bi-encoders with the transparency of lexical matching, but existing approaches struggle to scale beyond English. We introduce MILCO, an LSR architecture that maps queries and documents from different languages into a shared English lexical space via a multilingual connector. MILCO is trained with a specialized two-stage regime that combines Sparse Alignment Pretraining with contrastive training to provide representation transparency and effectiveness while mitigating semantic collapse. Motivated by the observation that uncommon entities are often lost when projected into English, we propose a new LexEcho head, which enhances robustness by augmenting the English lexical representation with a source-language view obtained through a special [ECHO] token. MILCO achieves state-of-the-art multilingual and cross-lingual LSR performance, outperforming leading dense, sparse, and multi-vector baselines such as BGE-M3 and Qwen3-Embed on standard multilingual benchmarks, while supporting dynamic efficiency through post-hoc pruning. Notably, when using mass-based pruning to reduce document representations to only 30 active dimensions on average, MILCO 560M outperforms the similarly-sized Qwen3-Embed 0.6B with 1024 dimensions, while achieving 3$\times$ lower retrieval latency and 10$\times$ smaller index size.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of scaling learned sparse retrieval beyond English, MILCO introduces a multilingual connector that maps queries and documents from multiple languages into a shared English lexical space. It employs a two-stage training regime combining sparse alignment pretraining and contrastive learning to preserve semantic fidelity while avoiding collapse. To address the loss of rare entities during projection, MILCO incorporates a LexEcho head that augments English representations with source-language context via a special [ECHO] token. Experimentally, MILCO achieves state-of-the-art performance on multilingual benchmarks, outperforming dense and sparse baselines like BGE-M3 and Qwen3-Embed, and demonstrates superior efficiency: with mass-based pruning reducing document vectors to 30 dimensions, its 560M variant surpasses Qwen3-Embed 0.6B (1024D) in retrieval accuracy while offering 3x lower latency and 10x smaller index size.
为了解决学习稀疏检索（LSR）难以扩展至多语言的问题，MILCO引入了一个多语言连接器，将不同语言的查询与文档映射到共享的英语词汇空间。其采用两阶段训练机制，结合稀疏对齐预训练与对比学习，以保持语义一致性并避免语义坍塌。为应对投影过程中罕见实体的丢失，MILCO提出LexEcho头，通过特殊[ECHO]标记增强英语表示，融入源语言上下文。实验表明，MILCO在多语言基准上达到最先进性能，超越BGE-M3和Qwen3-Embed等密集与稀疏基线模型；在使用基于质量的剪枝将文档表示压缩至平均30维时，MILCO 560M在检索精度上超越同样规模的Qwen3-Embed 0.6B（1024维），同时实现3倍更低延迟和10倍更小索引体积。
UGID: Unified Graph Isomorphism for Debiasing Large Language Models
Authors: Zikang Ding, Junchi Yao, Junhao Li, Yi Zhang, Wenbo Jiang, Hongbo Liu, Lijie Hu
First: 2026-03-19T16:59:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:59:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) exhibit pronounced social biases. Output-level or data-optimization--based debiasing methods cannot fully resolve these biases, and many prior works have shown that biases are embedded in internal representations. We propose \underline{U}nified \underline{G}raph \underline{I}somorphism for \underline{D}ebiasing large language models (\textit{\textbf{UGID}}), an internal-representation--level debiasing framework for large language models that models the Transformer as a structured computational graph, where attention mechanisms define the routing edges of the graph and hidden states define the graph nodes. Specifically, debiasing is formulated as enforcing invariance of the graph structure across counterfactual inputs, with differences allowed only on sensitive attributes. \textit{\textbf{UGID}} jointly constrains attention routing and hidden representations in bias-sensitive regions, effectively preventing bias migration across architectural components. To achieve effective behavioral alignment without degrading general capabilities, we introduce a log-space constraint on sensitive logits and a selective anchor-based objective to preserve definitional semantics. Extensive experiments on large language models demonstrate that \textit{\textbf{UGID}} effectively reduces bias under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings, significantly reduces internal structural discrepancies, and preserves model safety and utility.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistence of social biases in large language models despite output-level or data-based debiasing, this work proposes UGID, an internal-representation-level debiasing framework that models the Transformer as a computational graph with attention edges and hidden states as nodes. It enforces structural invariance across counterfactual inputs, allowing only sensitive attribute differences, and jointly constrains attention routing and hidden states in bias-sensitive regions to prevent bias migration. Experiments show UGID significantly reduces bias under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings, minimizes internal structural discrepancies, and maintains model safety and utility without degrading general performance.
鉴于输出层或数据优化方法无法彻底消除大语言模型中的社会偏见，本文提出UGID框架，将Transformer建模为计算图，其中注意力机制定义边，隐藏状态定义节点。该方法通过强制在反事实输入间保持图结构不变（仅允许敏感属性差异），并联合约束偏见敏感区域的注意力路由与隐藏状态，有效防止偏见跨组件迁移。实验表明，UGID在分布内与分布外场景下显著降低偏见，减少内部结构差异，同时保持模型安全性和实用性，未损害通用能力。
SHAPCA: Consistent and Interpretable Explanations for Machine Learning Models on Spectroscopy Data
Authors: Mingxing Zhang, Nicola Rossberg, Simone Innocente, Katarzyna Komolibus, Rekha Gautam, Barry O'Sullivan, Luca Longo, Andrea Visentin
First: 2026-03-19T16:58:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:58:31+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In recent years, machine learning models have been increasingly applied to spectroscopic datasets for chemical and biomedical analysis. For their successful adoption, particularly in clinical and safety-critical settings, professionals and researchers must be able to understand and trust the reasoning behind model predictions. However, the inherently high dimensionality and strong collinearity of spectroscopy data pose a fundamental challenge to model explainability. These properties not only complicate model training but also undermine the stability and consistency of explanations, leading to fluctuations in feature importance across repeated training runs. Feature extraction techniques have been used to reduce the input dimensionality; these new features hinder the connection between the prediction and the original signal. This study proposes SHAPCA, an explainable machine learning pipeline that combines Principal Component Analysis (for dimensionality reduction) and Shapely Additive exPlanations (for post hoc explanation) to provide explanations in the original input space, which a practitioner can interpret and link back to the biological components. The proposed framework enables analysis from both global and local perspectives, revealing the spectral bands that drive overall model behaviour as well as the instance-specific features that influence individual predictions. Numerical analysis demonstrated the interpretability of the results and greater consistency across different runs.
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for trustworthy model explanations in high-stakes spectroscopy applications, this study introduces SHAPCA, a pipeline that integrates Principal Component Analysis with Shapley Additive exPlanations to generate consistent, interpretable explanations in the original input space. By preserving the connection between model predictions and the original spectral signals, SHAPCA overcomes the instability caused by high dimensionality and collinearity in spectroscopic data. Experimental results show that SHAPCA delivers stable feature importance across repeated runs and enables both global and local interpretability, identifying key spectral bands that drive overall model behavior and instance-specific features influencing individual predictions.
为解决高维、强共线性光谱数据在临床等关键场景中模型可解释性不稳定的问题，本文提出SHAPCA方法，结合主成分分析与SHAP事后解释技术，直接在原始输入空间生成稳定、可解释的解释结果。该方法避免了传统特征提取导致的信号与预测脱节，实验表明其在多次训练中保持特征重要性的一致性，并支持全局与局部视角分析，揭示驱动整体模型行为的光谱波段及影响单个预测的实例特征，显著提升模型可解释性。
Clipped Gradient Methods for Nonsmooth Convex Optimization under Heavy-Tailed Noise: A Refined Analysis
Authors: Zijian Liu
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-12-29T03:35:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:58:28+00:00
Comments: A preliminary conference version is accepted at ICLR 2026. This full version includes the formal statements of lower bounds and their proofs. Moreover, the upper bounds are slightly improved
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Optimization under heavy-tailed noise has become popular recently, since it better fits many modern machine learning tasks, as captured by empirical observations. Concretely, instead of a finite second moment on gradient noise, a bounded ${\frak p}$-th moment where ${\frak p}\in(1,2]$ has been recognized to be more realistic (say being upper bounded by $σ_{\frak l}^{\frak p}$ for some $σ_{\frak l}\ge0$). A simple yet effective operation, gradient clipping, is known to handle this new challenge successfully. Specifically, Clipped Stochastic Gradient Descent (Clipped SGD) guarantees a high-probability rate ${\cal O}(σ_{\frak l}\ln(1/δ)T^{1/{\frak p}-1})$ (resp. ${\cal O}(σ_{\frak l}^2\ln^2(1/δ)T^{2/{\frak p}-2})$) for nonsmooth convex (resp. strongly convex) problems, where $δ\in(0,1]$ is the failure probability and $T\in\mathbb{N}$ is the time horizon. In this work, we provide a refined analysis for Clipped SGD and offer two faster rates, ${\cal O}(σ_{\frak l}d_{\rm eff}^{-1/2{\frak p}}\ln^{1-1/{\frak p}}(1/δ)T^{1/{\frak p}-1})$ and ${\cal O}(σ_{\frak l}^2d_{\rm eff}^{-1/{\frak p}}\ln^{2-2/{\frak p}}(1/δ)T^{2/{\frak p}-2})$, than the aforementioned best results, where $d_{\rm eff}\ge1$ is a quantity we call the $\textit{generalized effective dimension}$. Our analysis improves upon the existing approach on two sides: better utilization of Freedman's inequality and finer bounds for clipping error under heavy-tailed noise. In addition, we extend the refined analysis to convergence in expectation and obtain new rates that break the known lower bounds. Lastly, to complement the study, we establish new lower bounds for both high-probability and in-expectation convergence. Notably, the in-expectation lower bounds match our new upper bounds, indicating the optimality of our refined analysis for convergence in expectation.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better model real-world machine learning noise through heavy-tailed distributions, this work refines the analysis of clipped gradient methods for nonsmooth convex optimization under bounded p-th moment noise (p∈(1,2]). It introduces a generalized effective dimension (d_eff) and leverages Freedman’s inequality and tighter clipping error bounds to derive faster convergence rates: O(σ_l d_eff^{-1/(2p)} ln^{1-1/p}(1/δ) T^{1/p-1}) for nonsmooth convex and O(σ_l^2 d_eff^{-1/p} ln^{2-2/p}(1/δ) T^{2/p-2}) for strongly convex problems. These rates outperform prior results and are complemented by new lower bounds that match the upper bounds in expectation, demonstrating the optimality of the refined analysis.
为更真实地建模现代机器学习中的重尾噪声，本文对剪裁梯度方法在非光滑凸优化中的收敛性进行了精细化分析。通过引入广义有效维度 $d_{\rm eff}$，并结合改进的 Freedman 不等式与更精确的剪裁误差界，推导出优于现有结果的收敛速率：非光滑凸问题为 $\mathcal{O}(σ_{\frak l}d_{\rm eff}^{-1/2{\frak p}}\ln^{1-1/{\frak p}}(1/δ)T^{1/{\frak p}-1})$，强凸问题为 $\mathcal{O}(σ_{\frak l}^2d_{\rm eff}^{-1/{\frak p}}\ln^{2-2/{\frak p}}(1/δ)T^{2/{\frak p}-2})$。同时，该分析扩展至期望收敛，所得速率与新建立的下界一致，表明其在期望收敛性上具有最优性。
Hierarchical Latent Structure Learning through Online Inference
Authors: Ines Aitsahalia, Kiyohito Iigaya
First: 2026-03-19T16:57:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:57:36+00:00
Comments: 4 figures, 5 supplementary figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning systems must balance generalization across experiences with discrimination of task-relevant details. Effective learning therefore requires representations that support both. Online latent-cause models support incremental inference but assume flat partitions, whereas hierarchical Bayesian models capture multilevel structure but typically require offline inference. We introduce the Hierarchical Online Learning of Multiscale Experience Structure (HOLMES) model, a computational framework for hierarchical latent structure learning through online inference. HOLMES combines a variation on the nested Chinese Restaurant Process prior with sequential Monte Carlo inference to perform tractable trial-by-trial inference over hierarchical latent representations without explicit supervision over the latent structure. In simulations, HOLMES matched the predictive performance of flat models while learning more compact representations that supported one-shot transfer to higher-level latent categories. In a context-dependent task with nested temporal structure, HOLMES also improved outcome prediction relative to flat models. These results provide a tractable computational framework for discovering hierarchical structure in sequential data.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for learning systems to balance generalization and discrimination, this paper introduces HOLMES, a model that enables online inference over hierarchical latent structures. It combines a nested Chinese Restaurant Process prior with sequential Monte Carlo methods to perform trial-by-trial inference without supervision. Experimental results show HOLMES matches flat models in predictive performance while learning more compact representations that support one-shot transfer to higher-level categories, and outperforms flat models in a context-dependent task with nested temporal structure.
为解决学习系统在泛化与细节区分间需平衡的问题，本文提出HOLMES模型，通过在线推理学习分层潜在结构。该模型结合嵌套式中国餐馆过程先验与序列蒙特卡洛方法，实现无需显式监督的逐试推理。实验表明，HOLMES在预测性能上可媲美扁平模型，同时学习到更紧凑的表示，支持一次迁移至高层类别，并在具有嵌套时间结构的任务中优于扁平模型。
Implicit Patterns in LLM-Based Binary Analysis
Authors: Qiang Li, XiangRui Zhang, Haining Wang
First: 2026-03-19T16:56:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:56:56+00:00
Comments: 18 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Binary vulnerability analysis is increasingly performed by LLM-based agents in an iterative, multi-pass manner, with the model as the core decision-maker. However, how such systems organize exploration over hundreds of reasoning steps remains poorly understood, due to limited context windows and implicit token-level behaviors. We present the first large-scale, trace-level study showing that multi-pass LLM reasoning gives rise to structured, token-level implicit patterns. Analyzing 521 binaries with 99,563 reasoning steps, we identify four dominant patterns: early pruning, path-dependent lock-in, targeted backtracking, and knowledge-guided prioritization that emerge implicitly from reasoning traces. These token-level implicit patterns serve as an abstraction of LLM reasoning: instead of explicit control-flow or predefined heuristics, exploration is organized through implicit decisions regulating path selection, commitment, and revision. Our analysis shows these patterns form a stable, structured system with distinct temporal roles and measurable characteristics. Our results provide the first systematic characterization of LLM-driven binary analysis and a foundation for more reliable analysis systems.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of understanding of how LLM-based binary analysis agents organize exploration across hundreds of reasoning steps, this study conducts the first large-scale, trace-level analysis of 521 binaries and 99,563 reasoning steps to uncover implicit token-level patterns. The method involves systematic tracing and categorization of reasoning behavior, revealing four dominant implicit patterns: early pruning, path-dependent lock-in, targeted backtracking, and knowledge-guided prioritization. These patterns emerge organically from the model’s token-level decisions and collectively form a stable, structured system that governs path selection, commitment, and revision without explicit control-flow or predefined heuristics. The findings provide the first systematic characterization of LLM-driven binary analysis and offer a foundation for improving reliability in such systems.
为了解决LLM驱动的二进制分析系统在数百步推理过程中如何组织探索这一未被充分理解的问题，本研究首次对521个二进制文件的99,563步推理轨迹进行大规模追踪分析，识别出四种隐式令牌级模式：早期剪枝、路径依赖锁定、目标回溯和知识引导优先级选择。这些模式并非显式控制流或预设启发式规则，而是通过隐式令牌决策调节路径选择、承诺与修正，形成稳定且具有时间角色区分的结构化系统，为LLM驱动的二进制分析提供了首个系统性刻画，并为构建更可靠的分析系统奠定基础。
GSMem: 3D Gaussian Splatting as Persistent Spatial Memory for Zero-Shot Embodied Exploration and Reasoning
Authors: Yiren Lu, Yi Du, Disheng Liu, Yunlai Zhou, Chen Wang, Yu Yin
First: 2026-03-19T16:55:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:55:54+00:00
Comments: Project page at https://vulab-ai.github.io/GSMem/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Effective embodied exploration requires agents to accumulate and retain spatial knowledge over time. However, existing scene representations, such as discrete scene graphs or static view-based snapshots, lack \textit{post-hoc re-observability}. If an initial observation misses a target, the resulting memory omission is often irrecoverable. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{GSMem}, a zero-shot embodied exploration and reasoning framework built upon 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). By explicitly parameterizing continuous geometry and dense appearance, 3DGS serves as a persistent spatial memory that endows the agent with \textit{Spatial Recollection}: the ability to render photorealistic novel views from optimal, previously unoccupied viewpoints. To operationalize this, GSMem employs a retrieval mechanism that simultaneously leverages parallel object-level scene graphs and semantic-level language fields. This complementary design robustly localizes target regions, enabling the agent to ``hallucinate'' optimal views for high-fidelity Vision-Language Model (VLM) reasoning. Furthermore, we introduce a hybrid exploration strategy that combines VLM-driven semantic scoring with a 3DGS-based coverage objective, balancing task-aware exploration with geometric coverage. Extensive experiments on embodied question answering and lifelong navigation demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of our framework
中文标题/摘要
标题：GSMem：基于3D高斯点绘的零样本具身探索与推理的空间持久记忆
有效的具身探索要求代理在时间上积累和保留空间知识。然而，现有场景表示（如离散场景图或静态视图快照）缺乏\textit{事后可重观测性}。如果初始观测遗漏目标，由此导致的记忆缺失往往不可恢复。为弥合这一差距，我们提出\textbf{GSMem}，一个基于3D高斯点绘（3DGS）的零样本具身探索与推理框架。通过显式参数化连续几何与密集外观，3DGS作为持久空间记忆，赋予代理\textit{空间回忆}能力：即从最优、此前未占据的视角渲染逼真新视图。为实现此功能，GSMem采用检索机制，同时利用并行对象级场景图与语义级语言场。这种互补设计稳健定位目标区域，使代理能“生成”最优视角以支持高保真视觉-语言模型（VLM）推理。此外，我们引入混合探索策略，结合VLM驱动的语义评分与基于3DGS的覆盖目标，平衡任务感知探索与几何覆盖。在具身问答与终身导航上的大量实验验证了本框架的鲁棒性与有效性。

Adaptive Regime-Aware Stock Price Prediction Using Autoencoder-Gated Dual Node Transformers with Reinforcement Learning Control
Authors: Mohammad Al Ridhawi, Mahtab Haj Ali, Hussein Al Osman
First: 2026-03-19T16:55:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:55:33+00:00
Comments: Submitted to IEEE Transactions on Computational Social Systems. 17 pages, 9 figures, 10 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stock markets exhibit regime-dependent behavior where prediction models optimized for stable conditions often fail during volatile periods. Existing approaches typically treat all market states uniformly or require manual regime labeling, which is expensive and quickly becomes stale as market dynamics evolve. This paper introduces an adaptive prediction framework that adaptively identifies deviations from normal market conditions and routes data through specialized prediction pathways. The architecture consists of three components: (1) an autoencoder trained on normal market conditions that identifies anomalous regimes through reconstruction error, (2) dual node transformer networks specialized for stable and event-driven market conditions respectively, and (3) a Soft Actor-Critic reinforcement learning controller that adaptively tunes the regime detection threshold and pathway blending weights based on prediction performance feedback. The reinforcement learning component enables the system to learn adaptive regime boundaries, defining anomalies as market states where standard prediction approaches fail. Experiments on 20 S&P 500 stocks spanning 1982 to 2025 demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves 0.68% MAPE for one-day predictions without the reinforcement controller and 0.59% MAPE with the full adaptive system, compared to 0.80% for the baseline integrated node transformer. Directional accuracy reaches 72% with the complete framework. The system maintains robust performance during high-volatility periods, with MAPE below 0.85% when baseline models exceed 1.5%. Ablation studies confirm that each component contributes meaningfully: autoencoder routing accounts for 36% relative MAPE degradation upon removal, followed by the SAC controller at 15% and the dual-path architecture at 7%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于自编码器门控双节点变换器与强化学习控制的自适应市场状态感知股价预测
股票市场表现出依赖市场状态的行为特征，针对稳定条件优化的预测模型在波动期常失效。现有方法通常对所有市场状态一视同仁，或需人工标注市场状态，成本高昂且随市场动态快速过时。本文提出一种自适应预测框架，可自动识别偏离正常市场条件的状态，并将数据路由至专用预测路径。架构包含三部分：(1) 在正常市场条件下训练的自编码器，通过重构误差识别异常市场状态；(2) 分别针对稳定市场与事件驱动市场的双节点变换器网络；(3) 基于软演员-评论家（SAC）的强化学习控制器，根据预测性能反馈自适应调整状态检测阈值与路径融合权重。强化学习组件使系统能学习自适应市场边界，将异常定义为标准预测方法失效的市场状态。在1982至2025年间20只标普500股票上的实验表明，无强化控制器时单日预测MAPE为0.68%，完整自适应系统为0.59%，优于基线集成节点变换器（0.80%）；方向准确率达72%。系统在高波动期仍保持稳健性能，当基线模型MAPE超过1.5%时，本系统MAPE低于0.85%。消融实验表明各组件贡献显著：移除自编码器路由导致MAPE相对增加36%，SAC控制器贡献15%，双路径架构贡献7%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge that stock market prediction models often fail during volatile regimes due to their static design, this paper proposes an adaptive framework that dynamically identifies anomalous market states and routes data through specialized prediction pathways. The method combines an autoencoder to detect regime deviations via reconstruction error, dual node transformers tailored for stable and event-driven markets, and a Soft Actor-Critic reinforcement learning controller that tunes regime thresholds and pathway blending based on performance feedback. Experiments on 20 S&P 500 stocks from 1982 to 2025 show the full system achieves 0.59% MAPE and 72% directional accuracy, outperforming a baseline integrated transformer (0.80% MAPE) and maintaining robustness during high volatility, with MAPE below 0.85% while baseline exceeds 1.5%. Ablation studies confirm each component’s contribution, with autoencoder routing causing the largest degradation (36% relative MAPE increase) when removed.
为应对股票市场预测模型在波动期失效的问题，本文提出一种自适应框架，能动态检测市场异常并路由至专用预测路径。该方法结合自编码器通过重构误差识别异常市场状态，双节点Transformer分别处理稳定与事件驱动市场，以及基于Soft Actor-Critic的强化学习控制器动态调整检测阈值与路径融合权重。在1982至2025年20只标普500股票上的实验表明，完整系统MAPE达0.59%（优于0.80%基线），方向准确率72%，在高波动期仍保持MAPE低于0.85%，而基线模型MAPE超1.5%。
UDON: Uncertainty-weighted Distributed Optimization for Multi-Robot Neural Implicit Mapping under Extreme Communication Constraints
Authors: Hongrui Zhao, Xunlan Zhou, Boris Ivanovic, Negar Mehr
Venue: ICRA 2026
First: 2025-09-16T05:52:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:54:39+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IEEE International Conference on Robotics and Automation (ICRA 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-robot mapping with neural implicit representations enables the compact reconstruction of complex environments. However, it demands robustness against communication challenges like packet loss and limited bandwidth. While prior works have introduced various mechanisms to mitigate communication disruptions, performance degradation still occurs under extremely low communication success rates. This paper presents UDON, a real-time multi-agent neural implicit mapping framework that introduces a novel uncertainty-weighted distributed optimization to achieve high-quality mapping under severe communication deterioration. The uncertainty weighting prioritizes more reliable portions of the map, while the distributed optimization isolates and penalizes mapping disagreement between individual pairs of communicating agents. We conduct extensive experiments on standard benchmark datasets and real-world robot hardware. We demonstrate that UDON significantly outperforms existing baselines, maintaining high-fidelity reconstructions and consistent scene representations even under extreme communication degradation (as low as 1% success rate).
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable multi-robot mapping under extreme communication constraints such as packet loss and low bandwidth, this paper introduces UDON, a real-time distributed optimization framework that uses uncertainty weighting to prioritize reliable map regions and penalizes inter-agent mapping disagreements. Experiments on benchmark datasets and real robots show UDON maintains high-fidelity scene reconstructions and consistent representations even at communication success rates as low as 1%, significantly outperforming existing methods.
为应对通信丢包和带宽受限等极端通信挑战下多机器人神经隐式地图构建的可靠性问题，本文提出UDON框架，通过不确定性加权分布式优化，优先处理高置信度地图区域并惩罚通信对间地图不一致。实验表明，即使在通信成功率低至1%的极端条件下，UDON仍能保持高保真场景重建与一致地图表示，显著优于现有方法。
Efficient Reasoning with Balanced Thinking
Authors: Yulin Li, Tengyao Tu, Li Ding, Junjie Wang, Huiling Zhen, Yixin Chen, Yong Li, Zhuotao Tian
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-12T18:48:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:54:22+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have shown remarkable reasoning capabilities, yet they often suffer from overthinking, expending redundant computational steps on simple problems, or underthinking, failing to explore sufficient reasoning paths despite inherent capabilities. These issues lead to inefficiencies and potential inaccuracies, limiting practical deployment in resource-constrained settings. Existing methods to mitigate overthinking, such as suppressing reflective keywords or adjusting reasoning length, may inadvertently induce underthinking, compromising accuracy. Therefore, we propose ReBalance, a training-free framework that achieves efficient reasoning with balanced thinking. ReBalance leverages confidence as a continuous indicator of reasoning dynamics, identifying overthinking through high confidence variance and underthinking via consistent overconfidence. By aggregating hidden states from a small-scale dataset into reasoning mode prototypes, we compute a steering vector to guide LRMs' reasoning trajectories. A dynamic control function modulates this vector's strength and direction based on real-time confidence, pruning redundancy during overthinking, and promoting exploration during underthinking. Extensive experiments conducted on four models ranging from 0.5B to 32B, and across nine benchmarks in math reasoning, general question answering, and coding tasks demonstrate that ReBalance effectively reduces output redundancy while improving accuracy, offering a general, training-free, and plug-and-play strategy for efficient and robust LRM deployment. Project page and code are available at https://rebalance-ai.github.io .
中文标题/摘要
标题：平衡思维下的高效推理
大型推理模型（LRMs）虽具备卓越推理能力，但常因过度思考（在简单问题上消耗冗余计算步骤）或不足思考（未能充分探索推理路径）导致效率低下和潜在不准确，限制其在资源受限环境中的实际部署。现有缓解过度思考的方法（如抑制反思关键词或调整推理长度）可能无意中引发不足思考，损害准确性。为此，我们提出ReBalance，一种无需训练的框架，实现平衡思维下的高效推理。ReBalance利用置信度作为推理动态的连续指标，通过高置信度方差识别过度思考，通过持续过自信识别不足思考。通过将小规模数据集的隐藏状态聚类为推理模式原型，计算引导向量以调控LRMs的推理轨迹。动态控制函数根据实时置信度调节该向量的强度与方向，在过度思考时剪枝冗余，在不足思考时促进探索。在涵盖0.5B至32B参数的四个模型及数学推理、通用问答、编程任务的九个基准测试中，大量实验证明ReBalance有效减少输出冗余并提升准确性，提供了一种通用、无需训练、即插即用的LRM高效稳健部署策略。项目页面和代码见https://rebalance-ai.github.io。
Summary / 总结
To address the inefficiencies in large reasoning models caused by overthinking or underthinking, ReBalance introduces a training-free framework that balances reasoning dynamics using confidence as a continuous signal. It identifies overthinking through high confidence variance and underthinking via consistent overconfidence, then steers reasoning trajectories by aggregating hidden states into prototypes and applying a dynamic control function that modulates steering based on real-time confidence. Experiments across four model sizes (0.5B–32B) and nine benchmarks show ReBalance reduces output redundancy while improving accuracy, offering a plug-and-play solution for efficient and robust deployment.
为解决大推理模型因过度思考或思考不足导致的效率低下问题，本文提出ReBalance框架，该框架无需训练即可平衡推理动态。它利用置信度作为连续信号，识别过度思考（高方差）与思考不足（持续过高置信），并通过从小型数据集聚合的推理模式原型计算引导向量，动态调节推理轨迹。实验在四种模型规模和九个基准上表明，ReBalance能减少冗余并提升准确率，提供了一种即插即用的高效推理部署方案。
Infherno: End-to-end Agent-based FHIR Resource Synthesis from Free-form Clinical Notes
Authors: Johann Frei, Nils Feldhus, Lisa Raithel, Roland Roller, Alexander Meyer, Frank Kramer
First: 2025-07-16T14:06:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:52:38+00:00
Comments: EACL 2026 System Demonstrations | Code: https://github.com/j-frei/Infherno | Demo: https://infherno.misit-augsburg.de
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
For clinical data integration and healthcare services, the HL7 FHIR standard has established itself as a desirable format for interoperability between complex health data. Previous attempts at automating the translation from free-form clinical notes into structured FHIR resources address narrowly defined tasks and rely on modular approaches or LLMs with instruction tuning and constrained decoding. As those solutions frequently suffer from limited generalizability and structural inconformity, we propose an end-to-end framework powered by LLM agents, code execution, and healthcare terminology database tools to address these issues. Our solution, called Infherno, is designed to adhere to the FHIR document schema and competes well with a human baseline in predicting FHIR resources from unstructured text. The implementation features a front end for custom and synthetic data and both local and proprietary models, supporting clinical data integration processes and interoperability across institutions. Gemini 2.5-Pro excels in our evaluation on synthetic and clinical datasets, yet ambiguity and feasibility of collecting ground-truth data remain open problems.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for automated, generalizable conversion of unstructured clinical notes into standardized FHIR resources, the authors propose Infherno, an end-to-end agent-based framework leveraging LLMs, code execution, and healthcare terminology tools to ensure structural conformity. The system is evaluated against human baselines and demonstrates strong performance on both synthetic and real clinical datasets, with Gemini 2.5-Pro showing particular effectiveness. However, challenges remain in obtaining reliable ground-truth annotations for training and validation.
为解决将自由文本临床笔记自动转化为符合FHIR标准的结构化数据问题，作者提出Infherno框架，该框架结合LLM代理、代码执行与医学术语工具，实现端到端的结构化生成。实验表明，该系统在合成与真实临床数据上表现优于人工基准，其中Gemini 2.5-Pro表现突出，但仍面临真实标注数据获取困难与语义模糊性等挑战。
Introducing M: A Modular, Modifiable Social Robot
Authors: Victor Nikhil Antony, Zhili Gong, Yoonjae Kim, Chien-Ming Huang
First: 2026-03-19T16:51:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:51:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present M, an open-source, low-cost social robot platform designed to reduce platform friction that slows social robotics research by making robots easier to reproduce, modify, and deploy in real-world settings. M combines a modular mechanical design, multimodal sensing, and expressive yet mechanically simple actuation architecture with a ROS2-native software package that cleanly separates perception, expression control, and data management. The platform includes a simulation environment with interface equivalence to hardware to support rapid sim-to-real transfer of interaction behaviors. We demonstrate extensibility through additional sensing/actuation modules and provide example interaction templates for storytelling and two-way conversational coaching. Finally, we report real-world use in participatory design and week-long in-home deployments, showing how M can serve as a practical foundation for longitudinal, reproducible social robotics research.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to lower barriers to entry in social robotics, the authors introduce M, an open-source, low-cost robot platform designed for easy reproduction, modification, and real-world deployment. M integrates modular mechanical components, multimodal sensing, and a simplified actuation system with a ROS2-native software architecture that cleanly separates perception, expression, and data handling. The platform includes a simulation environment mirroring hardware for rapid behavior transfer and supports extensibility via plug-in modules and interaction templates for storytelling and conversational coaching. Experimental results from participatory design sessions and week-long in-home deployments confirm M’s practicality for longitudinal, reproducible social robotics research.
为降低社交机器人研究中的平台障碍，作者提出了M平台，一个开源、低成本的社交机器人系统，旨在便于复制、修改和真实场景部署。M采用模块化机械结构、多模态感知和简化机械执行系统，配合ROS2原生软件架构，清晰分离感知、表达与数据管理模块。平台配备与硬件等效的仿真环境，加速从仿真到现实的行为迁移。实验表明，通过扩展感知/执行模块，M可支持故事讲述与双向对话辅导等交互模板，并在参与式设计及家庭一周部署中验证其作为长期、可复现社交机器人研究基础的有效性。
From Inference Efficiency to Embodied Efficiency: Revisiting Efficiency Metrics for Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Zhuofan Li, Hongkun Yang, Zhenyang Chen, Yangxuan Chen, Yingyan, Lin, Chaojian Li
First: 2026-03-19T16:49:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:49:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently enabled embodied agents to perform increasingly complex tasks by jointly reasoning over visual, linguistic, and motor modalities. However, we find that the prevailing notion of ``efficiency'' in current VLA research, characterized by parameters, FLOPs, or token decoding throughput, does not reflect actual performance on robotic platforms. In real-world execution, efficiency is determined by system-level embodied behaviors such as task completion time, trajectory smoothness, cumulative joint rotation, and motion energy. Through controlled studies across model compression, token sparsification, and action sequence compression, we make several observations that challenge common assumptions. (1) Methods that reduce computation under conventional metrics often increase end-to-end execution cost or degrade motion quality, despite maintaining task success rates. (2) System-level embodied efficiency metrics reveal performance differences in the learned action policies that remain hidden under conventional evaluations. (3) Common adaptation methods such as in-context prompting or supervised fine-tuning show only mild and metric-specific improvements in embodied efficiency. While these methods can reduce targeted embodied-efficiency metrics such as jerk or action rate, the resulting gains may come with trade-offs in other metrics, such as longer completion time. Taken together, our results suggest that conventional inference efficiency metrics can overlook important aspects of embodied execution. Incorporating embodied efficiency provides a more complete view of policy behavior and practical performance, enabling fairer and more comprehensive comparisons of VLA models.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between conventional inference efficiency metrics and real-world robotic performance, this study redefines efficiency for Vision-Language-Action models by focusing on system-level embodied behaviors such as task completion time, motion smoothness, and energy consumption. Through controlled experiments involving model compression, token sparsification, and action sequence compression, the authors find that methods improving traditional metrics like FLOPs or token throughput often degrade actual execution efficiency or motion quality, while task success rates remain unchanged. Furthermore, embodied efficiency metrics reveal hidden differences in learned action policies, and common adaptation techniques like in-context prompting or fine-tuning yield only modest, metric-specific gains that may introduce trade-offs in other performance dimensions. The study concludes that embodied efficiency metrics are essential for capturing practical performance and enabling fairer model comparisons.
鉴于当前视觉-语言-动作模型的效率评估标准（如参数量、FLOPs或解码吞吐量）无法反映机器人平台的实际表现，本研究提出以系统级具身行为（如任务完成时间、轨迹平滑度和运动能耗）作为效率衡量标准。通过控制实验（模型压缩、令牌稀疏化、动作序列压缩）发现，降低计算量的方法常导致执行成本上升或运动质量下降，而具身效率指标揭示了传统评估中隐藏的动作策略差异；常见适配方法（如上下文提示或监督微调）仅能小幅改善特定具身效率指标，但常伴随其他指标（如完成时间）的恶化。研究结论认为传统效率指标忽略关键实际表现，主张引入具身效率以实现更全面、公平的模型比较。
On Optimizing Multimodal Jailbreaks for Spoken Language Models
Authors: Aravind Krishnan, Karolina Stańczak, Dietrich Klakow
First: 2026-03-19T16:48:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:48:28+00:00
Comments: Under Review at INTERSPEECH 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As Spoken Language Models (SLMs) integrate speech and text modalities, they inherit the safety vulnerabilities of their LLM backbone and an expanded attack surface. SLMs have been previously shown to be susceptible to jailbreaking, where adversarial prompts induce harmful responses. Yet existing attacks largely remain unimodal, optimizing either text or audio in isolation. We explore gradient-based multimodal jailbreaks by introducing JAMA (Joint Audio-text Multimodal Attack), a joint multimodal optimization framework combining Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG) for text and Projected Gradient Descent (PGD) for audio, to simultaneously perturb both modalities. Evaluations across four state-of-the-art SLMs and four audio types demonstrate that JAMA surpasses unimodal jailbreak rate by 1.5x to 10x. We analyze the operational dynamics of this joint attack and show that a sequential approximation method makes it 4x to 6x faster. Our findings suggest that unimodal safety is insufficient for robust SLMs. The code and data are available at https://repos.lsv.uni-saarland.de/akrishnan/multimodal-jailbreak-slm
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the safety risks in Spoken Language Models (SLMs) that combine speech and text, this work addresses the need for effective multimodal jailbreaks that exploit both modalities simultaneously. The authors propose JAMA, a joint optimization framework combining Greedy Coordinate Gradient for text and Projected Gradient Descent for audio, to generate adversarial inputs that jointly manipulate both modalities. Experiments across four SLMs and four audio types show JAMA achieves jailbreak success rates 1.5x to 10x higher than unimodal methods. Additionally, a sequential approximation technique reduces computation time by 4x to 6x without sacrificing performance, underscoring that unimodal defenses are inadequate for multimodal systems.
鉴于语音语言模型（SLM）融合语音与文本模态后安全风险加剧，本文提出JAMA框架，通过联合优化文本（使用贪心坐标梯度）与音频（使用投影梯度下降）实现跨模态对抗攻击。在四类SLM和四类音频上测试，JAMA的越狱成功率比单模态方法高1.5至10倍，且通过序列近似方法可提速4至6倍，表明仅依赖单模态安全防护不足以保障SLM的鲁棒性。
Splines-Based Feature Importance in Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks: A Framework for Supervised Tabular Data Dimensionality Reduction
Authors: Ange-Clément Akazan, Verlon Roel Mbingui
First: 2025-09-27T15:24:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:47:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Feature selection is a key step in many tabular prediction problems, where multiple candidate variables may be redundant, noisy, or weakly informative. We investigate feature selection based on Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs), which parameterize feature transformations with splines and expose per-feature importance scores in a natural way. From this idea we derive four KAN-based selection criteria (coefficient norms, gradient-based saliency, and knockout scores) and compare them with standard methods such as LASSO, Random Forest feature importance, Mutual Information, and SVM-RFE on a suite of real and synthetic classification and regression datasets. Using average F1 and $R^2$ scores across three feature-retention levels (20%, 40%, 60%), we find that KAN-based selectors are generally competitive with, and sometimes superior to, classical baselines. In classification, KAN criteria often match or exceed existing methods on multi-class tasks by removing redundant features and capturing nonlinear interactions. In regression, KAN-based scores provide robust performance on noisy and heterogeneous datasets, closely tracking strong ensemble predictors; we also observe characteristic failure modes, such as overly aggressive pruning with an $\ell_1$ criterion. Stability and redundancy analyses further show that KAN-based selectors yield reproducible feature subsets across folds while avoiding unnecessary correlation inflation, ensuring reliable and non-redundant variable selection. Overall, our findings demonstrate that KAN-based feature selection provides a powerful and interpretable alternative to traditional methods, capable of uncovering nonlinear and multivariate feature relevance beyond sparsity or impurity-based measures.
Summary / 总结
Feature selection is a key step in many tabular prediction problems, where multiple candidate variables may be redundant, noisy, or weakly informative.
Tendon-Actuated Robots with a Tapered, Flexible Polymer Backbone: Design, Fabrication, and Modeling
Authors: Harald Minde Hansen, Nandita Gallacher, Nicholas B. Andrews, Kristin Y. Pettersen, Jan Tommy Gravdahl, Mario di Castro
First: 2026-03-19T16:46:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:46:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents the design, modeling, and fabrication of 3D-printed, tendon-actuated continuum robots featuring a flexible, tapered backbone constructed from thermoplastic polyurethane (TPU). Our scalable design incorporates an integrated electronics base housing that enables direct tendon tension control and sensing via actuators and compression load cells. Unlike many continuum robots that are single-purpose and costly, the proposed design prioritizes customizability, rapid assembly, and low cost while enabling high curvature and enhanced distal compliance through geometric tapering, thereby supporting a broad range of compliant robotic inspection and manipulation tasks. We develop a generalized forward kinetostatic model of the tapered backbone based on Cosserat rod theory using a Newtonian approach, extending existing tendon-actuated Cosserat rod formulations to explicitly account for spatially varying backbone cross-sectional geometry. The model captures the graded stiffness profile induced by the tapering and enables systematic exploration of the configuration space as a function of the geometric design parameters. Specifically, we analyze how the backbone taper angle influences the robot's configuration space and manipulability. The model is validated against motion capture data, achieving centimeter-level shape prediction accuracy after calibrating Young's modulus via a line search that minimizes modeling error. We further demonstrate teleoperated grasping using an endoscopic gripper routed along the continuum robot, mounted on a 6-DoF robotic arm. Parameterized iLogic/CAD scripts are provided for rapid geometry generation and scaling. The presented framework establishes a simple, rapid, and reproducible pathway from parametric design to controlled tendon actuation for tapered, tendon-driven continuum robots manufactured using fused deposition modeling 3D printers.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for customizable, low-cost, and high-compliance continuum robots for inspection and manipulation tasks, this work designs and fabricates tendon-actuated robots with a tapered, flexible polymer backbone made from TPU. The method integrates an electronics base for direct tendon control and sensing, and develops a generalized Cosserat rod model that accounts for spatially varying cross-sections to predict robot behavior under tendon tension. Experimental results show the model accurately predicts robot shape within centimeter-level error after calibration of Young’s modulus, and demonstrates teleoperated grasping via an endoscopic gripper mounted on a 6-DoF arm, validating the design’s utility across a range of compliant tasks.
为满足对可定制、低成本且高柔顺性的连续体机器人在检测与操作任务中的需求，本研究设计并制造了采用腱驱动和锥形柔性聚合物背骨（TPU材料）的机器人。方法上，集成电子基座实现腱张力直接控制与传感，并建立基于Cosserat杆理论的通用正向静力学模型，以考虑截面空间变化对机器人行为的影响。实验结果表明，经Young模量校准后，模型可实现厘米级形状预测精度，并通过6自由度机械臂搭载内窥镜夹爪实现遥操作抓取，验证了该设计在多种柔顺任务中的实用性。
Revisiting Autoregressive Models for Generative Image Classification
Authors: Ilia Sudakov, Artem Babenko, Dmitry Baranchuk
First: 2026-03-19T16:44:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:44:23+00:00
Comments: Tech report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Class-conditional generative models have emerged as accurate and robust classifiers, with diffusion models demonstrating clear advantages over other visual generative paradigms, including autoregressive (AR) models. In this work, we revisit visual AR-based generative classifiers and identify an important limitation of prior approaches: their reliance on a fixed token order, which imposes a restrictive inductive bias for image understanding. We observe that single-order predictions rely more on partial discriminative cues, while averaging over multiple token orders provides a more comprehensive signal. Based on this insight, we leverage recent any-order AR models to estimate order-marginalized predictions, unlocking the high classification potential of AR models. Our approach consistently outperforms diffusion-based classifiers across diverse image classification benchmarks, while being up to 25x more efficient. Compared to state-of-the-art self-supervised discriminative models, our method delivers competitive classification performance - a notable achievement for generative classifiers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新审视用于生成式图像分类的自回归模型
类条件生成模型已成为准确且鲁棒的分类器，扩散模型在其他视觉生成范式（包括自回归模型）中展现出明显优势。本文重新审视基于视觉自回归的生成分类器，发现先前方法的重要局限：其依赖固定令牌顺序，对图像理解施加了限制性的归纳偏置。我们观察到，单顺序预测更依赖局部判别线索，而对多个令牌顺序取平均可提供更全面的信号。基于此见解，我们利用近期任意顺序自回归模型估计顺序边际预测，释放自回归模型的高分类潜力。我们的方法在多种图像分类基准上持续优于扩散式分类器，且效率高至25倍。与最先进的自监督判别模型相比，我们的方法实现具有竞争力的分类性能——这是生成式分类器的显著成就。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underperformance of autoregressive models in image classification due to their fixed token ordering, this work revisits AR-based generative classifiers and proposes leveraging any-order AR models to marginalize over token sequences, thereby reducing inductive bias. The method estimates classification predictions by averaging outputs across multiple token orders, capturing more comprehensive image signals. Experiments show this approach consistently outperforms diffusion-based classifiers on multiple benchmarks and achieves competitive results with state-of-the-art self-supervised discriminative models, while being up to 25x more computationally efficient.
鉴于传统自回归模型因固定令牌顺序导致图像分类性能受限，本文重新审视了自回归生成分类器，提出通过任意顺序自回归模型对令牌序列进行边际化平均，以降低模型的归纳偏置。该方法通过在多个令牌顺序上平均预测结果，捕捉更全面的图像信号。实验表明，该方法在多个图像分类基准上持续优于扩散模型分类器，同时与最先进的自监督判别模型相比表现竞争性，且效率高至25倍。
CustomTex: High-fidelity Indoor Scene Texturing via Multi-Reference Customization
Authors: Weilin Chen, Jiahao Rao, Wenhao Wang, Xinyang Li, Xuan Cheng, Liujuan Cao
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-19T16:44:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:44:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026. This version integrates the main paper and supplementary material
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The creation of high-fidelity, customizable 3D indoor scene textures remains a significant challenge. While text-driven methods offer flexibility, they lack the precision for fine-grained, instance-level control, and often produce textures with insufficient quality, artifacts, and baked-in shading. To overcome these limitations, we introduce CustomTex, a novel framework for instance-level, high-fidelity scene texturing driven by reference images. CustomTex takes an untextured 3D scene and a set of reference images specifying the desired appearance for each object instance, and generates a unified, high-resolution texture map. The core of our method is a dual-distillation approach that separates semantic control from pixel-level enhancement. We employ semantic-level distillation, equipped with an instance cross-attention, to ensure semantic plausibility and ``reference-instance'' alignment, and pixel-level distillation to enforce high visual fidelity. Both are unified within a Variational Score Distillation (VSD) optimization framework. Experiments demonstrate that CustomTex achieves precise instance-level consistency with reference images and produces textures with superior sharpness, reduced artifacts, and minimal baked-in shading compared to state-of-the-art methods. Our work establishes a more direct and user-friendly path to high-quality, customizable 3D scene appearance editing.
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for precise, high-fidelity 3D indoor scene texturing with instance-level control, CustomTex introduces a dual-distillation framework that combines semantic alignment via instance cross-attention with pixel-level fidelity enhancement, unified under a Variational Score Distillation optimization. Given an untextured 3D scene and reference images per object instance, the method generates a high-resolution texture map that maintains semantic consistency with references while minimizing artifacts and baked-in shading. Experimental results show CustomTex outperforms existing methods in instance-level accuracy, sharpness, and visual quality, enabling more direct and user-friendly customization of 3D scene appearances.
为实现高保真、实例级可控的3D室内场景纹理生成，CustomTex提出一种双蒸馏框架，结合实例交叉注意力引导的语义对齐与像素级视觉增强，统一于变分得分蒸馏优化下。输入为未纹理化的3D场景与每个物体实例的参考图像，输出高分辨率纹理图，保持语义一致性并减少伪影与烘焙阴影。实验表明，CustomTex在实例级一致性、清晰度与视觉质量上优于现有方法，为3D场景外观编辑提供了更直接、易用的定制化路径。
How Uncertainty Estimation Scales with Sampling in Reasoning Models
Authors: Maksym Del, Markus Kängsepp, Marharyta Domnich, Ardi Tampuu, Lisa Yankovskaya, Meelis Kull, Mark Fishel
First: 2026-03-19T16:42:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:42:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Uncertainty estimation is critical for deploying reasoning language models, yet remains poorly understood under extended chain-of-thought reasoning. We study parallel sampling as a fully black-box approach using verbalized confidence and self-consistency. Across three reasoning models and 17 tasks spanning mathematics, STEM, and humanities, we characterize how these signals scale. Both self-consistency and verbalized confidence scale in reasoning models, but self-consistency exhibits lower initial discrimination and lags behind verbalized confidence under moderate sampling. Most uncertainty gains, however, arise from signal combination: with just two samples, a hybrid estimator improves AUROC by up to $+12$ on average and already outperforms either signal alone even when scaled to much larger budgets, after which returns diminish. These effects are domain-dependent: in mathematics, the native domain of RLVR-style post-training, reasoning models achieve higher uncertainty quality and exhibit both stronger complementarity and faster scaling than in STEM or humanities.
Summary / 总结
Uncertainty estimation is critical for deploying reasoning language models, yet remains poorly understood under extended chain-of-thought reasoning.
为提升推理语言模型在长链推理中的不确定性估计能力，本研究考察了自洽性与语言化置信度在并行采样下的扩展表现。结果表明，两种信号均随采样量提升，但自洽性初始区分度较低，在中等采样下落后于语言化置信度。然而，二者结合可显著提升不确定性估计效果：仅用两样本的混合估计器平均提升AUROC达+12，且在更大采样预算下仍优于单一信号，之后收益递减。该效应因领域而异，数学领域（RLVR风格后训练原生领域）表现最优，自洽性与语言化置信度互补性更强、扩展效率更高，STEM与人文领域则表现较弱。
If Probable, Then Acceptable? Understanding Conditional Acceptability Judgments in Large Language Models
Authors: Jasmin Orth, Philipp Mondorf, Barbara Plank
First: 2025-10-09T16:12:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:35:09+00:00
Comments: EACL 2026 Main, 23 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conditional acceptability refers to how plausible a conditional statement is perceived to be. It plays an important role in communication and reasoning, as it influences how individuals interpret implications, assess arguments, and make decisions based on hypothetical scenarios. When humans evaluate how acceptable a conditional "If A, then B" is, their judgments are influenced by two main factors: the $\textit{conditional probability}$ of $B$ given $A$, and the $\textit{semantic relevance}$ of the antecedent $A$ given the consequent $B$ (i.e., whether $A$ meaningfully supports $B$). While prior work has examined how large language models (LLMs) draw inferences about conditional statements, it remains unclear how these models judge the $\textit{acceptability}$ of such statements. To address this gap, we present a comprehensive study of LLMs' conditional acceptability judgments across different model families, sizes, and prompting strategies. Using linear mixed-effects models and ANOVA tests, we find that models are sensitive to both conditional probability and semantic relevance$\unicode{x2014}$though to varying degrees depending on architecture and prompting style. A comparison with human data reveals that while LLMs incorporate probabilistic and semantic cues, they do so less consistently than humans. Notably, larger models do not necessarily align more closely with human judgments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如果可能，那么可接受？理解大语言模型中的条件可接受性判断
条件可接受性指条件语句被感知为多大程度上的合理。它在交流与推理中起重要作用，影响个体如何解读蕴含、评估论证及基于假设情境做决策。当人类评估条件语句“如果A，则B”的可接受性时，其判断受两大因素影响：B在A条件下的条件概率，以及前件A相对于后件B的语义相关性（即A是否对B有实质性支持）。尽管已有研究探讨大语言模型（LLMs）如何推断条件语句，但尚不清楚这些模型如何判断此类语句的可接受性。为填补这一空白，我们对不同模型家族、规模及提示策略下的LLMs条件可接受性判断进行了全面研究。通过线性混合效应模型和ANOVA检验发现，模型对条件概率和语义相关性均敏感——但敏感程度因架构和提示风格而异。与人类数据对比显示，尽管LLMs整合了概率和语义线索，但其整合一致性不及人类。值得注意的是，更大规模模型并不必然更贴近人类判断。
Summary / 总结
Conditional acceptability refers to how plausible a conditional statement is perceived to be.
该研究旨在探究大型语言模型如何评估条件语句的可接受性，动机在于理解模型是否能像人类一样推理假设性语义。研究者系统测试了不同架构和规模的模型在多种提示策略下的判断表现，采用线性混合效应模型和方差分析进行量化。结果表明，模型对条件概率和语义相关性均敏感，但敏感度因模型架构和提示方式而异。值得注意的是，尽管模型会整合这些线索，但其一致性不如人类，且模型规模更大并不必然更贴近人类判断。
MRD: Multi-resolution Retrieval-Detection Fusion for High-Resolution Image Understanding
Authors: Fan Yang, Xingping Dong, Xin Yu, Wenhan Luo, Wei Liu, Kaihao Zhang
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2025-12-02T16:22:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-19T16:35:02+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Understanding high-resolution (HR) images remains a critical challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Recent approaches leverage vision-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to retrieve query-relevant crops from HR images, improving understanding capacity of MLLMs. However, this paradigm often leads to object fragmentation, resulting in semantic bias and incomplete retrieval, while also introducing false positives from irrelevant background patches. To address these issues, we propose Multi-resolution Retrieval-Detection (MRD), a training-free framework that enhances HR image understanding from both local and global perspectives. Locally, MRD enforces cross-scale semantic consistency via multi-resolution semantic fusion to mitigate single-resolution bias and alleviate object fragmentation. Globally, it integrates open-vocabulary object detection (OVD) as localization priors within a unified framework. Extensive experiments across multiple MLLMs on HR image benchmarks demonstrate that MRD achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on both single-object and multi-object understanding tasks. Code will be available at: https://github.com/yf0412/MRD.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MRD：多分辨率检索-检测融合用于高分辨率图像理解
高分辨率（HR）图像的理解仍是多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的关键挑战。近期方法利用基于视觉的检索增强生成（RAG）从HR图像中检索与查询相关的局部区域，以提升MLLMs的理解能力。然而，该范式常导致物体碎片化，引发语义偏差和不完整检索，同时引入无关背景块的假阳性。为解决这些问题，我们提出多分辨率检索-检测（MRD），一种无需训练的框架，从局部和全局视角增强HR图像理解。局部上，MRD通过多分辨率语义融合强制跨尺度语义一致性，缓解单分辨率偏差并减轻物体碎片化；全局上，它将开放词汇目标检测（OVD）作为定位先验整合到统一框架中。在多个MLLMs上的HR图像基准测试中，MRD在单目标和多目标理解任务上均达到最先进（SOTA）性能。代码将在：https://github.com/yf0412/MRD 公开。

To improve multimodal large language models' understanding of high-resolution images, MRD addresses object fragmentation and semantic bias caused by single-resolution retrieval methods. It introduces a training-free framework that fuses multi-resolution semantic features to enforce cross-scale consistency locally and integrates open-vocabulary object detection as localization priors globally. Experiments across multiple MLLMs on HR benchmarks show MRD achieves state-of-the-art performance on both single-object and multi-object understanding tasks, outperforming prior retrieval-augmented approaches.
